            // UTF-16 null terminator
            stringsBuf.write(0)
            stringsBuf.write(0)
        }

        // Pad string data to 4-byte boundary
        while (stringsBuf.size() % 4 != 0) {
            stringsBuf.write(0)
        }

        val headerSize = 28
        val offsetsSize = strings.size * 4
        val stringsStart = headerSize + offsetsSize
        val chunkSize = stringsStart + stringsBuf.size()

        val buf = ByteBuffer.allocate(chunkSize).order(ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN)
        buf.putShort(RES_STRING_POOL_TYPE)
        buf.putShort(headerSize.toShort())
        buf.putInt(chunkSize)
        buf.putInt(strings.size) // stringCount
        buf.putInt(0) // styleCount
        buf.putInt(0) // flags: UTF-16
        buf.putInt(stringsStart)
        buf.putInt(0) // stylesStart
        offsets.forEach { buf.putInt(it) }
        buf.put(stringsBuf.toByteArray())
        return buf.array()
    }
}
package com.example.buildengine

import android.graphics.BitmapFactory
import com.example.core.ValidationUtils
import com.example.domain.model.BuildOutputFormat

/**
 * Immutable validated configuration derived from a BuildSpec before starting
 * on-device compilation and packaging.
 */
data class BuildConfiguration(
    val spec: BuildSpec,
    val wantApk: Boolean = spec.outputFormat == BuildOutputFormat.APK || spec.outputFormat == BuildOutputFormat.BOTH,
    val wantAab: Boolean = spec.outputFormat == BuildOutputFormat.AAB || spec.outputFormat == BuildOutputFormat.BOTH,
) {
    data class ValidationResult(
        val isValid: Boolean,
        val errors: List<String>,
    ) {
        val primaryError: String?
            get() = errors.firstOrNull()
    }

    companion object {
        fun validate(spec: BuildSpec): ValidationResult {
            val errors = mutableListOf<String>()

            if (spec.projectId <= 0L) {
                errors.add("شناسه پروژه نامعتبر است.")
            }
            val cleanAppName = spec.appName.trim()
            if (cleanAppName.isEmpty()) {
                errors.add("نام اپلیکیشن نمی‌تواند خالی باشد.")
            } else if (cleanAppName.length > 50) {
                errors.add("نام اپلیکیشن نباید بیش از ۵۰ کاراکتر باشد.")
            }

            val normalizedUrl = ValidationUtils.normalizeUrl(spec.websiteUrl)
            if (!ValidationUtils.isValidWebUrl(normalizedUrl)) {
                errors.add("آدرس وب‌سایت معتبر نیست (مثال: https://example.com).")
            } else if (spec.httpsOnly && normalizedUrl.startsWith("http://", ignoreCase = true)) {
                errors.add("نشانی با http:// تنظیم شده است در حالی که حالت HTTPS اجباری فعال است.")
            }

            if (!ValidationUtils.isValidPackageName(spec.packageName)) {
                errors.add("نام بسته (Package Name) نامعتبر است؛ از فرمت استاندارد مثل ir.webino.app استفاده کنید.")
            }

            if (spec.versionName.isBlank() || spec.versionName.contains('\n') || spec.versionName.contains('\r')) {
                errors.add("نام نسخه (Version Name) نامعتبر است.")
            }

            if (spec.versionCode < 1) {
                errors.add("کد نسخه (Version Code) باید حداقل ۱ باشد.")
            }

            if (spec.minSdk < 21 || spec.minSdk > 36) {
                errors.add("حداقل نسخه اندروید (minSdk) باید بین ۲۱ تا ۳۶ باشد.")
            }
            if (spec.targetSdk < spec.minSdk || spec.targetSdk > 36) {
                errors.add("نسخه هدف (targetSdk) باید بزرگتر یا مساوی minSdk و حداکثر ۳۶ باشد.")
            }

            if (!ValidationUtils.isValidHexColor(spec.primaryColorHex)) {
                errors.add("کد رنگ اصلی (${spec.primaryColorHex}) نامعتبر است.")
            }
            if (!ValidationUtils.isValidHexColor(spec.backgroundColorHex)) {
                errors.add("کد رنگ پس‌زمینه (${spec.backgroundColorHex}) نامعتبر است.")
            }

            if (spec.iconPngBytes != null) {
                val decoded = BitmapFactory.decodeByteArray(spec.iconPngBytes, 0, spec.iconPngBytes.size)
                if (decoded == null || decoded.width <= 0 || decoded.height <= 0) {
                    errors.add("فایل آیکون انتخاب‌شده قابل خواندن یا رمزگشایی نیست.")
                }
            }

            if (spec.splashPngBytes != null) {
                val decoded = BitmapFactory.decodeByteArray(spec.splashPngBytes, 0, spec.splashPngBytes.size)
                if (decoded == null || decoded.width <= 0 || decoded.height <= 0) {
                    errors.add("فایل تصویر Splash انتخاب‌شده نامعتبر است.")
                }
            }

            if (spec.keyAlias.isBlank()) {
                errors.add("نام مستعار کلید امضا (Key Alias) مشخص نشده است.")
            }
            if (spec.certificateChain.isEmpty()) {
                errors.add("گواهی دیجیتال X.509 برای امضای خروجی یافت نشد.")
            } else {
                val cert = spec.certificateChain.first()
                runCatching { cert.checkValidity() }.onFailure {
                    errors.add("گواهی دیجیتال امضا منقضی شده یا هنوز معتبر نیست.")
                }
            }

            return ValidationResult(isValid = errors.isEmpty(), errors = errors)
        }

        fun fromSpecOrThrow(spec: BuildSpec): BuildConfiguration {
            val result = validate(spec)
            require(result.isValid) { result.errors.joinToString("\n") }
            return BuildConfiguration(spec = spec)
        }
    }
}
package com.example.buildengine

import com.example.domain.model.EnvironmentCheckReport
import java.io.File

/**
 * Top-level contract for WEBINO's standalone On-Device Android Build Engine.
 */
interface BuildEngine {
    fun inspectEnvironment(): EnvironmentCheckReport
    fun validateConfiguration(spec: BuildSpec): BuildConfiguration.ValidationResult
    fun estimateStorage(spec: BuildSpec): BuildStorageManager.StorageEstimate
    suspend fun executeBuild(
        spec: BuildSpec,
        outputDir: File,
        isCancelled: () -> Boolean = { false },
        onProgress: suspend (BuildProgressUpdate) -> Unit,
    ): BuildExecutionResult
}
package com.example.buildengine

import android.content.Context
import com.example.domain.model.BuildOutputFormat
import com.example.domain.model.EnvironmentCheckReport
import java.io.File
import java.security.PrivateKey
import java.security.cert.X509Certificate

/**
 * Clean Architecture contracts for WEBINO's modular on-device build pipeline.
 * Each stage has a single clear responsibility and is backed by real local JVM tools.
 */
interface BuildEnvironmentChecker {
    fun inspect(context: Context): EnvironmentCheckReport
}

interface BuildProjectGenerator {
    fun generateSources(spec: BuildSpec, srcRootDir: File): List<File>
}

interface BuildResourceGenerator {
    fun generate(spec: BuildSpec): ResourceGenerator.GeneratedResources
}

interface BuildManifestGenerator {
    fun buildBinaryManifest(spec: BuildSpec): ByteArray
}

interface BuildCompiler {
    fun compileJavaToDex(
        context: Context,
        sourceFiles: List<File>,
        workDir: File,
        minSdk: Int,
        isCancelled: () -> Boolean,
    ): File
}

interface BuildPackager {
    fun packageUnsignedApk(entries: List<ApkPackager.ApkEntry>, outputApk: File)
    fun packageSignedAab(
        spec: BuildSpec,
        manifestNode: AxmlCompiler.XmlNode,
        generatedRes: ResourceGenerator.GeneratedResources,
        dexBytes: ByteArray,
        outputAab: File,
    )
}

interface BuildSigner {
    fun signApk(
        unsignedApk: File,
        signedApk: File,
        signerName: String,
        privateKey: PrivateKey,
        certificates: List<X509Certificate>,
        minSdkVersion: Int,
    )
}

interface BuildValidator {
    fun validate(artifactFile: File, format: BuildOutputFormat, expectedSpec: BuildSpec? = null): OutputValidator.ValidationReport
}

/**
 * Default local JVM implementations wired directly to WEBINO's real on-device tools.
 */
object LocalBuildPipelineAdapters :
    BuildEnvironmentChecker,
    BuildProjectGenerator,
    BuildResourceGenerator,
    BuildManifestGenerator,
    BuildCompiler,
    BuildPackager,
    BuildSigner,
    BuildValidator {

    override fun inspect(context: Context): EnvironmentCheckReport = EnvironmentChecker.inspect(context)

    override fun generateSources(spec: BuildSpec, srcRootDir: File): List<File> =
        ProjectGenerator.generateSources(spec, srcRootDir)

    override fun generate(spec: BuildSpec): ResourceGenerator.GeneratedResources =
        ResourceGenerator.generate(spec)

    override fun buildBinaryManifest(spec: BuildSpec): ByteArray =
        AxmlCompiler.compile(AxmlCompiler.buildManifestNode(spec))

    override fun compileJavaToDex(
        context: Context,
        sourceFiles: List<File>,
        workDir: File,
        minSdk: Int,
        isCancelled: () -> Boolean,
    ): File {
        val stubsJar = JavaCompiler.extractStubsJar(context, workDir)
        val classesDir = File(workDir, "classes")
        val compileRes = JavaCompiler.compile(sourceFiles, stubsJar, classesDir, isCancelled)
        if (isCancelled()) throw BuildCancelledException()
        check(compileRes.success) { compileRes.diagnostics }
        val dexOut = File(workDir, "classes.dex")
        val dexRes = DexGenerator.compileToDex(classesDir, dexOut, minSdk)
        check(dexRes.success) { dexRes.errorMessage ?: "DEX compilation failed" }
        return dexOut
    }

    override fun packageUnsignedApk(entries: List<ApkPackager.ApkEntry>, outputApk: File) {
        ApkPackager.packageUnsignedApk(entries, outputApk)
    }

    override fun packageSignedAab(
        spec: BuildSpec,
        manifestNode: AxmlCompiler.XmlNode,
        generatedRes: ResourceGenerator.GeneratedResources,
        dexBytes: ByteArray,
        outputAab: File,
    ) {
        AabPackager.packageAndSignAab(
            bundleConfigPb = ProtoAabEncoder.encodeBundleConfig(),
            protoManifestPb = ProtoAabEncoder.encodeProtoXmlNode(manifestNode),
            protoResourcesPb = ProtoAabEncoder.encodeProtoResourceTable(
                packageName = spec.packageName,
                appName = spec.appName,
                primaryColorArgb = generatedRes.primaryColorArgb,
                backgroundColorArgb = generatedRes.backgroundColorArgb,
            ),
            dexBytes = dexBytes,
            iconPng = generatedRes.iconPng,
            roundIconPng = generatedRes.roundIconPng,
            splashPng = generatedRes.splashPng,
            configJsonBytes = generatedRes.configJsonBytes,
            outputAab = outputAab,
            signerAlias = spec.keyAlias,
            privateKey = spec.privateKey,
            certificates = spec.certificateChain,
        )
    }

    override fun signApk(
        unsignedApk: File,
        signedApk: File,
        signerName: String,
        privateKey: PrivateKey,
        certificates: List<X509Certificate>,
        minSdkVersion: Int,
    ) {
        ApkSigner.sign(unsignedApk, signedApk, signerName, privateKey, certificates, minSdkVersion)
    }

    override fun validate(
        artifactFile: File,
        format: BuildOutputFormat,
        expectedSpec: BuildSpec?,
    ): OutputValidator.ValidationReport =
        if (format == BuildOutputFormat.AAB) {
            OutputValidator.validateAab(artifactFile, expectedSpec)
        } else {
            OutputValidator.validateApk(artifactFile, expectedSpec)
        }
}
package com.example.buildengine

import com.example.domain.model.BackNavigationBehavior
import com.example.domain.model.BuildOutputFormat
import com.example.domain.model.BuildStep
import com.example.domain.model.CacheModeOption
import com.example.domain.model.ExternalLinkMode
import com.example.domain.model.LoadingAnimationType
import com.example.domain.model.MixedContentOption
import com.example.domain.model.OrientationMode
import com.example.domain.model.PopupBehaviorMode
import com.example.domain.model.SslPolicy
import com.example.domain.model.ThemePreset
import com.example.domain.model.ToolbarStyle
import java.security.PrivateKey
import java.security.cert.X509Certificate

data class BuildSpec(
    val projectId: Long,
    val appName: String,
    val packageName: String,
    val versionName: String,
    val versionCode: Int,
    val minSdk: Int = 24,
    val targetSdk: Int = 36,
    val websiteUrl: String,
    val startPagePath: String = "",
    val developerName: String = "",
    val primaryColorHex: String = "#7C3AED",
    val backgroundColorHex: String = "#0B1026",
    val themePreset: ThemePreset = ThemePreset.DARK,
    val toolbarStyle: ToolbarStyle = ToolbarStyle.TOP_BAR,
    val loadingAnimation: LoadingAnimationType = LoadingAnimationType.PROGRESS_BAR,
    val splashEnabled: Boolean = true,
    val splashDurationMs: Int = 1400,
    val orientationMode: OrientationMode = OrientationMode.AUTO,
    val fullscreenMode: Boolean = false,
    val showStatusBar: Boolean = true,
    val showNavigationBar: Boolean = true,
    val backBehavior: BackNavigationBehavior = BackNavigationBehavior.HISTORY_THEN_EXIT,
    val supportRtl: Boolean = true,
    // WebView settings
    val javaScriptEnabled: Boolean = true,
    val domStorageEnabled: Boolean = true,
    val webSqlEnabled: Boolean = false,
    val clipboardAccessEnabled: Boolean = true,
    val cookiesEnabled: Boolean = true,
    val thirdPartyCookiesEnabled: Boolean = false,
    val fileUploadEnabled: Boolean = true,
    val downloadManagerEnabled: Boolean = true,
    val cameraPermission: Boolean = false,
    val microphonePermission: Boolean = false,
    val geolocationEnabled: Boolean = false,
    val webRtcEnabled: Boolean = false,
    val fullscreenVideoEnabled: Boolean = true,
    val multipleWindowsEnabled: Boolean = false,
    val popupsEnabled: Boolean = false,
    val popupBehavior: PopupBehaviorMode = PopupBehaviorMode.SAME_PAGE,
    val customUserAgent: String = "",
    val cacheMode: CacheModeOption = CacheModeOption.DEFAULT,
    val zoomEnabled: Boolean = false,
    val mixedContentMode: MixedContentOption = MixedContentOption.NEVER_ALLOW,
    val sslPolicy: SslPolicy = SslPolicy.STRICT_BLOCK,
    val httpsOnly: Boolean = true,
    val navigationRulesRaw: String = "",
    // Advanced features
    val enableDeepLinks: Boolean = false,
    val deepLinkScheme: String = "webino",
    val enableAppLinks: Boolean = false,
    val appLinkHost: String = "",
    val enableFileSharing: Boolean = true,
    val enablePhoneDialer: Boolean = true,
    val enableEmailIntent: Boolean = true,
    val enableMapsIntent: Boolean = true,
    val enablePaymentGatewayBridge: Boolean = true,
    val externalLinkMode: ExternalLinkMode = ExternalLinkMode.ASK_OR_ALLOWLIST,
    // Media & Output
    val iconPngBytes: ByteArray? = null,
    val splashPngBytes: ByteArray? = null,
    val outputFormat: BuildOutputFormat = BuildOutputFormat.APK,
    val keyAlias: String = "webino_key",
    val privateKey: PrivateKey,
    val certificateChain: List<X509Certificate>,
)

data class BuildLogEntry(
    val timestamp: Long = System.currentTimeMillis(),
    val level: String = "INFO",
    val step: BuildStep,
    val message: String,
)

data class BuildProgressUpdate(
    val step: BuildStep,
    val progressPercent: Int,
    val statusMessage: String,
    val logEntry: BuildLogEntry? = null,
)

data class GeneratedArtifactInfo(
    val format: BuildOutputFormat,
    val filePath: String,
    val fileSizeBytes: Long,
    val isSigned: Boolean,
    val isVerified: Boolean,
    val sha256CertFingerprint: String,
    val validationSummary: String,
)

data class BuildExecutionResult(
    val success: Boolean,
    val artifacts: List<GeneratedArtifactInfo>,
    val logs: List<BuildLogEntry>,
    val durationMs: Long,
    val errorMessage: String? = null,
    val cancelled: Boolean = false,
)

class BuildCancelledException(message: String = "عملیات ساخت توسط کاربر لغو شد.") : Exception(message)
package com.example.buildengine

import android.content.Context
import kotlinx.serialization.Serializable
import kotlinx.serialization.encodeToString
import kotlinx.serialization.json.Json
import java.io.File

@Serializable
data class BuildReportArtifactItem(
    val format: String,
    val filePath: String,
    val fileSizeBytes: Long,
    val isSigned: Boolean,
    val isVerified: Boolean,
    val certSha256: String,
    val validationSummary: String,
)

@Serializable
data class BuildReportLogItem(
    val timestamp: Long,
    val level: String,
    val step: String,
    val message: String,
)

@Serializable
data class BuildReport(
    val taskId: Long,
    val projectId: Long,
    val appName: String,
    val packageName: String,
    val versionName: String,
    val versionCode: Int,
    val outputFormat: String,
    val keyAlias: String,
    val success: Boolean,
    val durationMs: Long,
    val generatedAtMs: Long,
    val estimatedTempBytes: Long,
    val estimatedOutputBytes: Long,
    val errorMessage: String? = null,
    val artifacts: List<BuildReportArtifactItem> = emptyList(),
    val logs: List<BuildReportLogItem> = emptyList(),
)

/**
 * Generates structured persistent JSON build reports and enforces sensitive-data redaction
 * so keystore passwords, vault tokens, or private keys never leak into saved logs.
 */
object BuildReportGenerator {

    private val json = Json {
        prettyPrint = true
        ignoreUnknownKeys = true
    }

    private val sensitivePatterns = listOf(
        Regex("""(?i)(password|passwd|pwd|storePassword|keyPassword|token|secret)\s*[:=]\s*[^\s,;]+"""),
        Regex("""(AKS|LOC):[A-Za-z0-9+/=]{12,}"""),
        Regex("""-----BEGIN [A-Z ]*PRIVATE KEY-----[\s\S]*?-----END [A-Z ]*PRIVATE KEY-----"""),
    )

    fun redactSensitive(
        message: String,
        enabled: Boolean = true,
        explicitSecrets: List<String> = emptyList(),
    ): String {
        if (!enabled) return message
        var out = message
        explicitSecrets.filter { it.length >= 4 }.forEach { secret ->
            out = out.replace(secret, "***REDACTED***")
        }
        sensitivePatterns.forEach { regex ->
            out = regex.replace(out) { match ->
                val raw = match.value
                val sepIdx = raw.indexOfAny(charArrayOf(':', '='))
                if (sepIdx > 0 && !raw.startsWith("AKS:") && !raw.startsWith("LOC:")) {
                    "${raw.substring(0, sepIdx + 1)} ***REDACTED***"
                } else {
                    "***REDACTED***"
                }
            }
        }
        return out
    }

    fun generateReport(
        taskId: Long,
        spec: BuildSpec,
        result: BuildExecutionResult,
        storageEstimate: BuildStorageManager.StorageEstimate?,
        redactSecrets: Boolean = true,
    ): BuildReport {
        val redactedLogs = result.logs.map { entry ->
            BuildReportLogItem(
                timestamp = entry.timestamp,
                level = entry.level,
                step = entry.step.name,
                message = redactSensitive(entry.message, enabled = redactSecrets),
            )
        }
        val redactedError = result.errorMessage?.let { redactSensitive(it, enabled = redactSecrets) }
        return BuildReport(
            taskId = taskId,
            projectId = spec.projectId,
            appName = spec.appName,
            packageName = spec.packageName,
            versionName = spec.versionName,
            versionCode = spec.versionCode,
            outputFormat = spec.outputFormat.name,
            keyAlias = spec.keyAlias,
            success = result.success,
            durationMs = result.durationMs,
            generatedAtMs = System.currentTimeMillis(),
            estimatedTempBytes = storageEstimate?.estimatedTempBytes ?: 0L,
            estimatedOutputBytes = storageEstimate?.estimatedOutputBytes ?: 0L,
            errorMessage = redactedError,
            artifacts = result.artifacts.map { art ->
                BuildReportArtifactItem(
                    format = art.format.name,
                    filePath = art.filePath,
                    fileSizeBytes = art.fileSizeBytes,
                    isSigned = art.isSigned,
                    isVerified = art.isVerified,
                    certSha256 = art.sha256CertFingerprint,
                    validationSummary = art.validationSummary,
                )
            },
            logs = redactedLogs,
        )
    }

    fun saveReport(
        context: Context,
        report: BuildReport,
    ): File {
        val dir = BuildStorageManager.getReportsDir(context)
        val outFile = File(dir, "build_report_${report.taskId}.json")
        outFile.writeText(json.encodeToString(report), Charsets.UTF_8)
        return outFile
    }
}
package com.example.buildengine

import android.content.Context
import android.os.StatFs
import java.io.File

/**
 * Manages isolated temporary build workspaces, pre-build disk headroom estimation,
 * post-build / post-cancel cleanup, and output/report storage accounting.
 */
object BuildStorageManager {

    private const val BASE_TEMP_OVERHEAD_BYTES = 14L * 1024L * 1024L // android-stubs + ECJ classes + dx temp
    private const val BASE_ARTIFACT_BYTES = 2L * 1024L * 1024L
    private const val SAFETY_HEADROOM_BYTES = 8L * 1024L * 1024L

    data class StorageEstimate(
        val estimatedTempBytes: Long,
        val estimatedOutputBytes: Long,
        val requiredTotalBytes: Long,
        val availableBytes: Long,
        val hasEnoughSpace: Boolean,
        val summaryFa: String,
    )

    data class StorageBreakdown(
        val outputsBytes: Long,
        val reportsBytes: Long,
        val tempBuildBytes: Long,
        val cacheBytes: Long,
        val mediaBytes: Long,
        val freeInternalBytes: Long,
    )

    fun estimateBuildStorage(context: Context, spec: BuildSpec): StorageEstimate {
        val mediaBytes = (spec.iconPngBytes?.size?.toLong() ?: 0L) +
            (spec.splashPngBytes?.size?.toLong() ?: 0L)
        val formatMultiplier = when (spec.outputFormat) {
            com.example.domain.model.BuildOutputFormat.BOTH -> 2L
            else -> 1L
        }
        val estimatedTemp = BASE_TEMP_OVERHEAD_BYTES + mediaBytes * 2L
        val estimatedOutput = (BASE_ARTIFACT_BYTES + mediaBytes) * formatMultiplier
        val requiredTotal = estimatedTemp + estimatedOutput + SAFETY_HEADROOM_BYTES
        val available = queryAvailableBytes(context.filesDir)
        val enough = available >= requiredTotal

        val reqMb = (requiredTotal / (1024.0 * 1024.0))
        val availMb = (available / (1024.0 * 1024.0))
        val summaryFa = if (enough) {
            String.format(
                java.util.Locale.US,
                "فضای موردنیاز برآوردی: %.1f مگابایت • فضای آزاد دستگاه: %.1f مگابایت",
                reqMb,
                availMb,
            )
        } else {
            String.format(
                java.util.Locale.US,
                "فضای خالی دستگاه کافی نیست (نیاز: %.1f مگابایت، موجود: %.1f مگابایت).",
                reqMb,
                availMb,
            )
        }

        return StorageEstimate(
            estimatedTempBytes = estimatedTemp,
            estimatedOutputBytes = estimatedOutput,
            requiredTotalBytes = requiredTotal,
            availableBytes = available,
            hasEnoughSpace = enough,
            summaryFa = summaryFa,
        )
    }

    fun queryAvailableBytes(path: File): Long {
        val target = if (path.exists()) path else (path.parentFile ?: path)
        return runCatching {
            val stat = StatFs(target.absolutePath)
            val statAvail = stat.availableBlocksLong * stat.blockSizeLong
            if (statAvail > 0L) statAvail else target.usableSpace
        }.getOrElse { target.usableSpace }
    }

    fun getBuildsCacheRoot(context: Context): File =
        File(context.cacheDir, "webino_builds").apply { mkdirs() }

    fun createWorkDir(context: Context, projectId: Long, buildId: Long): File =
        File(getBuildsCacheRoot(context), "build_${projectId}_$buildId").apply { mkdirs() }

    fun getOutputsDir(context: Context): File =
        File(context.filesDir, "outputs").apply { mkdirs() }

    fun getReportsDir(context: Context): File =
        File(context.filesDir, "build_reports").apply { mkdirs() }

    fun cleanupWorkDir(workDir: File?) {
        if (workDir != null && workDir.exists()) {
            runCatching { workDir.deleteRecursively() }
        }
    }

    fun cleanupOrphanedWorkDirs(context: Context, maxAgeMs: Long = 3_600_000L): Int {
        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
        var deletedCount = 0
        getBuildsCacheRoot(context).listFiles()?.forEach { dir ->
            if (now - dir.lastModified() >= maxAgeMs) {
                if (dir.deleteRecursively()) deletedCount++
            }
        }
        // Also sweep legacy webino_build_* cache folders
        context.cacheDir.listFiles()?.forEach { dir ->
            if (dir.isDirectory && dir.name.startsWith("webino_build_") && now - dir.lastModified() >= maxAgeMs) {
                if (dir.deleteRecursively()) deletedCount++
            }
        }
        return deletedCount
    }

    fun calculateBreakdown(context: Context): StorageBreakdown {
        val outputs = dirSizeBytes(getOutputsDir(context))
        val reports = dirSizeBytes(getReportsDir(context))
        val tempBuilds = dirSizeBytes(getBuildsCacheRoot(context))
        val cache = dirSizeBytes(context.cacheDir)
        val media = dirSizeBytes(File(context.filesDir, "project_media"))
        val free = queryAvailableBytes(context.filesDir)
        return StorageBreakdown(
            outputsBytes = outputs,
            reportsBytes = reports,
            tempBuildBytes = tempBuilds,
            cacheBytes = cache,
            mediaBytes = media,
            freeInternalBytes = free,
        )
    }

    fun clearTempBuildCache(context: Context): Long {
        val freed = dirSizeBytes(context.cacheDir)
        context.cacheDir.listFiles()?.forEach { it.deleteRecursively() }
        return freed
    }

    private fun dirSizeBytes(dir: File): Long {
        if (!dir.exists()) return 0L
        return dir.walkTopDown().filter { it.isFile }.sumOf { it.length() }
    }
}
package com.example.buildengine

import com.android.dx.command.dexer.Main
import java.io.File

/**
 * In-process Dalvik/ART DEX bytecode compiler powered by dalvik-dx.
 * Converts ECJ-compiled .class files into classes.dex without spawning child processes.
 */
object DexGenerator {

    data class DexResult(
        val success: Boolean,
        val dexFile: File,
        val errorMessage: String? = null,
    )

    fun compileToDex(
        classesRootDir: File,
        dexOutputFile: File,
        minSdk: Int = 24,
    ): DexResult {
        dexOutputFile.parentFile?.mkdirs()
        return try {
            val arguments = Main.Arguments().apply {
                outName = dexOutputFile.absolutePath
                fileNames = arrayOf(classesRootDir.absolutePath)
                minSdkVersion = minSdk
                strictNameCheck = false
                emptyOk = false
                verbose = false
            }
            val exitCode = Main.run(arguments)
            val valid = exitCode == 0 && dexOutputFile.exists() && dexOutputFile.length() > 112L
            DexResult(
                success = valid,
                dexFile = dexOutputFile,
                errorMessage = if (valid) null else "DX exited with code $exitCode",
            )
        } catch (t: Throwable) {
            DexResult(
                success = false,
                dexFile = dexOutputFile,
                errorMessage = t.message ?: t.javaClass.simpleName,
            )
        }
    }
}
package com.example.buildengine

import android.app.ActivityManager
import android.content.Context
import android.os.Build
import android.os.StatFs
import com.example.domain.model.EnvironmentCheckReport

/**
 * Checks real device resources (RAM, internal storage) and verifies all embedded
 * on-device compilation engines are present and loadable.
 */
object EnvironmentChecker {

    // حداقل فضای لازم به 100 مگابایت افزایش یافت تا فرآیند کامپایل و استخراج فایل‌ها با خطا مواجه نشود
    private const val MIN_REQUIRED_STORAGE_MB = 100L
    private const val MIN_REQUIRED_RAM_MB = 32L

    fun inspect(context: Context): EnvironmentCheckReport {
        val issues = mutableListOf<String>()

        val statFs = StatFs(context.filesDir.absolutePath)
        val statBytes = statFs.availableBlocksLong * statFs.blockSizeLong
        val freeStorageBytes = if (statBytes > 0L) statBytes else context.filesDir.usableSpace
        val freeStorageMb = freeStorageBytes / (1024L * 1024L)
        if (freeStorageMb < MIN_REQUIRED_STORAGE_MB) {
            issues.add("فضای خالی حافظه داخلی کمتر از $MIN_REQUIRED_STORAGE_MB مگابایت است.")
        }

        val runtime = Runtime.getRuntime()
        val maxHeapMb = runtime.maxMemory() / (1024L * 1024L)
        val usedHeapMb = (runtime.totalMemory() - runtime.freeMemory()) / (1024L * 1024L)
        val freeHeapMb = (maxHeapMb - usedHeapMb).coerceAtLeast(0L)

        val am = context.getSystemService(Context.ACTIVITY_SERVICE) as? ActivityManager
        val memInfo = ActivityManager.MemoryInfo()
        am?.getMemoryInfo(memInfo)
        val totalDeviceRamMb = if (memInfo.totalMem > 0L) memInfo.totalMem / (1024L * 1024L) else maxHeapMb

        if (freeHeapMb < MIN_REQUIRED_RAM_MB) {
            issues.add("حافظه رم آزاد برای کامپایل کافی نیست.")
        }

        val hasStubs = runCatching {
            context.assets.open("compiler/android-stubs.jar").use { it.read() != -1 }
        }.getOrDefault(false)
        if (!hasStubs) issues.add("فایل کتابخانه پایه android-stubs.jar در بسته برنامه یافت نشد.")

        val hasJavaCompiler = runCatching {
            Class.forName("org.eclipse.jdt.internal.compiler.batch.Main")
            true
        }.getOrDefault(false)
        if (!hasJavaCompiler) issues.add("موتور کامپایلر جاوا (ECJ) در دسترس نیست.")

        val hasDexCompiler = runCatching {
            Class.forName("com.android.dx.command.dexer.Main")
            true
        }.getOrDefault(false)
        if (!hasDexCompiler) issues.add("موتور تولید DEX در دسترس نیست.")

        val hasApkSigner = runCatching {
            Class.forName("com.android.apksig.ApkSigner")
            true
        }.getOrDefault(false)
        if (!hasApkSigner) issues.add("موتور امضای دیجیتال APK در دسترس نیست.")

        val hasCrypto = runCatching {
            Class.forName("org.bouncycastle.jce.provider.BouncyCastleProvider")
            true
        }.getOrDefault(false)
        if (!hasCrypto) issues.add("ماژول رمزنگاری گواهی امضا در دسترس نیست.")

        val engineStatus = when {
            issues.isEmpty() -> com.example.domain.model.BuildEngineStatus.READY
            !hasStubs || !hasJavaCompiler || !hasDexCompiler || !hasApkSigner || !hasCrypto ->
                com.example.domain.model.BuildEngineStatus.UNSUPPORTED
            else -> com.example.domain.model.BuildEngineStatus.ERROR
        }

        return EnvironmentCheckReport(
            isReady = issues.isEmpty(),
            status = engineStatus,
            freeStorageMb = freeStorageMb,
            freeRamMb = freeHeapMb,
            totalRamMb = totalDeviceRamMb,
            hasEmbeddedStubs = hasStubs,
            hasJavaCompiler = hasJavaCompiler,
            hasDexCompiler = hasDexCompiler,
            hasApkSigner = hasApkSigner,
            hasKeystoreCrypto = hasCrypto,
            androidSdkInt = Build.VERSION.SDK_INT,
            issues = issues,
        )
    }
}package com.example.buildengine

import android.content.Context
import org.eclipse.jdt.core.compiler.CompilationProgress
import org.eclipse.jdt.internal.compiler.batch.Main
import java.io.File
import java.io.PrintWriter
import java.io.StringWriter

/**
 * In-process Java 8 compiler backed by Eclipse JDT Core (ECJ) and our embedded
 * compact android-stubs.jar so Java sources compile directly inside Android's sandbox.
 */
object JavaCompiler {

    data class CompileResult(
        val success: Boolean,
        val classFiles: List<File>,
        val diagnostics: String,
    )

    fun extractStubsJar(context: Context, workDir: File): File {
        val stubsFile = File(workDir, "android-stubs.jar")
        if (!stubsFile.exists() || stubsFile.length() < 1024) {
            context.assets.open("compiler/android-stubs.jar").use { input ->
                stubsFile.outputStream().use { output ->
                    input.copyTo(output)
                }
            }
        }
        return stubsFile
    }

    fun compile(
        sourceFiles: List<File>,
        stubsJar: File,
        classesOutDir: File,
        isCancelled: () -> Boolean = { false },
    ): CompileResult {
        classesOutDir.mkdirs()
        val outWriter = StringWriter()
        val errWriter = StringWriter()

        // فقط از فایل Jar داخل assets استفاده می‌کنیم تا مشکل دسترسی اندروید 10+ پیش نیاید
        val classpathString = stubsJar.absolutePath

        val args = mutableListOf(
            "-1.8",
            "-nowarn",
            "-proc:none",
            "-encoding", "UTF-8",
            "-bootclasspath", classpathString,
            "-classpath", classpathString,
            "-d", classesOutDir.absolutePath,
        )
        sourceFiles.forEach { args.add(it.absolutePath) }

        val progress = object : CompilationProgress() {
            override fun begin(remainingWork: Int) {}
            override fun done() {}
            override fun isCanceled(): Boolean = isCancelled()
            override fun setTaskName(name: String?) {}
            override fun worked(workIncrement: Int, remainingWork: Int) {}
        }

        return try {
            val ok = Main.compile(
                args.toTypedArray(),
                PrintWriter(outWriter),
                PrintWriter(errWriter),
                progress,
            )

            val classFiles = classesOutDir.walkTopDown().filter { it.isFile && it.extension == "class" }.toList()
            val diag = buildString {
                val o = outWriter.toString().trim()
                val e = errWriter.toString().trim()
                if (o.isNotEmpty()) appendLine(o)
                if (e.isNotEmpty()) appendLine(e)
            }.trim()

            CompileResult(
                success = ok && classFiles.isNotEmpty(),
                classFiles = classFiles,
                diagnostics = diag,
            )
        } catch (t: Throwable) {
            val sw = StringWriter()
            t.printStackTrace(PrintWriter(sw))
            CompileResult(
                success = false,
                classFiles = emptyList(),
                diagnostics = "خطای داخلی کامپایلر ECJ:\n${t.message}\n\n${sw.toString()}"
            )
        }
    }
}package com.example.buildengine

import android.content.Context
import com.example.domain.model.BuildOutputFormat
import com.example.domain.model.BuildStep
import com.example.domain.model.EnvironmentCheckReport
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.ensureActive
import kotlinx.coroutines.isActive
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import java.io.File
import kotlin.coroutines.coroutineContext

/**
 * Standalone On-Device Build Engine Orchestrator implementing [BuildEngine].
 * Executes all real build stages:
 * 1. Checking Environment & Pre-Build Validation -> 2. Preparing Resources ->
 * 3. Generating Project -> 4. Compiling Java & DEX -> 5. Packaging APK/AAB ->
 * 6. Signing -> 7. Validating -> 8. Completed.
 */
class OnDeviceBuildOrchestrator(private val context: Context) : BuildEngine {

    override fun inspectEnvironment(): EnvironmentCheckReport = EnvironmentChecker.inspect(context)

    override fun validateConfiguration(spec: BuildSpec): BuildConfiguration.ValidationResult =
        BuildConfiguration.validate(spec)

    override fun estimateStorage(spec: BuildSpec): BuildStorageManager.StorageEstimate =
        BuildStorageManager.estimateBuildStorage(context, spec)

    override suspend fun executeBuild(
        spec: BuildSpec,
        outputDir: File,
        isCancelled: () -> Boolean,
        onProgress: suspend (BuildProgressUpdate) -> Unit,
    ): BuildExecutionResult = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val startTime = System.currentTimeMillis()
        var currentStep = BuildStep.CHECKING_ENV
        val logs = mutableListOf<BuildLogEntry>()
        val artifacts = mutableListOf<GeneratedArtifactInfo>()
        val safePkgSlug = spec.packageName.replace('.', '_')
        val workDir = BuildStorageManager.createWorkDir(context, spec.projectId, startTime)
        outputDir.mkdirs()

        fun checkCancelled() {
            if (!coroutineContext.isActive || isCancelled()) {
                throw BuildCancelledException()
            }
        }

        suspend fun logAndEmit(step: BuildStep, percent: Int, msg: String, level: String = "INFO") {
            coroutineContext.ensureActive()
            checkCancelled()
            currentStep = step
            val entry = BuildLogEntry(timestamp = System.currentTimeMillis(), level = level, step = step, message = msg)
            logs.add(entry)
            onProgress(
                BuildProgressUpdate(
                    step = step,
                    progressPercent = percent,
                    statusMessage = msg,
                    logEntry = entry,
                )
            )
        }

        try {
            // Stage 1: Input Validation, Storage Estimation & Environment Check
            logAndEmit(BuildStep.CHECKING_ENV, 3, "اعتبارسنجی مشخصات پروژه و برآورد فضای ذخیره‌سازی...")
            val configResult = validateConfiguration(spec)
            if (!configResult.isValid) {
                throw IllegalArgumentException(configResult.errors.joinToString("\n"))
            }
            val config = BuildConfiguration(spec)

            val storageEst = estimateStorage(spec)
            if (!storageEst.hasEnoughSpace) {
                throw IllegalStateException(storageEst.summaryFa)
            }
            logAndEmit(BuildStep.CHECKING_ENV, 6, storageEst.summaryFa)

            val env = inspectEnvironment()
            if (!env.isReady) {
                throw IllegalStateException(env.issues.joinToString(" | "))
            }
            logAndEmit(
                BuildStep.CHECKING_ENV,
                10,
                "محیط آماده است • حافظه آزاد: ${env.freeStorageMb}MB • رم آزاد: ${env.freeRamMb}MB"
            )

            // Stage 2: Prepare Resources
            logAndEmit(BuildStep.PREPARING_RESOURCES, 15, "در حال تولید آیکونهای تطبیقی و پیکربندی منابع برنامه...")
            val generatedRes = ResourceGenerator.generate(spec)
            logAndEmit(
                BuildStep.PREPARING_RESOURCES,
                17,
                "آیکون: " + (spec.iconPngBytes?.let { "تصویر سفارشی (${it.size} بایت)" } ?: "پیش‌فرض (تصویری انتخاب نشده یا فایل یافت نشد)") +
                    " • Splash: " + (spec.splashPngBytes?.let { "تصویر سفارشی (${it.size} بایت)" } ?: "پیش‌فرض"),
            )
            val arscBytes = ArscCompiler.compile(
                packageName = spec.packageName,
                appName = spec.appName,
                primaryColorArgb = generatedRes.primaryColorArgb,
                backgroundColorArgb = generatedRes.backgroundColorArgb,
            )
            val manifestNode = AxmlCompiler.buildManifestNode(spec)
            val binaryManifestBytes = AxmlCompiler.compile(manifestNode)
            logAndEmit(
                BuildStep.PREPARING_RESOURCES,
                22,
                "جدول منابع resources.arsc (${arscBytes.size} بایت) و مانیفست باینری (${binaryManifestBytes.size} بایت) آماده شد."
            )

            // Stage 3: Generate Project Sources
            logAndEmit(BuildStep.GENERATING_PROJECT, 28, "تولید سورسکد جاوا برای بسته ${spec.packageName}...")
            val srcDir = File(workDir, "src")
            val sourceFiles = ProjectGenerator.generateSources(spec, srcDir)
            val stubsJar = JavaCompiler.extractStubsJar(context, workDir)
            logAndEmit(
                BuildStep.GENERATING_PROJECT,
                36,
                "تولید ${sourceFiles.size} فایل سورس جاوا و بارگذاری کتابخانه پایه اندروید انجام شد."
            )

            // Stage 4: Compile Java -> .class -> classes.dex
            logAndEmit(BuildStep.COMPILING, 44, "کامپایل سورسکد جاوا با موتور ECJ روی دستگاه...")
            val classesDir = File(workDir, "classes")
            val compileResult = JavaCompiler.compile(
                sourceFiles = sourceFiles,
                stubsJar = stubsJar,
                classesOutDir = classesDir,
                isCancelled = { !coroutineContext.isActive || isCancelled() },
            )
            checkCancelled()
            if (!compileResult.success) {
                throw IllegalStateException("خطا در کامپایل جاوا:\n${compileResult.diagnostics}")
            }
            logAndEmit(
                BuildStep.COMPILING,
                50,
                "کامپایل جاوا موفق بود (${compileResult.classFiles.size} فایل بایتکد .class). در حال تبدیل به DEX..."
            )

            val dexFile = File(workDir, "classes.dex")
            val dexResult = DexGenerator.compileToDex(
                classesRootDir = classesDir,
                dexOutputFile = dexFile,
                minSdk = spec.minSdk,
            )
            checkCancelled()
            if (!dexResult.success) {
                throw IllegalStateException("خطا در تولید DEX: ${dexResult.errorMessage}")
            }
            val dexBytes = dexFile.readBytes()
            logAndEmit(
                BuildStep.COMPILING,
                58,
                "بایتکد Dalvik/ART (classes.dex) به حجم ${dexBytes.size} بایت ساخته شد."
            )

            val timestampTag = startTime
            val certSha256 = spec.certificateChain.firstOrNull()?.let { OutputValidator.sha256Fingerprint(it) }.orEmpty()

            var unsignedApk: File? = null
            var signedApk: File? = null
            var bundleConfigPb: ByteArray? = null
            var protoManifestPb: ByteArray? = null
            var protoResourcesPb: ByteArray? = null
            var signedAab: File? = null

            // Stage 5: Packaging
            if (config.wantApk) {
                logAndEmit(BuildStep.PACKAGING, 64, "بستهبندی و همترازی ۴بایتی (ZipAlign) فایل APK...")
                val apkRaw = File(workDir, "unsigned.apk")
                val entries = listOf(
                    ApkPackager.ApkEntry("AndroidManifest.xml", binaryManifestBytes, storedAligned = false),
                    ApkPackager.ApkEntry("resources.arsc", arscBytes, storedAligned = true),
                    ApkPackager.ApkEntry("classes.dex", dexBytes, storedAligned = false),
                    ApkPackager.ApkEntry(ArscCompiler.ICON_APK_PATH, generatedRes.iconPng, storedAligned = true),
                    ApkPackager.ApkEntry(ArscCompiler.ICON_ROUND_APK_PATH, generatedRes.roundIconPng, storedAligned = true),
                    ApkPackager.ApkEntry("assets/splash.png", generatedRes.splashPng, storedAligned = true),
                    ApkPackager.ApkEntry("assets/webino_config.json", generatedRes.configJsonBytes, storedAligned = false),
                )
                ApkPackager.packageUnsignedApk(entries, apkRaw)
                unsignedApk = apkRaw
                logAndEmit(BuildStep.PACKAGING, 70, "فایل APK خام ساخته شد (${apkRaw.length()} بایت).")
            }
            if (config.wantAab) {
                logAndEmit(BuildStep.PACKAGING, 74, "ساخت ساختار Protobuf و بستهبندی Android App Bundle (.aab)...")
                bundleConfigPb = ProtoAabEncoder.encodeBundleConfig()
                protoManifestPb = ProtoAabEncoder.encodeProtoXmlNode(manifestNode)
                protoResourcesPb = ProtoAabEncoder.encodeProtoResourceTable(
                    packageName = spec.packageName,
                    appName = spec.appName,
                    primaryColorArgb = generatedRes.primaryColorArgb,
                    backgroundColorArgb = generatedRes.backgroundColorArgb,
                )
            }

            // Stage 6: Signing
            if (config.wantApk && unsignedApk != null) {
                logAndEmit(BuildStep.SIGNING, 80, "اعمال امضای دیجیتال v1 + v2 + v3 با گواهی ${spec.keyAlias}...")
                val apkOut = File(outputDir, "${safePkgSlug}_v${spec.versionName}_$timestampTag.apk")
                ApkSigner.sign(
                    unsignedApk = unsignedApk,
                    signedApk = apkOut,
                    signerName = spec.keyAlias,
                    privateKey = spec.privateKey,
                    certificates = spec.certificateChain,
                    minSdkVersion = spec.minSdk,
                )
                signedApk = apkOut
            }
            if (config.wantAab && bundleConfigPb != null && protoManifestPb != null && protoResourcesPb != null) {
                logAndEmit(BuildStep.SIGNING, 86, "امضای CMS/PKCS#7 بسته AAB...")
                val aabOut = File(outputDir, "${safePkgSlug}_v${spec.versionName}_$timestampTag.aab")
                AabPackager.packageAndSignAab(
                    bundleConfigPb = bundleConfigPb,
                    protoManifestPb = protoManifestPb,
                    protoResourcesPb = protoResourcesPb,
                    dexBytes = dexBytes,
                    iconPng = generatedRes.iconPng,
                    roundIconPng = generatedRes.roundIconPng,
                    splashPng = generatedRes.splashPng,
                    configJsonBytes = generatedRes.configJsonBytes,
                    outputAab = aabOut,
                    signerAlias = spec.keyAlias,
                    privateKey = spec.privateKey,
                    certificates = spec.certificateChain,
                )
                signedAab = aabOut
            }

            // Stage 7: Validating
            if (signedApk != null) {
                logAndEmit(BuildStep.VALIDATING, 91, "اعتبارسنجی رمزنگاری امضا و ساختار ۱۰گانه فایل APK...")
                val validation = OutputValidator.validateApk(signedApk, expectedSpec = spec)
                if (!validation.isValid) {
                    signedApk.delete()
                    throw IllegalStateException("اعتبارسنجی خروجی APK ناموفق بود: ${validation.summary}")
                }
                logAndEmit(BuildStep.VALIDATING, 94, "خروجی APK تأیید شد: ${validation.summary}")
                artifacts.add(
                    GeneratedArtifactInfo(
                        format = BuildOutputFormat.APK,
                        filePath = signedApk.absolutePath,
                        fileSizeBytes = signedApk.length(),
                        isSigned = validation.isSigned,
                        isVerified = validation.isValid,
                        sha256CertFingerprint = validation.certSha256.ifEmpty { certSha256 },
                        validationSummary = validation.summary,
                    )
                )
            }
            if (signedAab != null) {
                logAndEmit(BuildStep.VALIDATING, 96, "اعتبارسنجی ساختار Protobuf و امضای CMS بسته AAB...")
                val validation = OutputValidator.validateAab(signedAab, expectedSpec = spec)
                if (!validation.isValid) {
                    signedAab.delete()
                    throw IllegalStateException("اعتبارسنجی خروجی AAB ناموفق بود: ${validation.summary}")
                }
                logAndEmit(BuildStep.VALIDATING, 98, "خروجی AAB تأیید شد: ${validation.summary}")
                artifacts.add(
                    GeneratedArtifactInfo(
                        format = BuildOutputFormat.AAB,
                        filePath = signedAab.absolutePath,
                        fileSizeBytes = signedAab.length(),
                        isSigned = validation.isSigned,
                        isVerified = validation.isValid,
                        sha256CertFingerprint = validation.certSha256.ifEmpty { certSha256 },
                        validationSummary = validation.summary,
                    )
                )
            }

            val totalMs = System.currentTimeMillis() - startTime
            logAndEmit(BuildStep.COMPLETED, 100, "ساخت با موفقیت کامل شد (${artifacts.size} خروجی معتبر در ${totalMs}ms).")

            BuildExecutionResult(
                success = true,
                artifacts = artifacts,
                logs = logs,
                durationMs = totalMs,
            )
        } catch (cancelled: BuildCancelledException) {
            artifacts.forEach { File(it.filePath).delete() }
            val msg = cancelled.message ?: "عملیات ساخت توسط کاربر لغو شد."
            logs.add(
                BuildLogEntry(
                    timestamp = System.currentTimeMillis(),
                    level = "WARN",
                    step = currentStep,
                    message = msg,
                )
            )
            BuildExecutionResult(
                success = false,
                artifacts = emptyList(),
                logs = logs,
                durationMs = System.currentTimeMillis() - startTime,
                errorMessage = msg,
                cancelled = true,
            )
        } catch (t: Throwable) {
            artifacts.forEach { File(it.filePath).delete() }
            val errMsg = t.message ?: t.javaClass.simpleName
            logs.add(
                BuildLogEntry(
                    timestamp = System.currentTimeMillis(),
                    level = "ERROR",
                    step = currentStep,
                    message = errMsg,
                )
            )
            BuildExecutionResult(
                success = false,
                artifacts = emptyList(),
                logs = logs,
                durationMs = System.currentTimeMillis() - startTime,
                errorMessage = errMsg,
                cancelled = false,
            )
        } finally {
            BuildStorageManager.cleanupWorkDir(workDir)
        }
    }
}
package com.example.buildengine

import com.android.apksig.ApkVerifier
import kotlinx.serialization.json.Json
import kotlinx.serialization.json.intOrNull
import kotlinx.serialization.json.jsonObject
import kotlinx.serialization.json.jsonPrimitive
import org.bouncycastle.cert.X509CertificateHolder
import org.bouncycastle.cert.jcajce.JcaX509CertificateConverter
import org.bouncycastle.cms.CMSProcessableByteArray
import org.bouncycastle.cms.CMSSignedData
import org.bouncycastle.cms.jcajce.JcaSimpleSignerInfoVerifierBuilder
import org.bouncycastle.jce.provider.BouncyCastleProvider
import java.io.File
import java.io.InputStream
import java.nio.ByteBuffer
import java.nio.ByteOrder
import java.security.MessageDigest
import java.security.Security
import java.security.cert.X509Certificate
import java.util.zip.ZipFile

/**
 * Cryptographic and structural validator for compiled APK and AAB artifacts.
 * Ensures incomplete, tampered, mismatched, or unsigned outputs are never marked valid.
 */
object OutputValidator {

    init {
        if (Security.getProvider(BouncyCastleProvider.PROVIDER_NAME) == null) {
            Security.addProvider(BouncyCastleProvider())
        }
    }

    // افزودن تابع کمکی برای خواندن امن بایت‌ها در همه نسخه‌های اندروید
    private fun InputStream.readBytesSafely(maxBytes: Int): ByteArray {
        val buffer = ByteArray(maxBytes)
        var bytesRead = 0
        while (bytesRead < maxBytes) {
            val result = this.read(buffer, bytesRead, maxBytes - bytesRead)
            if (result == -1) break
            bytesRead += result
        }
        return if (bytesRead == 0) ByteArray(0) else if (bytesRead < maxBytes) buffer.copyOf(bytesRead) else buffer
    }

    data class ManifestIdentity(
        val packageName: String? = null,
        val versionCode: Int? = null,
        val versionName: String? = null,
    )

    data class ValidationReport(
        val isValid: Boolean,
        val isSigned: Boolean,
        val v1Verified: Boolean,
        val v2Verified: Boolean,
        val v3Verified: Boolean,
        val certSha256: String,
        val summary: String,
        val errors: List<String> = emptyList(),
        val parsedManifest: ManifestIdentity? = null,
    )

    fun validateApk(apkFile: File, expectedSpec: BuildSpec? = null): ValidationReport {
        if (!apkFile.exists() || apkFile.length() < 1024L) {
            return ValidationReport(
                isValid = false,
                isSigned = false,
                v1Verified = false,
                v2Verified = false,
                v3Verified = false,
                certSha256 = "",
                summary = "فایل APK وجود ندارد یا حجم آن نامعتبر است.",
                errors = listOf("Missing or empty APK file"),
            )
        }

        val structuralErrors = mutableListOf<String>()
        var parsedManifest: ManifestIdentity? = null

        try {
            ZipFile(apkFile).use { zip ->
                val manifestEntry = zip.getEntry("AndroidManifest.xml")
                val arscEntry = zip.getEntry("resources.arsc")
                val dexEntry = zip.getEntry("classes.dex")
                val iconEntry = zip.getEntry(ArscCompiler.ICON_APK_PATH)
                val roundIconEntry = zip.getEntry(ArscCompiler.ICON_ROUND_APK_PATH)
                val configEntry = zip.getEntry("assets/webino_config.json")

                if (manifestEntry == null || manifestEntry.size < 32) {
                    structuralErrors.add("Missing binary AndroidManifest.xml")
                } else {
                    val manifestBytes = zip.getInputStream(manifestEntry).use { it.readBytes() }
                    if (manifestBytes.size < 8 || manifestBytes[0] != 0x03.toByte() || manifestBytes[1] != 0x00.toByte()) {
                        structuralErrors.add("Invalid AXML binary header in AndroidManifest.xml")
                    } else {
                        parsedManifest = parseBinaryManifestIdentity(manifestBytes)
                        if (parsedManifest?.packageName.isNullOrBlank()) {
                            structuralErrors.add("Binary AndroidManifest.xml missing package attribute")
                        }
                        if (expectedSpec != null && parsedManifest != null) {
                            if (parsedManifest!!.packageName != expectedSpec.packageName) {
                                structuralErrors.add("Package name mismatch: expected ${expectedSpec.packageName}, got ${parsedManifest!!.packageName}")
                            }
                            if (parsedManifest!!.versionCode != expectedSpec.versionCode) {
                                structuralErrors.add("Version code mismatch: expected ${expectedSpec.versionCode}, got ${parsedManifest!!.versionCode}")
                            }
                            if (parsedManifest!!.versionName != expectedSpec.versionName) {
                                structuralErrors.add("Version name mismatch: expected ${expectedSpec.versionName}, got ${parsedManifest!!.versionName}")
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                if (arscEntry == null || arscEntry.size < 32) {
                    structuralErrors.add("Missing resources.arsc")
                } else {
                    // استفاده از تابع امن جایگزین
                    val arscHdr = zip.getInputStream(arscEntry).use { it.readBytesSafely(4) }
                    if (arscHdr.size < 4 || arscHdr[0] != 0x02.toByte() || arscHdr[1] != 0x00.toByte()) {
                        structuralErrors.add("Invalid RES_TABLE_TYPE header in resources.arsc")
                    }
                }

                if (dexEntry == null || dexEntry.size < 112) {
                    structuralErrors.add("Missing classes.dex")
                } else {
                    // استفاده از تابع امن جایگزین
                    val dexHdr = zip.getInputStream(dexEntry).use { it.readBytesSafely(4) }
                    if (String(dexHdr, Charsets.US_ASCII) != "dex\n") {
                        structuralErrors.add("Invalid DEX magic header in classes.dex")
                    }
                }

                if (iconEntry == null || iconEntry.size < 32) {
                    structuralErrors.add("Missing launcher icon: ${ArscCompiler.ICON_APK_PATH}")
                } else {
                    // استفاده از تابع امن جایگزین
                    val pngHdr = zip.getInputStream(iconEntry).use { it.readBytesSafely(4) }
                    if (!isPngHeader(pngHdr)) {
                        structuralErrors.add("Invalid PNG header in ${ArscCompiler.ICON_APK_PATH}")
                    }
                }

                if (roundIconEntry == null || roundIconEntry.size < 32) {
                    structuralErrors.add("Missing round launcher icon: ${ArscCompiler.ICON_ROUND_APK_PATH}")
                }

                if (configEntry == null || configEntry.size < 10) {
                    structuralErrors.add("Missing assets/webino_config.json")
                }
            }
        } catch (t: Throwable) {
            structuralErrors.add("Invalid APK archive structure: ${t.message ?: t.javaClass.simpleName}")
        }

        val verifierResult = runCatching {
            ApkVerifier.Builder(apkFile)
                .setMinCheckedPlatformVersion(minOf(expectedSpec?.minSdk ?: 21, 21))
                .build()
                .verify()
        }.getOrElse { err ->
            return ValidationReport(
                isValid = false,
                isSigned = false,
                v1Verified = false,
                v2Verified = false,
                v3Verified = false,
                certSha256 = "",
                summary = "خطا در بررسی امضای APK: ${err.message}",
                errors = structuralErrors + listOf(err.message ?: "ApkVerifier error"),
                parsedManifest = parsedManifest,
            )
        }

        val errors = structuralErrors + verifierResult.errors.map { it.toString() }
        val certSha256 = verifierResult.signerCertificates.firstOrNull()?.let(::sha256Fingerprint).orEmpty()
        val verified = verifierResult.isVerified && structuralErrors.isEmpty()

        val summary = if (verified) {
            val pkgTag = parsedManifest?.packageName?.let { "$it • " }.orEmpty()
            "${pkgTag}تأیید شده (v1=${verifierResult.isVerifiedUsingV1Scheme}, v2=${verifierResult.isVerifiedUsingV2Scheme}, v3=${verifierResult.isVerifiedUsingV3Scheme})"
        } else {
            "اعتبارسنجی ناموفق: ${errors.firstOrNull() ?: "خطای امضای دیجیتال"}"
        }

        return ValidationReport(
            isValid = verified,
            isSigned = verifierResult.signerCertificates.isNotEmpty(),
            v1Verified = verifierResult.isVerifiedUsingV1Scheme,
            v2Verified = verifierResult.isVerifiedUsingV2Scheme,
            v3Verified = verifierResult.isVerifiedUsingV3Scheme,
            certSha256 = certSha256,
            summary = summary,
            errors = errors,
            parsedManifest = parsedManifest,
        )
    }

    fun validateAab(aabFile: File, expectedSpec: BuildSpec? = null): ValidationReport {
        if (!aabFile.exists() || aabFile.length() < 1024L) {
            return ValidationReport(
                isValid = false,
                isSigned = false,
                v1Verified = false,
                v2Verified = false,
                v3Verified = false,
                certSha256 = "",
                summary = "فایل AAB نامعتبر است.",
                errors = listOf("Missing or empty AAB file"),
            )
        }

        val errors = mutableListOf<String>()
        var hasSigVerified = false
        var certSha256 = ""
        var parsedManifest: ManifestIdentity? = null

        try {
            ZipFile(aabFile).use { zip ->
                val required = listOf(
                    "BundleConfig.pb",
                    "base/manifest/AndroidManifest.xml",
                    "base/resources.pb",
                    "base/dex/classes.dex",
                    "base/${ArscCompiler.ICON_APK_PATH}",
                    "base/${ArscCompiler.ICON_ROUND_APK_PATH}",
                    "base/assets/webino_config.json",
                    "META-INF/MANIFEST.MF",
                )
                required.forEach { path ->
                    val e = zip.getEntry(path)
                    if (e == null || e.size <= 0) {
                        errors.add("Missing required AAB entry: $path")
                    }
                }

                zip.getEntry("base/dex/classes.dex")?.let { dexEntry ->
                    // استفاده از تابع امن جایگزین
                    val hdr = zip.getInputStream(dexEntry).use { it.readBytesSafely(4) }
                    if (String(hdr, Charsets.US_ASCII) != "dex\n") {
                        errors.add("Invalid DEX magic in base/dex/classes.dex")
                    }
                }

                zip.getEntry("base/${ArscCompiler.ICON_APK_PATH}")?.let { iconEntry ->
                    // استفاده از تابع امن جایگزین
                    val hdr = zip.getInputStream(iconEntry).use { it.readBytesSafely(4) }
                    if (!isPngHeader(hdr)) {
                        errors.add("Invalid PNG icon in AAB")
                    }
                }

                zip.getEntry("base/assets/webino_config.json")?.let { cfgEntry ->
                    val cfgText = zip.getInputStream(cfgEntry).use { it.readBytes().toString(Charsets.UTF_8) }
                    runCatching {
                        val obj = Json.parseToJsonElement(cfgText).jsonObject
                        val pkg = obj["packageName"]?.jsonPrimitive?.content
                        val verName = obj["versionName"]?.jsonPrimitive?.content
                        val verCode = obj["versionCode"]?.jsonPrimitive?.intOrNull
                        parsedManifest = ManifestIdentity(pkg, verCode, verName)
                        if (expectedSpec != null) {
                            if (pkg != expectedSpec.packageName) {
                                errors.add("AAB package mismatch: expected ${expectedSpec.packageName}, got $pkg")
                            }
                            if (verCode != expectedSpec.versionCode) {
                                errors.add("AAB versionCode mismatch: expected ${expectedSpec.versionCode}, got $verCode")
                            }
                            if (verName != expectedSpec.versionName) {
                                errors.add("AAB versionName mismatch: expected ${expectedSpec.versionName}, got $verName")
                            }
                        }
                    }.onFailure {
                        errors.add("Invalid JSON in base/assets/webino_config.json")
                    }
                }

                var sfEntryName: String? = null
                var sigBlockName: String? = null
                val entries = zip.entries()
                while (entries.hasMoreElements()) {
                    val name = entries.nextElement().name
                    if (name.startsWith("META-INF/") && name.endsWith(".SF")) {
                        sfEntryName = name
                    } else if (name.startsWith("META-INF/") && (name.endsWith(".RSA") || name.endsWith(".EC"))) {
                        sigBlockName = name
                    }
                }

                if (sfEntryName == null || sigBlockName == null) {
                    errors.add("Missing META-INF JAR signature (.SF / .RSA / .EC)")
                } else {
                    val sfBytes = zip.getInputStream(zip.getEntry(sfEntryName)).use { it.readBytes() }
                    val sigBytes = zip.getInputStream(zip.getEntry(sigBlockName)).use { it.readBytes() }
                    runCatching {
                        val cms = CMSSignedData(CMSProcessableByteArray(sfBytes), sigBytes)
                        val certStore = cms.certificates
                        val signer = cms.signerInfos.signers.firstOrNull()
                            ?: error("No CMS SignerInformation found")
                        @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
                        val matches = certStore.getMatches(signer.sid as org.bouncycastle.util.Selector<X509CertificateHolder>)
                        val certHolder = matches.firstOrNull() ?: error("No signer certificate in CMS store")
                        val x509 = JcaX509CertificateConverter()
                            .setProvider(BouncyCastleProvider.PROVIDER_NAME)
                            .getCertificate(certHolder)
                        val verifier = JcaSimpleSignerInfoVerifierBuilder()
                            .setProvider(BouncyCastleProvider.PROVIDER_NAME)
                            .build(x509)
                        if (signer.verify(verifier)) {
                            hasSigVerified = true
                            certSha256 = sha256Fingerprint(x509)
                        } else {
                            errors.add("CMS signature verification returned false")
                        }
                    }.onFailure { err ->
                        errors.add("Invalid AAB CMS signature: ${err.message ?: err.javaClass.simpleName}")
                    }
                }
            }
        } catch (t: Throwable) {
            errors.add("Invalid AAB archive: ${t.message ?: t.javaClass.simpleName}")
        }

        val valid = errors.isEmpty() && hasSigVerified
        return ValidationReport(
            isValid = valid,
            isSigned = hasSigVerified,
            v1Verified = hasSigVerified,
            v2Verified = false,
            v3Verified = false,
            certSha256 = certSha256,
            summary = if (valid) "بسته AAB با ساختار Protobuf و امضای CMS تأیید شد" else errors.joinToString("; "),
            errors = errors,
            parsedManifest = parsedManifest,
        )
    }

    fun parseBinaryManifestIdentity(axmlBytes: ByteArray): ManifestIdentity? {
        return runCatching {
            val buf = ByteBuffer.wrap(axmlBytes).order(ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN)
            if (buf.capacity() < 8 || buf.getShort(0) != 0x0003.toShort()) return null
            var offset = buf.getShort(2).toInt() and 0xFFFF
            var strings: List<String> = emptyList()

            while (offset + 8 <= axmlBytes.size) {
                val chunkType = buf.getShort(offset).toInt() and 0xFFFF
                val headerSize = buf.getShort(offset + 2).toInt() and 0xFFFF
                val chunkSize = buf.getInt(offset + 4)
                if (chunkSize <= 0 || offset + chunkSize > axmlBytes.size) break

                when (chunkType) {
                    0x0001 -> {
                        strings = decodeStringPool(axmlBytes, offset, chunkSize)
                    }
                    0x0102 -> {
                        // RES_XML_START_ELEMENT_TYPE
                        val nameIdx = buf.getInt(offset + 20)
                        val tagName = strings.getOrNull(nameIdx)
                        if (tagName == "manifest") {
                            val attrStart = buf.getShort(offset + 24).toInt() and 0xFFFF
                            val attrSize = buf.getShort(offset + 26).toInt() and 0xFFFF
                            val attrCount = buf.getShort(offset + 28).toInt() and 0xFFFF
                            var pkg: String? = null
                            var verCode: Int? = null
                            var verName: String? = null
                            val attrsBase = offset + 16 + attrStart
                            for (i in 0 until attrCount) {
                                val pos = attrsBase + i * attrSize
                                if (pos + 20 > offset + chunkSize) break
                                val attrNameIdx = buf.getInt(pos + 4)
                                val rawValueIdx = buf.getInt(pos + 8)
                                val valueType = buf.get(pos + 15).toInt() and 0xFF
                                val valueData = buf.getInt(pos + 16)
                                val attrName = strings.getOrNull(attrNameIdx)
                                when (attrName) {
                                    "package" -> {
                                        pkg = when (valueType) {
                                            0x03 -> strings.getOrNull(valueData)
                                            else -> strings.getOrNull(rawValueIdx)
                                        }
                                    }
                                    "versionCode" -> {
                                        if (valueType == 0x10 || valueType == 0x11) {
                                            verCode = valueData
                                        }
                                    }
                                    "versionName" -> {
                                        verName = when (valueType) {
                                            0x03 -> strings.getOrNull(valueData)
                                            else -> strings.getOrNull(rawValueIdx)
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                            return ManifestIdentity(pkg, verCode, verName)
                        }
                    }
                }
                offset += if (chunkType == 0x0003) headerSize else chunkSize
            }
            null
        }.getOrNull()
    }

    private fun decodeStringPool(data: ByteArray, poolOffset: Int, poolSize: Int): List<String> {
        val buf = ByteBuffer.wrap(data).order(ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN)
        val stringCount = buf.getInt(poolOffset + 8)
        val flags = buf.getInt(poolOffset + 16)
        val stringsStart = buf.getInt(poolOffset + 20)
        val isUtf8 = (flags and 0x100) != 0
        val out = ArrayList<String>(stringCount)
        for (i in 0 until stringCount) {
            val strRelOffset = buf.getInt(poolOffset + 28 + i * 4)
            var pos = poolOffset + stringsStart + strRelOffset
            if (pos < poolOffset || pos >= poolOffset + poolSize) {
                out.add("")
                continue
            }
            if (isUtf8) {
                // Skip char count (1-2 bytes), read byte length (1-2 bytes)
                val c1 = data[pos++].toInt() and 0xFF
                if ((c1 and 0x80) != 0) pos++
                var byteLen = data[pos++].toInt() and 0xFF
                if ((byteLen and 0x80) != 0) {
                    val b2 = data[pos++].toInt() and 0xFF
                    byteLen = ((byteLen and 0x7F) shl 8) or b2
                }
                val safeLen = byteLen.coerceAtMost((poolOffset + poolSize) - pos)
                out.add(String(data, pos, safeLen, Charsets.UTF_8))
            } else {
                var len = ((data[pos].toInt() and 0xFF) or ((data[pos + 1].toInt() and 0xFF) shl 8))
                pos += 2
                if ((len and 0x8000) != 0) {
                    val low = ((data[pos].toInt() and 0xFF) or ((data[pos + 1].toInt() and 0xFF) shl 8))
                    pos += 2
                    len = ((len and 0x7FFF) shl 16) or low
                }
                val chars = CharArray(len)
                for (c in 0 until len) {
                    if (pos + 1 >= poolOffset + poolSize) break
                    val code = (data[pos].toInt() and 0xFF) or ((data[pos + 1].toInt() and 0xFF) shl 8)
                    chars[c] = code.toChar()
                    pos += 2
                }
                out.add(String(chars))
            }
        }
        return out
    }

    private fun isPngHeader(bytes: ByteArray): Boolean =
        bytes.size >= 4 &&
            bytes[0] == 0x89.toByte() &&
            bytes[1] == 0x50.toByte() &&
            bytes[2] == 0x4E.toByte() &&
            bytes[3] == 0x47.toByte()

    fun sha256Fingerprint(cert: X509Certificate): String {
        val digest = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256").digest(cert.encoded)
        return digest.joinToString(":") { "%02X".format(it) }
    }
}package com.example.buildengine

import com.example.domain.model.CacheModeOption
import com.example.domain.model.MixedContentOption
import com.example.domain.model.SslPolicy
import com.example.domain.model.ToolbarStyle
import java.io.File

/**
 * Generates clean, self-contained Java 8 source code for the target standalone
 * Android WebView application tailored to the user's BuildSpec with full Prompt 04 parity.
 */
object ProjectGenerator {

    fun generateSources(spec: BuildSpec, srcRootDir: File): List<File> {
        val pkgDir = File(srcRootDir, spec.packageName.replace('.', '/'))
        pkgDir.mkdirs()

        val mainActivityFile = File(pkgDir, "MainActivity.java")
        mainActivityFile.writeText(buildMainActivitySource(spec), Charsets.UTF_8)
        return listOf(mainActivityFile)
    }

    private fun escapeJava(raw: String): String =
        raw.replace("\\", "\\\\")
            .replace("\"", "\\\"")
            .replace("\n", "\\n")
            .replace("\r", "")

    private fun buildMainActivitySource(spec: BuildSpec): String {
        val initialUrl = buildString {
            append(spec.websiteUrl.trimEnd('/'))
            val path = spec.startPagePath.trim()
            if (path.isNotEmpty()) {
                if (!path.startsWith("/")) append("/")
                append(path)
            }
        }
        val primaryColor = spec.primaryColorHex.ifBlank { "#7C3AED" }
        val bgColor = spec.backgroundColorHex.ifBlank { "#0B1026" }
        val cacheConst = when (spec.cacheMode) {
            CacheModeOption.DEFAULT -> "WebSettings.LOAD_DEFAULT"
            CacheModeOption.NO_CACHE -> "WebSettings.LOAD_NO_CACHE"
            CacheModeOption.CACHE_ELSE_NETWORK -> "WebSettings.LOAD_CACHE_ELSE_NETWORK"
        }
        val mixedConst = when (spec.mixedContentMode) {
            MixedContentOption.NEVER_ALLOW -> "WebSettings.MIXED_CONTENT_NEVER_ALLOW"
            MixedContentOption.COMPATIBILITY -> "WebSettings.MIXED_CONTENT_COMPATIBILITY_MODE"
        }
        val showTopBar = spec.toolbarStyle == ToolbarStyle.TOP_BAR
        val showBottomBar = spec.toolbarStyle == ToolbarStyle.BOTTOM_BAR || spec.toolbarStyle == ToolbarStyle.FLOATING_PILL
        val isDark = spec.themePreset != com.example.domain.model.ThemePreset.LIGHT
        val textOnBg = if (isDark) "#F8FAFC" else "#0F172A"
        val errorCardBg = if (isDark) "#161E3D" else "#FFFFFF"

        return """
package ${spec.packageName};

import android.Manifest;
import android.app.Activity;
import android.app.AlertDialog;
import android.app.DownloadManager;
import android.content.ClipData;
import android.content.ClipboardManager;
import android.content.Context;
import android.content.DialogInterface;
import android.content.Intent;
import android.content.pm.PackageManager;
import android.graphics.Bitmap;
import android.graphics.BitmapFactory;
import android.graphics.Color;
import android.graphics.Typeface;
import android.graphics.drawable.GradientDrawable;
import android.location.LocationManager;
import android.net.ConnectivityManager;
import android.net.Network;
import android.net.NetworkCapabilities;
import android.net.Uri;
import android.net.http.SslError;
import android.os.Build;
import android.os.Bundle;
import android.os.Environment;
import android.os.Handler;
import android.os.Looper;
import android.os.Message;
import android.util.TypedValue;
import android.view.Gravity;
import android.view.KeyEvent;
import android.view.View;
import android.view.ViewGroup;
import android.view.Window;
import android.view.WindowManager;
import android.webkit.CookieManager;
import android.webkit.DownloadListener;
import android.webkit.GeolocationPermissions;

public class MainActivity extends Activity {

    private static final String APP_NAME = "${escapeJava(spec.appName)}";
