        val multipleWindowsEnabled: Boolean = false,
        val popupsEnabled: Boolean = false,
        val popupBehavior: String = "SAME_PAGE",
        val customUserAgent: String = "",
        val cacheMode: String = "DEFAULT",
        val zoomEnabled: Boolean = false,
        val mixedContentMode: String = "NEVER_ALLOW",
        val sslPolicy: String = "STRICT_BLOCK",
        val navigationRulesRaw: String = "",
        val httpsOnly: Boolean = true,
    )

    fun exportToJson(bundle: FullProjectBundle): String {
        val s = bundle.settings
        val w = bundle.webSettings
        val dto = ExportedProjectConfig(
            appName = s?.appName?.ifBlank { bundle.project.name } ?: bundle.project.name,
            websiteUrl = bundle.project.websiteUrl,
            packageName = bundle.project.packageName,
            versionName = bundle.project.versionName,
            versionCode = bundle.project.versionCode,
            description = bundle.project.description,
            templateId = bundle.project.templateId,
            developerName = s?.developerName.orEmpty(),
            primaryColorHex = s?.primaryColorHex ?: "#7C3AED",
            backgroundColorHex = s?.backgroundColorHex ?: "#0B1026",
            themePreset = s?.themePreset ?: "DARK",
            fontName = s?.fontName ?: "VAZIRMATN",
            toolbarStyle = s?.toolbarStyle ?: "TOP_BAR",
            startPagePath = s?.startPagePath.orEmpty(),
            loadingAnimation = s?.loadingAnimation ?: "PROGRESS_BAR",
            splashEnabled = s?.splashEnabled ?: true,
            splashDurationMs = s?.splashDurationMs ?: 1400,
            orientationMode = s?.orientationMode ?: "AUTO",
            fullscreenMode = s?.fullscreenMode ?: false,
            showStatusBar = s?.showStatusBar ?: true,
            showNavigationBar = s?.showNavigationBar ?: true,
            backBehavior = s?.backBehavior ?: "HISTORY_THEN_EXIT",
            supportRtl = s?.supportRtl ?: true,
            enableDeepLinks = s?.enableDeepLinks ?: false,
            deepLinkScheme = s?.deepLinkScheme ?: "webino",
            enableAppLinks = s?.enableAppLinks ?: false,
            appLinkHost = s?.appLinkHost.orEmpty(),
            enableFileSharing = s?.enableFileSharing ?: true,
            enablePhoneDialer = s?.enablePhoneDialer ?: true,
            enableEmailIntent = s?.enableEmailIntent ?: true,
            enableMapsIntent = s?.enableMapsIntent ?: true,
            enablePaymentGatewayBridge = s?.enablePaymentGatewayBridge ?: true,
            externalLinkMode = s?.externalLinkMode ?: "ASK_OR_ALLOWLIST",
            web = ExportedWebSettings(
                javaScriptEnabled = w?.javaScriptEnabled ?: true,
                domStorageEnabled = w?.domStorageEnabled ?: true,
                webSqlEnabled = w?.webSqlEnabled ?: false,
                clipboardAccessEnabled = w?.clipboardAccessEnabled ?: true,
                cookiesEnabled = w?.cookiesEnabled ?: true,
                thirdPartyCookiesEnabled = w?.thirdPartyCookiesEnabled ?: false,
                fileUploadEnabled = w?.fileUploadEnabled ?: true,
                downloadManagerEnabled = w?.downloadManagerEnabled ?: true,
                cameraPermission = w?.cameraPermission ?: false,
                microphonePermission = w?.microphonePermission ?: false,
                geolocationEnabled = w?.geolocationEnabled ?: false,
                webRtcEnabled = w?.webRtcEnabled ?: false,
                fullscreenVideoEnabled = w?.fullscreenVideoEnabled ?: true,
                multipleWindowsEnabled = w?.multipleWindowsEnabled ?: false,
                popupsEnabled = w?.popupsEnabled ?: false,
                popupBehavior = w?.popupBehavior ?: "SAME_PAGE",
                customUserAgent = w?.customUserAgent.orEmpty(),
                cacheMode = w?.cacheMode ?: "DEFAULT",
                zoomEnabled = w?.zoomEnabled ?: false,
                mixedContentMode = w?.mixedContentMode ?: "NEVER_ALLOW",
                sslPolicy = w?.sslPolicy ?: "STRICT_BLOCK",
                navigationRulesRaw = w?.navigationRulesRaw.orEmpty(),
                httpsOnly = w?.httpsOnly ?: true,
            ),
        )
        return json.encodeToString(dto)
    }

    fun parseAndValidate(rawJson: String): Result<ExportedProjectConfig> = runCatching {
        val bytes = rawJson.toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8)
        require(bytes.isNotEmpty() && bytes.size <= MAX_IMPORT_BYTES) {
            "حجم فایل JSON نامعتبر یا بیش از حد مجاز است."
        }
        val parsed = json.decodeFromString<ExportedProjectConfig>(rawJson)
        require(parsed.schemaVersion in 1..SCHEMA_VERSION) {
            "نسخه فایل پیکربندی پشتیبانی نمی‌شود."
        }
        val cleanName = parsed.appName.trim()
        require(cleanName.isNotEmpty() && cleanName.length <= 64) {
            "نام اپلیکیشن در فایل JSON معتبر نیست."
        }
        val cleanUrl = ValidationUtils.normalizeUrl(parsed.websiteUrl)
        require(safeSchemeRegex.matches(cleanUrl) && ValidationUtils.isValidWebUrl(cleanUrl)) {
            "آدرس وب‌سایت در فایل JSON غیرمجاز یا ناامن است."
        }
        require(ValidationUtils.isValidPackageName(parsed.packageName.trim())) {
            "نام بسته (Package Name) در فایل JSON معتبر نیست."
        }
        require(hexColorRegex.matches(parsed.primaryColorHex) && hexColorRegex.matches(parsed.backgroundColorHex)) {
            "کد رنگ در فایل JSON معتبر نیست."
        }
        require(parsed.versionCode in 1..1_000_000) {
            "کد نسخه نامعتبر است."
        }
        parsed.copy(
            appName = cleanName,
            websiteUrl = cleanUrl,
            packageName = parsed.packageName.trim(),
            splashDurationMs = parsed.splashDurationMs.coerceIn(400, 5000),
            startPagePath = parsed.startPagePath.take(256),
            deepLinkScheme = parsed.deepLinkScheme.filter { it.isLetterOrDigit() || it == '-' }.ifBlank { "webino" },
        )
    }

    fun toEntities(config: ExportedProjectConfig, projectId: Long = 0L): Triple<ProjectEntity, ProjectSettingsEntity, WebSettingsEntity> {
        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
        val project = ProjectEntity(
            id = projectId,
            name = config.appName,
            websiteUrl = config.websiteUrl,
            packageName = config.packageName,
            versionName = config.versionName.ifBlank { "1.0.0" }.take(24),
            versionCode = config.versionCode,
            description = config.description.take(500),
            templateId = config.templateId,
            createdAt = now,
            updatedAt = now,
        )
        val settings = ProjectSettingsEntity(
            projectId = projectId,
            appName = config.appName,
            developerName = config.developerName.take(64),
            primaryColorHex = config.primaryColorHex,
            backgroundColorHex = config.backgroundColorHex,
            themePreset = config.themePreset,
            fontName = config.fontName,
            toolbarStyle = config.toolbarStyle,
            startPagePath = config.startPagePath,
            loadingAnimation = config.loadingAnimation,
            splashEnabled = config.splashEnabled,
            splashDurationMs = config.splashDurationMs,
            orientationMode = config.orientationMode,
            fullscreenMode = config.fullscreenMode,
            showStatusBar = config.showStatusBar,
            showNavigationBar = config.showNavigationBar,
            backBehavior = config.backBehavior,
            supportRtl = config.supportRtl,
            enableDeepLinks = config.enableDeepLinks,
            deepLinkScheme = config.deepLinkScheme,
            enableAppLinks = config.enableAppLinks,
            appLinkHost = config.appLinkHost.take(128),
            enableFileSharing = config.enableFileSharing,
            enablePhoneDialer = config.enablePhoneDialer,
            enableEmailIntent = config.enableEmailIntent,
            enableMapsIntent = config.enableMapsIntent,
            enablePaymentGatewayBridge = config.enablePaymentGatewayBridge,
            externalLinkMode = config.externalLinkMode,
        )
        val web = WebSettingsEntity(
            projectId = projectId,
            javaScriptEnabled = config.web.javaScriptEnabled,
            domStorageEnabled = config.web.domStorageEnabled,
            webSqlEnabled = config.web.webSqlEnabled,
            clipboardAccessEnabled = config.web.clipboardAccessEnabled,
            cookiesEnabled = config.web.cookiesEnabled,
            thirdPartyCookiesEnabled = config.web.thirdPartyCookiesEnabled,
            fileUploadEnabled = config.web.fileUploadEnabled,
            downloadManagerEnabled = config.web.downloadManagerEnabled,
            cameraPermission = config.web.cameraPermission,
            microphonePermission = config.web.microphonePermission,
            geolocationEnabled = config.web.geolocationEnabled,
            webRtcEnabled = config.web.webRtcEnabled,
            fullscreenVideoEnabled = config.web.fullscreenVideoEnabled,
            multipleWindowsEnabled = config.web.multipleWindowsEnabled,
            popupsEnabled = config.web.popupsEnabled,
            popupBehavior = config.web.popupBehavior,
            customUserAgent = config.web.customUserAgent.take(256),
            cacheMode = config.web.cacheMode,
            zoomEnabled = config.web.zoomEnabled,
            mixedContentMode = config.web.mixedContentMode,
            sslPolicy = config.web.sslPolicy,
            navigationRulesRaw = config.web.navigationRulesRaw.take(1024),
            httpsOnly = config.web.httpsOnly,
        )
        return Triple(project, settings, web)
    }
}
package com.example.core

import java.net.URI
import java.util.Locale
import java.util.regex.Pattern

object ValidationUtils {

    private val PACKAGE_REGEX = Pattern.compile("^[a-z][a-z0-9_]*(\\.[a-z][a-z0-9_]*){1,6}$")
    private val HEX_COLOR_REGEX = Pattern.compile("^#([A-Fa-f0-9]{6}|[A-Fa-f0-9]{8})$")

    fun normalizeUrl(raw: String): String {
        val trimmed = raw.trim()
        if (trimmed.isEmpty()) return ""
        return if (trimmed.startsWith("http://", ignoreCase = true) ||
            trimmed.startsWith("https://", ignoreCase = true)
        ) {
            trimmed
        } else {
            "https://$trimmed"
        }
    }

    fun isValidWebUrl(raw: String): Boolean {
        val normalized = normalizeUrl(raw)
        if (normalized.isBlank()) return false
        return try {
            val uri = URI(normalized)
            val scheme = uri.scheme?.lowercase(Locale.US)
            val host = uri.host
            (scheme == "https" || scheme == "http") && !host.isNullOrBlank() && host.contains('.')
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            false
        }
    }

    fun isHttpsUrl(raw: String): Boolean {
        val normalized = normalizeUrl(raw)
        return normalized.startsWith("https://", ignoreCase = true)
    }

    fun isIranDomain(raw: String): Boolean {
        val normalized = normalizeUrl(raw)
        return try {
            val host = URI(normalized).host?.lowercase(Locale.US) ?: return false
            host.endsWith(".ir") || host.endsWith(".xn--mgba3a4f16a")
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            false
        }
    }

    fun extractHost(raw: String): String {
        val normalized = normalizeUrl(raw)
        return try {
            URI(normalized).host?.lowercase(Locale.US) ?: ""
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            ""
        }
    }

    /** Reserved Java words cannot be package segments: the generated Java sources would not compile. */
    private val JAVA_KEYWORDS = setOf(
        "abstract", "assert", "boolean", "break", "byte", "case", "catch", "char", "class", "const",
        "continue", "default", "do", "double", "else", "enum", "extends", "final", "finally", "float",
        "for", "goto", "if", "implements", "import", "instanceof", "int", "interface", "long", "native",
        "new", "package", "private", "protected", "public", "return", "short", "static", "strictfp",
        "super", "switch", "synchronized", "this", "throw", "throws", "transient", "try", "void",
        "volatile", "while", "true", "false", "null",
    )

    fun isValidPackageName(pkg: String): Boolean {
        val trimmed = pkg.trim()
        return PACKAGE_REGEX.matcher(trimmed).matches() &&
            !trimmed.contains("..") &&
            trimmed.split('.').none { it in JAVA_KEYWORDS }
    }

    fun suggestPackageName(url: String, appName: String): String {
        val host = extractHost(url).removePrefix("www.")
        if (host.isNotBlank()) {
            val parts = host.split('.')
                .map { seg -> seg.lowercase(Locale.US).replace(Regex("[^a-z0-9]"), "") }
                .filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
                .reversed()
            if (parts.size >= 2) {
                val safeParts = parts.mapIndexed { idx, p ->
                    val cleaned = if (p.first().isDigit()) "app$p" else p
                    if (cleaned in JAVA_KEYWORDS || (idx == 0 && cleaned in setOf("in", "is", "as"))) {
                        "site$cleaned"
                    } else {
                        cleaned
                    }
                }
                val candidate = safeParts.joinToString(".")
                if (isValidPackageName(candidate)) return candidate
            }
        }
        val slug = appName.lowercase(Locale.US).replace(Regex("[^a-z0-9]"), "").ifBlank { "webapp" }
        val prefix = if (slug.first().isDigit() || slug in JAVA_KEYWORDS) "app$slug" else slug
        return "ir.webino.$prefix"
    }

    fun isValidHexColor(hex: String): Boolean = HEX_COLOR_REGEX.matcher(hex.trim()).matches()

    fun formatFileSize(bytes: Long, isPersian: Boolean, usePersianDigits: Boolean): String {
        val raw = when {
            bytes < 1024L -> if (isPersian) "$bytes بایت" else "$bytes B"
            bytes < 1024L * 1024L -> {
                val kb = String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f", bytes / 1024.0)
                if (isPersian) "$kb کیلوبایت" else "$kb KB"
            }
            bytes < 1024L * 1024L * 1024L -> {
                val mb = String.format(Locale.US, "%.2f", bytes / (1024.0 * 1024.0))
                if (isPersian) "$mb مگابایت" else "$mb MB"
            }
            else -> {
                val gb = String.format(Locale.US, "%.2f", bytes / (1024.0 * 1024.0 * 1024.0))
                if (isPersian) "$gb گیگابایت" else "$gb GB"
            }
        }
        return JalaliCalendar.localizeDigits(raw, usePersianDigits && isPersian)
    }

    fun formatDuration(millis: Long, isPersian: Boolean, usePersianDigits: Boolean): String {
        val totalSec = (millis / 1000L).coerceAtLeast(0L)
        val min = totalSec / 60L
        val sec = totalSec % 60L
        val raw = if (min > 0) {
            if (isPersian) "$min دقیقه و $sec ثانیه" else "${min}m ${sec}s"
        } else {
            if (isPersian) "$sec ثانیه" else "${sec}s"
        }
        return JalaliCalendar.localizeDigits(raw, usePersianDigits && isPersian)
    }
}
package com.example.data.crypto

import android.content.Context
import android.security.keystore.KeyGenParameterSpec
import android.security.keystore.KeyProperties
import android.util.Base64
import com.example.buildengine.OutputValidator
import org.bouncycastle.asn1.x500.X500Name
import org.bouncycastle.cert.jcajce.JcaX509CertificateConverter
import org.bouncycastle.cert.jcajce.JcaX509v3CertificateBuilder
import org.bouncycastle.jce.provider.BouncyCastleProvider
import org.bouncycastle.operator.jcajce.JcaContentSignerBuilder
import java.io.File
import java.math.BigInteger
import java.security.KeyPairGenerator
import java.security.KeyStore
import java.security.MessageDigest
import java.security.PrivateKey
import java.security.SecureRandom
import java.security.Security
import java.security.cert.X509Certificate
import java.util.Date
import javax.crypto.Cipher
import javax.crypto.KeyGenerator
import javax.crypto.SecretKey
import javax.crypto.spec.GCMParameterSpec

data class CreatedKeystoreInfo(
    val alias: String,
    val keystorePath: String,
    val keystoreType: String,
    val commonName: String,
    val organization: String,
    val country: String,
    val validityYears: Int,
    val sha256Fingerprint: String,
    val sha1Fingerprint: String,
    val encryptedPasswordToken: String,
)

data class LoadedSigningMaterial(
    val alias: String,
    val privateKey: PrivateKey,
    val certificateChain: List<X509Certificate>,
)

/**
 * Manages real PKCS#12 signing keystores, X.509 v3 certificate generation, password
 * encryption via AndroidKeyStore AES-GCM, password rotation, import, and verification.
 */
class SigningManager(private val context: Context) {

    init {
        if (Security.getProvider(BouncyCastleProvider.PROVIDER_NAME) == null) {
            Security.addProvider(BouncyCastleProvider())
        }
    }

    private val keystoresDir: File
        get() = File(context.filesDir, "keystores").apply { mkdirs() }

    fun createNewKeystore(
        alias: String,
        password: String,
        commonName: String,
        organization: String,
        country: String,
        validityYears: Int = 25,
    ): CreatedKeystoreInfo {
        val cleanAlias = alias.trim().ifBlank { "webino_key" }
        val keyGen = KeyPairGenerator.getInstance("RSA").apply {
            initialize(2048, SecureRandom())
        }
        val keyPair = keyGen.generateKeyPair()

        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
        val notBefore = Date(now - 60_000L)
        val notAfter = Date(now + validityYears.toLong() * 365L * 24L * 3600L * 1000L)
        val dn = X500Name("CN=${sanitizeDn(commonName)}, O=${sanitizeDn(organization)}, C=${sanitizeDn(country)}")

        val certBuilder = JcaX509v3CertificateBuilder(
            dn,
            BigInteger(64, SecureRandom()).abs(),
            notBefore,
            notAfter,
            dn,
            keyPair.public,
        )
        val contentSigner = JcaContentSignerBuilder("SHA256withRSA").build(keyPair.private)
        val certHolder = certBuilder.build(contentSigner)
        val cert = JcaX509CertificateConverter().getCertificate(certHolder)

        val safeName = cleanAlias.replace(Regex("[^A-Za-z0-9_-]"), "_").take(40).ifBlank { "key" }
        val ksFile = File(keystoresDir, "${safeName}_${System.currentTimeMillis()}.p12")
        val ks = KeyStore.getInstance("PKCS12").apply {
            load(null, null)
            setKeyEntry(cleanAlias, keyPair.private, password.toCharArray(), arrayOf(cert))
        }
        ksFile.outputStream().use { fos ->
            ks.store(fos, password.toCharArray())
        }

        return CreatedKeystoreInfo(
            alias = cleanAlias,
            keystorePath = ksFile.absolutePath,
            keystoreType = "PKCS12",
            commonName = commonName,
            organization = organization,
            country = country,
            validityYears = validityYears,
            sha256Fingerprint = OutputValidator.sha256Fingerprint(cert),
            sha1Fingerprint = sha1Fingerprint(cert),
            encryptedPasswordToken = protectSecret(password),
        )
    }

    fun importKeystoreBytes(
        rawBytes: ByteArray,
        preferredFileName: String,
        password: String,
        aliasHint: String? = null,
    ): CreatedKeystoreInfo {
        val candidates = if (preferredFileName.endsWith(".bks", ignoreCase = true)) {
            listOf("BKS", "PKCS12")
        } else {
            listOf("PKCS12", "BKS", "JKS")
        }
        var loaded: KeyStore? = null
        var wrongPassword = false
        for (type in candidates) {
            try {
                loaded = KeyStore.getInstance(type).apply {
                    load(rawBytes.inputStream(), password.toCharArray())
                }
                break
            } catch (e: java.io.IOException) {
                // PKCS12/BKS report a bad password as an IOException caused by UnrecoverableKeyException.
                if (e.cause is java.security.UnrecoverableKeyException ||
                    e.message?.contains("password", ignoreCase = true) == true
                ) {
                    wrongPassword = true
                }
            } catch (_: Exception) {
                // This keystore type is unavailable on Android or does not match the file; try the next one.
            }
        }
        val ks = loaded ?: throw IllegalArgumentException(
            if (wrongPassword) {
                "رمز Keystore اشتباه است."
            } else {
                "فرمت Keystore پشتیبانی نمی‌شود. فایل JKS را با keytool به PKCS12 (.p12) تبدیل کنید."
            }
        )

        val aliases = ks.aliases().toList()
        val chosenAlias = aliasHint?.takeIf { ks.containsAlias(it) }
            ?: aliases.firstOrNull { ks.isKeyEntry(it) }
            ?: throw IllegalArgumentException("هیچ کلید امضای معتبری در فایل Keystore یافت نشد.")

        val key = ks.getKey(chosenAlias, password.toCharArray()) as? PrivateKey
            ?: throw IllegalArgumentException("امکان استخراج کلید خصوصی با رمز واردشده وجود ندارد.")
        val chain = ks.getCertificateChain(chosenAlias)?.filterIsInstance<X509Certificate>()
            ?: listOfNotNull(ks.getCertificate(chosenAlias) as? X509Certificate)
        if (chain.isEmpty()) {
            throw IllegalArgumentException("گواهی X.509 در فایل یافت نشد.")
        }

        val destFile = File(keystoresDir, "imported_${System.currentTimeMillis()}.p12")
        val normalizedKs = KeyStore.getInstance("PKCS12").apply {
            load(null, null)
            setKeyEntry(chosenAlias, key, password.toCharArray(), chain.toTypedArray())
        }
        destFile.outputStream().use { normalizedKs.store(it, password.toCharArray()) }

        val cert = chain.first()
        return CreatedKeystoreInfo(
            alias = chosenAlias,
            keystorePath = destFile.absolutePath,
            keystoreType = "PKCS12",
            commonName = cert.subjectX500Principal.name,
            organization = "Imported",
            country = "IR",
            validityYears = 25,
            sha256Fingerprint = OutputValidator.sha256Fingerprint(cert),
            sha1Fingerprint = sha1Fingerprint(cert),
            encryptedPasswordToken = protectSecret(password),
        )
    }

    fun loadSigningMaterial(
        keystorePath: String,
        alias: String,
        encryptedPasswordToken: String,
    ): LoadedSigningMaterial {
        val password = unprotectSecret(encryptedPasswordToken)
        return loadSigningMaterialWithPassword(keystorePath, alias, password)
    }

    fun loadSigningMaterialWithPassword(
        keystorePath: String,
        alias: String,
        password: String,
    ): LoadedSigningMaterial {
        val file = File(keystorePath)
        require(file.exists()) { "فایل Keystore در مسیر ذخیرهشده وجود ندارد." }
        val ks = KeyStore.getInstance("PKCS12")
        file.inputStream().use { ks.load(it, password.toCharArray()) }
        val key = ks.getKey(alias, password.toCharArray()) as? PrivateKey
            ?: throw IllegalStateException("کلید امضا با این رمز باز نشد.")
        val chain = ks.getCertificateChain(alias)?.filterIsInstance<X509Certificate>()
            ?: listOfNotNull(ks.getCertificate(alias) as? X509Certificate)
        require(chain.isNotEmpty()) { "گواهی دیجیتال نامعتبر است." }
        return LoadedSigningMaterial(alias = alias, privateKey = key, certificateChain = chain)
    }

    fun changeKeystorePassword(
        keystorePath: String,
        alias: String,
        oldEncryptedToken: String,
        newPassword: String,
    ): String {
        val material = loadSigningMaterial(keystorePath, alias, oldEncryptedToken)
        val ks = KeyStore.getInstance("PKCS12").apply {
            load(null, null)
            setKeyEntry(
                alias,
                material.privateKey,
                newPassword.toCharArray(),
                material.certificateChain.toTypedArray(),
            )
        }
        // Write to a temp file first and swap atomically, so a crash can never leave a truncated keystore.
        val target = File(keystorePath)
        val tmp = File(target.parentFile, "${target.name}.tmp")
        tmp.outputStream().use { ks.store(it, newPassword.toCharArray()) }
        if (!tmp.renameTo(target)) {
            target.delete()
            check(tmp.renameTo(target)) { "ذخیره‌ی کلید با رمز جدید ناموفق بود." }
        }
        return protectSecret(newPassword)
    }

    fun verifyKeystoreIntegrity(keystorePath: String, alias: String, encryptedToken: String): Boolean =
        runCatching {
            val mat = loadSigningMaterial(keystorePath, alias, encryptedToken)
            mat.certificateChain.first().checkValidity()
            true
        }.getOrDefault(false)

    private fun sha1Fingerprint(cert: X509Certificate): String {
        val digest = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-1").digest(cert.encoded)
        return digest.joinToString(":") { "%02X".format(it) }
    }

    private fun sanitizeDn(value: String): String =
        value.replace(",", " ").replace("=", " ").trim().ifBlank { "WEBINO" }

    private fun protectSecret(plain: String): String {
        return try {
            val secretKey = getOrCreateMasterKey()
            val cipher = Cipher.getInstance("AES/GCM/NoPadding")
            cipher.init(Cipher.ENCRYPT_MODE, secretKey)
            val iv = cipher.iv
            val enc = cipher.doFinal(plain.toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8))
            val combined = ByteArray(1 + iv.size + enc.size)
            combined[0] = iv.size.toByte()
            System.arraycopy(iv, 0, combined, 1, iv.size)
            System.arraycopy(enc, 0, combined, 1 + iv.size, enc.size)
            "AKS:" + Base64.encodeToString(combined, Base64.NO_WRAP)
        } catch (e: Throwable) {
            // The weaker local-seed fallback exists only for JVM unit tests, where the AndroidKeyStore
            // provider is absent. On a real device a keystore failure must surface instead of silently
            // protecting the password with a key stored next to the .p12.
            if (Security.getProvider("AndroidKeyStore") != null) throw e
            val prefsKey = getOrCreateLocalFallbackSecret()
            val cipher = Cipher.getInstance("AES/GCM/NoPadding")
            cipher.init(Cipher.ENCRYPT_MODE, prefsKey)
            val iv = cipher.iv
            val enc = cipher.doFinal(plain.toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8))
            val combined = ByteArray(1 + iv.size + enc.size)
            combined[0] = iv.size.toByte()
            System.arraycopy(iv, 0, combined, 1, iv.size)
            System.arraycopy(enc, 0, combined, 1 + iv.size, enc.size)
            "LOC:" + Base64.encodeToString(combined, Base64.NO_WRAP)
        }
    }

    private fun unprotectSecret(token: String): String {
        return if (token.startsWith("AKS:")) {
            val bytes = Base64.decode(token.removePrefix("AKS:"), Base64.NO_WRAP)
            val ivLen = bytes[0].toInt() and 0xFF
            val iv = bytes.copyOfRange(1, 1 + ivLen)
            val enc = bytes.copyOfRange(1 + ivLen, bytes.size)
            val cipher = Cipher.getInstance("AES/GCM/NoPadding")
            cipher.init(Cipher.DECRYPT_MODE, getOrCreateMasterKey(), GCMParameterSpec(128, iv))
            String(cipher.doFinal(enc), Charsets.UTF_8)
        } else {
            val bytes = Base64.decode(token.removePrefix("LOC:"), Base64.NO_WRAP)
            val ivLen = bytes[0].toInt() and 0xFF
            val iv = bytes.copyOfRange(1, 1 + ivLen)
            val enc = bytes.copyOfRange(1 + ivLen, bytes.size)
            val cipher = Cipher.getInstance("AES/GCM/NoPadding")
            cipher.init(Cipher.DECRYPT_MODE, getOrCreateLocalFallbackSecret(), GCMParameterSpec(128, iv))
            String(cipher.doFinal(enc), Charsets.UTF_8)
        }
    }

    private fun getOrCreateMasterKey(): SecretKey = synchronized(vaultLock) {
        val ks = KeyStore.getInstance("AndroidKeyStore").apply { load(null) }
        val alias = "webino_vault_master_key"
        val existing = ks.getKey(alias, null) as? SecretKey
        if (existing != null) return existing

        val kg = KeyGenerator.getInstance(KeyProperties.KEY_ALGORITHM_AES, "AndroidKeyStore")
        val spec = KeyGenParameterSpec.Builder(
            alias,
            KeyProperties.PURPOSE_ENCRYPT or KeyProperties.PURPOSE_DECRYPT,
        )
            .setBlockModes(KeyProperties.BLOCK_MODE_GCM)
            .setEncryptionPaddings(KeyProperties.ENCRYPTION_PADDING_NONE)
            .setKeySize(256)
            .build()
        kg.init(spec)
        return kg.generateKey()
    }

    private fun getOrCreateLocalFallbackSecret(): SecretKey = synchronized(vaultLock) {
        val file = File(keystoresDir, ".vault_seed")
        val raw = if (file.exists() && file.length() == 32L) {
            file.readBytes()
        } else {
            ByteArray(32).also { seed ->
                SecureRandom().nextBytes(seed)
                val tmp = File(keystoresDir, ".vault_seed.tmp.${System.nanoTime()}")
                tmp.writeBytes(seed)
                if (!file.exists() || file.length() != 32L) {
                    tmp.renameTo(file)
                } else {
                    tmp.delete()
                }
            }
            if (file.exists() && file.length() == 32L) file.readBytes() else ByteArray(32)
        }
        return javax.crypto.spec.SecretKeySpec(raw, "AES")
    }

    companion object {
        private val vaultLock = Any()
    }
}
package com.example.data.local.db

import androidx.room.Dao
import androidx.room.Insert
import androidx.room.OnConflictStrategy
import androidx.room.Query
import androidx.room.Transaction
import androidx.room.Update
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow

@Dao
interface ProjectDao {
    @Transaction
    @Query("SELECT * FROM projects ORDER BY updatedAt DESC")
    fun observeAllProjects(): Flow<List<FullProjectBundle>>

    @Transaction
    @Query("SELECT * FROM projects WHERE id = :projectId")
    fun observeProject(projectId: Long): Flow<FullProjectBundle?>

    @Transaction
    @Query("SELECT * FROM projects WHERE id = :projectId")
    suspend fun getProjectById(projectId: Long): FullProjectBundle?

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertProject(project: ProjectEntity): Long

    @Update
    suspend fun updateProject(project: ProjectEntity)

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun upsertProjectSettings(settings: ProjectSettingsEntity)

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun upsertWebSettings(webSettings: WebSettingsEntity)

    @Query("DELETE FROM projects WHERE id = :projectId")
    suspend fun deleteProjectById(projectId: Long)

    @Transaction
    @Query(
        "SELECT * FROM projects WHERE name LIKE '%' || :query || '%' " +
            "OR websiteUrl LIKE '%' || :query || '%' " +
            "OR packageName LIKE '%' || :query || '%' ORDER BY updatedAt DESC"
    )
    fun searchProjects(query: String): Flow<List<FullProjectBundle>>

    @Query("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM projects WHERE packageName = :packageName AND id != :excludeProjectId")
    suspend fun countByPackageName(packageName: String, excludeProjectId: Long = 0L): Int

    @Query("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM projects")
    fun observeProjectCount(): Flow<Int>
}

@Dao
interface BuildDao {
    @Query("SELECT * FROM build_tasks ORDER BY startedAt DESC LIMIT 1")
    fun observeLatestBuildTask(): Flow<BuildTaskEntity?>

    @Query("SELECT * FROM build_tasks ORDER BY startedAt DESC LIMIT 10")
    fun observeRecentBuildTasks(): Flow<List<BuildTaskEntity>>

    @Query("SELECT * FROM build_tasks ORDER BY startedAt DESC LIMIT 200")
    fun observeAllBuildTasks(): Flow<List<BuildTaskEntity>>

    @Query("SELECT * FROM build_logs WHERE taskId = :taskId ORDER BY id ASC")
    suspend fun getBuildLogs(taskId: Long): List<BuildLogEntity>

    @Query("SELECT * FROM build_tasks WHERE id = :taskId")
    fun observeBuildTask(taskId: Long): Flow<BuildTaskEntity?>

    @Query("SELECT * FROM build_tasks WHERE id = :taskId")
    suspend fun getBuildTask(taskId: Long): BuildTaskEntity?

    @Query("SELECT * FROM build_tasks WHERE status IN ('QUEUED', 'RUNNING')")
    suspend fun getActiveBuildTasks(): List<BuildTaskEntity>

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertBuildTask(task: BuildTaskEntity): Long

    @Update
    suspend fun updateBuildTask(task: BuildTaskEntity)

    @Query("SELECT * FROM build_logs WHERE taskId = :taskId ORDER BY id ASC")
    fun observeBuildLogs(taskId: Long): Flow<List<BuildLogEntity>>

    @Insert
    suspend fun insertBuildLog(log: BuildLogEntity)

    @Insert
    suspend fun insertBuildLogs(logs: List<BuildLogEntity>)

    @Query("SELECT * FROM build_results ORDER BY createdAt DESC")
    fun observeAllBuildResults(): Flow<List<BuildResultEntity>>

    @Query("SELECT * FROM build_results WHERE id = :resultId")
    suspend fun getBuildResultById(resultId: Long): BuildResultEntity?

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertBuildResult(result: BuildResultEntity): Long

    @Update
    suspend fun updateBuildResult(result: BuildResultEntity)

    @Query("DELETE FROM build_results WHERE id = :resultId")
    suspend fun deleteBuildResultById(resultId: Long)
}

@Dao
interface SigningDao {
    @Query("SELECT * FROM signing_keys ORDER BY isDefault DESC, createdAt DESC")
    fun observeSigningKeys(): Flow<List<SigningKeyMetadataEntity>>

    @Query("SELECT * FROM signing_keys ORDER BY isDefault DESC, createdAt DESC")
    suspend fun getAllSigningKeys(): List<SigningKeyMetadataEntity>

    @Query("SELECT * FROM signing_keys WHERE id = :id")
    suspend fun getSigningKeyById(id: Long): SigningKeyMetadataEntity?

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertSigningKey(key: SigningKeyMetadataEntity): Long

    @Update
    suspend fun updateSigningKey(key: SigningKeyMetadataEntity)

    @Query("UPDATE signing_keys SET isDefault = 0")
    suspend fun clearDefaultKeys()

    @Query("UPDATE signing_keys SET isDefault = 1 WHERE id = :id")
    suspend fun markAsDefault(id: Long)

    @Query("DELETE FROM signing_keys WHERE id = :id")
    suspend fun deleteSigningKey(id: Long)
}

@Dao
interface TemplateAndHistoryDao {
    @Query("SELECT * FROM app_templates")
    fun observeTemplates(): Flow<List<AppTemplateEntity>>

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertTemplates(templates: List<AppTemplateEntity>)

    @Query("SELECT * FROM recent_websites ORDER BY inspectedAt DESC LIMIT 12")
    fun observeRecentWebsites(): Flow<List<RecentWebsiteEntity>>

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun upsertRecentWebsite(website: RecentWebsiteEntity)
}
package com.example.data.local.db

import android.content.Context
import androidx.room.Database
import androidx.room.Room
import androidx.room.RoomDatabase
import androidx.room.migration.Migration
import androidx.sqlite.db.SupportSQLiteDatabase
import com.example.domain.model.AppTemplateType

@Database(
    entities = [
        ProjectEntity::class,
        ProjectSettingsEntity::class,
        WebSettingsEntity::class,
        BuildTaskEntity::class,
        BuildResultEntity::class,
        SigningKeyMetadataEntity::class,
        BuildLogEntity::class,
        AppTemplateEntity::class,
        RecentWebsiteEntity::class,
    ],
    version = 2,
    exportSchema = false,
)
abstract class WebinoDatabase : RoomDatabase() {
    abstract fun projectDao(): ProjectDao
    abstract fun buildDao(): BuildDao
    abstract fun signingDao(): SigningDao
    abstract fun templateAndHistoryDao(): TemplateAndHistoryDao

    companion object {
        @Volatile
        private var INSTANCE: WebinoDatabase? = null

        val MIGRATION_1_2 = object : Migration(1, 2) {
            override fun migrate(db: SupportSQLiteDatabase) {
                db.execSQL("ALTER TABLE project_settings ADD COLUMN showStatusBar INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1")
                db.execSQL("ALTER TABLE project_settings ADD COLUMN showNavigationBar INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1")
                db.execSQL("ALTER TABLE project_settings ADD COLUMN backBehavior TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'HISTORY_THEN_EXIT'")
                db.execSQL("ALTER TABLE web_settings ADD COLUMN webSqlEnabled INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0")
                db.execSQL("ALTER TABLE web_settings ADD COLUMN clipboardAccessEnabled INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1")
                db.execSQL("ALTER TABLE web_settings ADD COLUMN popupBehavior TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'SAME_PAGE'")
            }
        }

        fun getInstance(context: Context): WebinoDatabase =
            INSTANCE ?: synchronized(this) {
                INSTANCE ?: Room.databaseBuilder(
                    context.applicationContext,
                    WebinoDatabase::class.java,
                    "webino_studio.db",
                )
                    .addMigrations(MIGRATION_1_2)
                    .build()
                    .also { INSTANCE = it }
            }

        fun defaultTemplates(): List<AppTemplateEntity> = listOf(
            AppTemplateEntity(
                id = AppTemplateType.STORE.id,
                titleFa = AppTemplateType.STORE.titleFa,
                titleEn = AppTemplateType.STORE.titleEn,

                primaryHex = AppTemplateType.STORE.primaryHex,
                backgroundHex = AppTemplateType.STORE.backgroundHex,
                recommendedToolbar = "BOTTOM_BAR",
                recommendedCache = "DEFAULT",
                recommendedJs = true,
                recommendedDom = true,
                recommendedPaymentBridge = true,
            ),
            AppTemplateEntity(
                id = AppTemplateType.CORPORATE.id,
                titleFa = AppTemplateType.CORPORATE.titleFa,
                titleEn = AppTemplateType.CORPORATE.titleEn,
                descriptionFa = AppTemplateType.CORPORATE.subtitleFa,
                primaryHex = AppTemplateType.CORPORATE.primaryHex,
                backgroundHex = AppTemplateType.CORPORATE.backgroundHex,
                recommendedToolbar = "TOP_BAR",
                recommendedCache = "DEFAULT",
                recommendedJs = true,
                recommendedDom = true,
                recommendedPaymentBridge = false,
            ),
            AppTemplateEntity(
                id = AppTemplateType.NEWS.id,
                titleFa = AppTemplateType.NEWS.titleFa,
                titleEn = AppTemplateType.NEWS.titleEn,
                descriptionFa = AppTemplateType.NEWS.subtitleFa,
                primaryHex = AppTemplateType.NEWS.primaryHex,
                backgroundHex = AppTemplateType.NEWS.backgroundHex,
                recommendedToolbar = "TOP_BAR",
                recommendedCache = "CACHE_ELSE_NETWORK",
                recommendedJs = true,
                recommendedDom = true,
                recommendedPaymentBridge = false,
            ),
            AppTemplateEntity(
                id = AppTemplateType.EDUCATIONAL.id,
                titleFa = AppTemplateType.EDUCATIONAL.titleFa,
                titleEn = AppTemplateType.EDUCATIONAL.titleEn,
                descriptionFa = AppTemplateType.EDUCATIONAL.subtitleFa,
                primaryHex = AppTemplateType.EDUCATIONAL.primaryHex,
                backgroundHex = AppTemplateType.EDUCATIONAL.backgroundHex,
                recommendedToolbar = "BOTTOM_BAR",
                recommendedCache = "DEFAULT",
                recommendedJs = true,

                recommendedPaymentBridge = true,
            ),
            AppTemplateEntity(

package com.example.data.local.db

import androidx.room.Embedded
import androidx.room.Entity
import androidx.room.ForeignKey
import androidx.room.Index
import androidx.room.PrimaryKey
import androidx.room.Relation

@Entity(tableName = "projects")
data class ProjectEntity(
    @PrimaryKey(autoGenerate = true) val id: Long = 0,
    val name: String,
    val websiteUrl: String,
    val packageName: String,
    val versionName: String = "1.0.0",
    val versionCode: Int = 1,
    val description: String = "",
    val templateId: String = "store",
    val faviconUrl: String? = null,
    val changeHistoryJson: String = "[]",
    val createdAt: Long = System.currentTimeMillis(),
    val updatedAt: Long = System.currentTimeMillis(),
)

@Entity(
    tableName = "project_settings",
    foreignKeys = [
        ForeignKey(
            entity = ProjectEntity::class,
            parentColumns = ["id"],
            childColumns = ["projectId"],
            onDelete = ForeignKey.CASCADE,
        )
    ]
)
data class ProjectSettingsEntity(
    @PrimaryKey val projectId: Long,
    val appName: String,
    val developerName: String = "",
    val iconFilePath: String? = null,
    val splashImagePath: String? = null,
    val primaryColorHex: String = "#7C3AED",
    val backgroundColorHex: String = "#0B1026",
    val themePreset: String = "DARK",
    val fontName: String = "VAZIRMATN",
    val toolbarStyle: String = "TOP_BAR",
    val startPagePath: String = "",
    val loadingAnimation: String = "PROGRESS_BAR",
    val splashEnabled: Boolean = true,
    val splashDurationMs: Int = 1400,
    val orientationMode: String = "AUTO",
    val fullscreenMode: Boolean = false,
    val showStatusBar: Boolean = true,
    val showNavigationBar: Boolean = true,
    val backBehavior: String = "HISTORY_THEN_EXIT",
    val supportRtl: Boolean = true,
    // Advanced features
    val enablePushIntegration: Boolean = false,
    val pushProviderNote: String = "",
    val enableDeepLinks: Boolean = false,
    val deepLinkScheme: String = "webino",
    val enableAppLinks: Boolean = false,
    val appLinkHost: String = "",
    val enableFileSharing: Boolean = true,
    val enablePhoneDialer: Boolean = true,
    val enableEmailIntent: Boolean = true,
    val enableMapsIntent: Boolean = true,
    val enablePaymentGatewayBridge: Boolean = true,
    val externalLinkMode: String = "ASK_OR_ALLOWLIST",
)

@Entity(
    tableName = "web_settings",
    foreignKeys = [
        ForeignKey(
            entity = ProjectEntity::class,
            parentColumns = ["id"],
            childColumns = ["projectId"],
            onDelete = ForeignKey.CASCADE,
        )
    ]
)
data class WebSettingsEntity(
    @PrimaryKey val projectId: Long,
    val javaScriptEnabled: Boolean = true,
    val domStorageEnabled: Boolean = true,
    val webSqlEnabled: Boolean = false,
    val clipboardAccessEnabled: Boolean = true,
    val cookiesEnabled: Boolean = true,
    val thirdPartyCookiesEnabled: Boolean = false,
    val fileUploadEnabled: Boolean = true,
    val downloadManagerEnabled: Boolean = true,
    val cameraPermission: Boolean = false,
    val microphonePermission: Boolean = false,
    val geolocationEnabled: Boolean = false,
    val webRtcEnabled: Boolean = false,
    val fullscreenVideoEnabled: Boolean = true,
    val multipleWindowsEnabled: Boolean = false,
    val popupsEnabled: Boolean = false,
    val popupBehavior: String = "SAME_PAGE",
    val customUserAgent: String = "",
    val cacheMode: String = "DEFAULT",
    val zoomEnabled: Boolean = false,
    val mixedContentMode: String = "NEVER_ALLOW",
    val sslPolicy: String = "STRICT_BLOCK",
    val navigationRulesRaw: String = "",
    val httpsOnly: Boolean = true,
)

@Entity(
    tableName = "build_tasks",
    indices = [Index("projectId")]
)
data class BuildTaskEntity(
    @PrimaryKey(autoGenerate = true) val id: Long = 0,
    val projectId: Long,
    val outputFormat: String = "APK",
    val signingKeyId: Long = 0L,
    val status: String = "QUEUED",
    val currentStep: String = "CHECKING_ENV",
    val progressPercent: Int = 0,
    val startedAt: Long = System.currentTimeMillis(),
    val finishedAt: Long? = null,
    val errorMessage: String? = null,
    val workRequestId: String? = null,
)

@Entity(
    tableName = "build_results",
    indices = [Index("projectId"), Index("taskId")]
)
data class BuildResultEntity(
    @PrimaryKey(autoGenerate = true) val id: Long = 0,
    val taskId: Long,
    val projectId: Long,
    val appName: String,
    val packageName: String,
    val versionName: String,
    val versionCode: Int,
    val format: String,
    val filePath: String,
    val fileSizeBytes: Long,
    val isSigned: Boolean,
    val isVerified: Boolean,
    val certFingerprintSha256: String,
    val validationSummary: String,
    val createdAt: Long = System.currentTimeMillis(),
)

@Entity(tableName = "signing_keys")
data class SigningKeyMetadataEntity(
    @PrimaryKey(autoGenerate = true) val id: Long = 0,
    val alias: String,
    val keystorePath: String,
    val keystoreType: String = "PKCS12",
    val commonName: String,
    val organization: String,
    val country: String = "IR",
    val validityYears: Int = 25,
    val sha256Fingerprint: String,
    val sha1Fingerprint: String,
    val encryptedPasswordToken: String,
    val isDefault: Boolean = false,
    val createdAt: Long = System.currentTimeMillis(),
)

@Entity(
    tableName = "build_logs",
    indices = [Index("taskId")]
)
data class BuildLogEntity(
    @PrimaryKey(autoGenerate = true) val id: Long = 0,
    val taskId: Long,
    val timestamp: Long = System.currentTimeMillis(),
    val level: String = "INFO",
    val stepName: String,
    val message: String,
)

@Entity(tableName = "app_templates")
data class AppTemplateEntity(
    @PrimaryKey val id: String,
    val titleFa: String,
    val titleEn: String,
    val descriptionFa: String,
    val primaryHex: String,
    val backgroundHex: String,
    val recommendedToolbar: String,
    val recommendedCache: String,
    val recommendedJs: Boolean,
    val recommendedDom: Boolean,
    val recommendedPaymentBridge: Boolean,
)

@Entity(tableName = "recent_websites")
data class RecentWebsiteEntity(
    @PrimaryKey val url: String,
    val title: String,
    val description: String = "",
    val faviconUrl: String? = null,
    val themeColorHex: String? = null,
    val isHttps: Boolean = true,
    val inspectedAt: Long = System.currentTimeMillis(),
)

data class FullProjectBundle(
    @Embedded val project: ProjectEntity,
    @Relation(parentColumn = "id", entityColumn = "projectId")
    val settings: ProjectSettingsEntity?,
    @Relation(parentColumn = "id", entityColumn = "projectId")
    val webSettings: WebSettingsEntity?,
)
package com.example.data.local.prefs

import android.content.Context
import androidx.datastore.core.DataStore
import androidx.datastore.preferences.core.Preferences
import androidx.datastore.preferences.core.booleanPreferencesKey
import androidx.datastore.preferences.core.edit
import androidx.datastore.preferences.core.stringPreferencesKey
import androidx.datastore.preferences.preferencesDataStore
import com.example.domain.model.AppFontChoice
import com.example.domain.model.AppLanguage
import com.example.domain.model.AppThemeMode
import com.example.domain.model.CalendarType
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.map

private val Context.webinoDataStore: DataStore<Preferences> by preferencesDataStore(name = "webino_user_prefs")

data class UserPreferences(
    val language: AppLanguage = AppLanguage.FA,
    val themeMode: AppThemeMode = AppThemeMode.LIGHT,
    val fontChoice: AppFontChoice = AppFontChoice.VAZIRMATN,
    val calendarType: CalendarType = CalendarType.JALALI,
    val usePersianDigits: Boolean = true,
    val clearPreviewCacheOnExit: Boolean = false,
    val redactSensitiveLogs: Boolean = true,
    // Defaults to true so the tutorial never flashes before DataStore finishes loading.
    val onboardingCompleted: Boolean = true,
)

class UserPreferencesDataStore(private val context: Context) {

    private object Keys {
        val LANGUAGE = stringPreferencesKey("language")
        val THEME_MODE = stringPreferencesKey("theme_mode")
        val FONT_CHOICE = stringPreferencesKey("font_choice")
        val CALENDAR_TYPE = stringPreferencesKey("calendar_type")
        val PERSIAN_DIGITS = booleanPreferencesKey("persian_digits")
        val CLEAR_CACHE_EXIT = booleanPreferencesKey("clear_cache_exit")
        val REDACT_LOGS = booleanPreferencesKey("redact_logs")
        val ONBOARDING_DONE = booleanPreferencesKey("onboarding_done")
    }

    val preferences: Flow<UserPreferences> = context.webinoDataStore.data.map { prefs ->
        UserPreferences(
            language = prefs[Keys.LANGUAGE]?.let { runCatching { AppLanguage.valueOf(it) }.getOrNull() }
                ?: AppLanguage.FA,
            themeMode = prefs[Keys.THEME_MODE]?.let { runCatching { AppThemeMode.valueOf(it) }.getOrNull() }
                ?: AppThemeMode.LIGHT,
            fontChoice = prefs[Keys.FONT_CHOICE]?.let { runCatching { AppFontChoice.valueOf(it) }.getOrNull() }
                ?: AppFontChoice.VAZIRMATN,
            calendarType = prefs[Keys.CALENDAR_TYPE]?.let { runCatching { CalendarType.valueOf(it) }.getOrNull() }
                ?: CalendarType.JALALI,
            usePersianDigits = prefs[Keys.PERSIAN_DIGITS] ?: true,
            clearPreviewCacheOnExit = prefs[Keys.CLEAR_CACHE_EXIT] ?: false,
            redactSensitiveLogs = prefs[Keys.REDACT_LOGS] ?: true,
            onboardingCompleted = prefs[Keys.ONBOARDING_DONE] ?: false,
        )
    }

    suspend fun updateLanguage(language: AppLanguage) {
        context.webinoDataStore.edit { it[Keys.LANGUAGE] = language.name }
    }

    suspend fun updateThemeMode(mode: AppThemeMode) {
        context.webinoDataStore.edit { it[Keys.THEME_MODE] = mode.name }
    }

    suspend fun updateFontChoice(font: AppFontChoice) {
        context.webinoDataStore.edit { it[Keys.FONT_CHOICE] = font.name }
    }

    suspend fun updateCalendarType(calendar: CalendarType) {
        context.webinoDataStore.edit { it[Keys.CALENDAR_TYPE] = calendar.name }
    }

    suspend fun updatePersianDigits(enabled: Boolean) {
        context.webinoDataStore.edit { it[Keys.PERSIAN_DIGITS] = enabled }
    }

    suspend fun updateClearCacheOnExit(enabled: Boolean) {
        context.webinoDataStore.edit { it[Keys.CLEAR_CACHE_EXIT] = enabled }
    }

    suspend fun updateRedactLogs(enabled: Boolean) {
        context.webinoDataStore.edit { it[Keys.REDACT_LOGS] = enabled }
    }

    suspend fun updateOnboardingCompleted(done: Boolean) {
        context.webinoDataStore.edit { it[Keys.ONBOARDING_DONE] = done }
    }
}
package com.example.data.network

import com.example.core.ValidationUtils
import com.example.domain.model.SiteMetadata
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import okhttp3.OkHttpClient
import okhttp3.Request
import org.jsoup.Jsoup
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit

/**
 * Fetches live website metadata (title, description, favicon, theme-color, HTTPS status)
 * directly from the target URL using OkHttp + Jsoup.
 */
class WebsiteInspector {

    private val client = OkHttpClient.Builder()
        .connectTimeout(12, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
        .readTimeout(12, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
        .followRedirects(true)
        .followSslRedirects(true)
        .build()

    suspend fun inspect(rawUrl: String): SiteMetadata = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val normalized = ValidationUtils.normalizeUrl(rawUrl)
        if (!ValidationUtils.isValidWebUrl(normalized)) {
            return@withContext SiteMetadata(
                requestedUrl = rawUrl,
                finalUrl = normalized,
                title = "",
                description = "",
                faviconUrl = null,
                themeColorHex = null,
                isHttps = false,
                isIranDomain = false,
                httpStatusCode = 0,
                isReachable = false,
                errorMessage = "نشانی وبسایت معتبر نیست.",
            )
        }

        try {
            val request = Request.Builder()
                .url(normalized)
                .header(
                    "User-Agent",
                    "Mozilla/5.0 (Linux; Android 14; Mobile) AppleWebKit/537.36 Chrome/128.0 Mobile Safari/537.36"
                )
                .get()
                .build()

            client.newCall(request).execute().use { response ->
                val finalUrl = response.request.url.toString()
                // Only the <head> matters; cap the read so a huge page cannot exhaust memory.
                val bodyStr = runCatching { response.peekBody(1_500_000L).string() }.getOrDefault("")
                val doc = Jsoup.parse(bodyStr, finalUrl)

                val title = doc.title().trim()
                    .ifBlank { doc.selectFirst("meta[property=og:title]")?.attr("content")?.trim().orEmpty() }
                    .ifBlank { ValidationUtils.extractHost(finalUrl) }

                val description = doc.selectFirst("meta[name=description]")?.attr("content")?.trim()
                    ?.ifBlank { doc.selectFirst("meta[property=og:description]")?.attr("content")?.trim() }
                    .orEmpty()

                val themeColor = doc.selectFirst("meta[name=theme-color]")?.attr("content")?.trim()
                    ?.takeIf { ValidationUtils.isValidHexColor(it) }

                val iconHref = doc.selectFirst("link[rel~=^(apple-touch-icon|icon|shortcut icon)$]")
                    ?.absUrl("href")
                    ?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
                    ?: "${response.request.url.scheme}://${response.request.url.host}/favicon.ico"

                SiteMetadata(
                    requestedUrl = rawUrl,
                    finalUrl = finalUrl,
                    title = title,
                    description = description,
                    faviconUrl = iconHref,
                    themeColorHex = themeColor,
                    isHttps = finalUrl.startsWith("https://", ignoreCase = true),
                    isIranDomain = ValidationUtils.isIranDomain(finalUrl),
                    httpStatusCode = response.code,
                    isReachable = response.isSuccessful,
                    errorMessage = if (response.isSuccessful) null else "کد پاسخ سرور: ${response.code}",
                )
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            SiteMetadata(
                requestedUrl = rawUrl,
                finalUrl = normalized,
                title = ValidationUtils.extractHost(normalized),
                description = "",
                faviconUrl = null,
                themeColorHex = null,
                isHttps = ValidationUtils.isHttpsUrl(normalized),
                isIranDomain = ValidationUtils.isIranDomain(normalized),
                httpStatusCode = 0,
                isReachable = false,
                errorMessage = e.localizedMessage ?: "عدم امکان برقراری ارتباط با وبسایت",
            )
        }
    }
}
