@Composable
fun WebinoAmbientBackground(
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    content: @Composable BoxScope.() -> Unit,
) {
    val tokens = WebinoThemeTokens.colors
    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(
                Brush.verticalGradient(
                    colors = listOf(
                        tokens.canvasTop,
                        tokens.canvasMid,
                        tokens.canvasBottom,
                    )
                )
            )
            .drawBehind {
                drawCircle(
                    brush = Brush.radialGradient(
                        colors = listOf(tokens.ambientVioletOrb, Color.Transparent),
                        center = Offset(size.width * 0.16f, size.height * 0.10f),
                        radius = size.width * 0.65f,
                    ),
                    radius = size.width * 0.65f,
                    center = Offset(size.width * 0.16f, size.height * 0.10f),
                )
                drawCircle(
                    brush = Brush.radialGradient(
                        colors = listOf(tokens.ambientCyanOrb, Color.Transparent),
                        center = Offset(size.width * 0.88f, size.height * 0.24f),
                        radius = size.width * 0.58f,
                    ),
                    radius = size.width * 0.58f,
                    center = Offset(size.width * 0.88f, size.height * 0.24f),
                )
                if (!tokens.isDark) {
                    drawCircle(
                        brush = Brush.linearGradient(
                            colors = listOf(Color(0x66FF9A8B), Color(0x66FF6AA8)),
                            start = Offset(-size.width * 0.2f, -size.width * 0.1f),
                            end = Offset(size.width * 0.3f, size.width * 0.4f),
                        ),
                        radius = size.width * 0.34f,
                        center = Offset(-size.width * 0.04f, size.height * 0.03f),
                    )
                    drawCircle(
                        brush = Brush.linearGradient(
                            colors = listOf(Color(0x55B28BFF), Color(0x556C8CFF)),
                            start = Offset(size.width * 0.7f, size.height * 0.4f),
                            end = Offset(size.width * 1.1f, size.height * 0.6f),
                        ),
                        radius = size.width * 0.30f,
                        center = Offset(size.width * 1.02f, size.height * 0.52f),
                    )
                }
                drawCircle(
                    brush = Brush.radialGradient(
                        colors = listOf(tokens.ambientMintOrb, Color.Transparent),
                        center = Offset(size.width * 0.35f, size.height * 0.84f),
                        radius = size.width * 0.60f,
                    ),
                    radius = size.width * 0.60f,
                    center = Offset(size.width * 0.35f, size.height * 0.84f),
                )
            },
        content = content,
    )
}

@Composable
fun GlassCard(
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    borderColor: Color? = null,
    containerColor: Color? = null,
    onClick: (() -> Unit)? = null,
    contentPadding: PaddingValues = PaddingValues(16.dp),
    content: @Composable ColumnScope.() -> Unit,
) {
    val tokens = WebinoThemeTokens.colors
    val shape = RoundedCornerShape(26.dp)
    val interactionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() }
    val pressed by interactionSource.collectIsPressedAsState()
    val scale by animateFloatAsState(
        targetValue = if (pressed && onClick != null) 0.985f else 1f,
        animationSpec = spring(dampingRatio = Spring.DampingRatioMediumBouncy, stiffness = 500f),
        label = "glassCardScale",
    )

    val resolvedFill = containerColor ?: tokens.glassContainerStrong
    val fillBrush = when {
        containerColor != null -> Brush.linearGradient(listOf(containerColor, containerColor))
        tokens.isDark -> Brush.linearGradient(listOf(tokens.glassContainerStrong, tokens.glassContainer))
        else -> Brush.linearGradient(listOf(Color(0xD1FFFFFF), Color(0x8CFFFFFF)))
    }
    val borderBrush = if (borderColor != null) {
        Brush.linearGradient(listOf(borderColor.copy(alpha = 0.72f), borderColor.copy(alpha = 0.28f)))
    } else {
        Brush.linearGradient(listOf(tokens.glassStrokeTop, tokens.glassStrokeBottom))
    }

    val clickableMod = if (onClick != null) {
        Modifier
            .clip(shape)
            .clickable(
                interactionSource = interactionSource,
                indication = androidx.compose.material3.ripple(),
                onClick = onClick,
            )
    } else {
        Modifier
    }

    Surface(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .scale(scale)
            .shadow(
                elevation = if (tokens.isDark) 4.dp else 14.dp,
                shape = shape,
                ambientColor = tokens.cardShadowTint,
                spotColor = tokens.cardShadowTint,
            )
            .then(clickableMod),
        shape = shape,
        color = Color.Transparent,
        border = BorderStroke(1.2.dp, borderBrush),
        tonalElevation = 0.dp,
    ) {
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .background(fillBrush)
                .padding(contentPadding),
            content = content,
        )
    }
}

@Composable
fun GradientPrimaryButton(
    text: String,
    icon: ImageVector,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    enabled: Boolean = true,
    testTag: String = "gradient_primary_btn",
) {
    val tokens = WebinoThemeTokens.colors
    val interactionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() }
    val pressed by interactionSource.collectIsPressedAsState()
    val scale by animateFloatAsState(
        targetValue = if (pressed && enabled) 0.97f else 1f,
        animationSpec = spring(dampingRatio = Spring.DampingRatioMediumBouncy, stiffness = 520f),
        label = "btnScale",
    )
    val shape = RoundedCornerShape(18.dp)
    val brush = if (enabled) {
        Brush.horizontalGradient(listOf(ElectricViolet, IndigoDeep, SkyBlue))
    } else {
        val disabledColor = if (tokens.isDark) Color(0xFF28345E) else Color(0xFFCBD5E1)
        Brush.horizontalGradient(listOf(disabledColor, disabledColor))
    }
    Button(
        onClick = onClick,
        enabled = enabled,
        interactionSource = interactionSource,
        modifier = modifier
            .height(56.dp) // افزایش ارتفاع دکمه
            .minimumInteractiveComponentSize()
            .scale(scale)
            .shadow(
                elevation = if (enabled) 10.dp else 0.dp,
                shape = shape,
                ambientColor = ElectricViolet.copy(alpha = 0.35f),
                spotColor = SkyBlue.copy(alpha = 0.35f),
            )
            .testTag(testTag),
        contentPadding = PaddingValues(0.dp),
        shape = shape,
        colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(
            containerColor = Color.Transparent,
            disabledContainerColor = Color.Transparent,
            contentColor = Color.White,
        ),
    ) {
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .height(56.dp) // افزایش ارتفاع دکمه
                .background(brush, shape)
                .border(
                    width = 1.dp,
                    color = Color.White.copy(alpha = if (enabled) 0.32f else 0.1f),
                    shape = shape,
                ),
            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
        ) {
            Row(
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
                modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 20.dp),
            ) {
                Icon(imageVector = icon, contentDescription = null, modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp), tint = Color.White)
                Spacer(Modifier.width(10.dp))
                Text(
                    text = text,
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleSmall,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    color = Color.White,
                    maxLines = 1,
                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun WebinoSecondaryButton(
    text: String,
    icon: ImageVector? = null,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    accent: Color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary,
) {
    val tokens = WebinoThemeTokens.colors
    OutlinedButton(
        onClick = onClick,
        modifier = modifier
            .height(56.dp) // افزایش ارتفاع دکمه
            .minimumInteractiveComponentSize(),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
        border = BorderStroke(1.dp, accent.copy(alpha = 0.35f)),
        colors = ButtonDefaults.outlinedButtonColors(
            containerColor = tokens.glassContainerStrong,
            contentColor = accent,
        ),
    ) {
        if (icon != null) {
            Icon(icon, contentDescription = null, modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp))
            Spacer(Modifier.width(6.dp))
        }
        Text(text, style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge, maxLines = 1, overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis)
    }
}

@Composable
fun WebinoScreenHeader(
    title: String,
    subtitle: String? = null,
    iconType: Webino3DIconType? = null,
    onBack: (() -> Unit)? = null,
    backContentDescription: String = "بازگشت",
    actions: @Composable RowScope.() -> Unit = {},
) {
    val tokens = WebinoThemeTokens.colors
    Surface(
        color = tokens.glassContainerStrong,
        border = BorderStroke(1.dp, tokens.navPillBorder.copy(alpha = 0.65f)),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(bottomStart = 24.dp, bottomEnd = 24.dp),
        shadowElevation = if (tokens.isDark) 2.dp else 6.dp,
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
    ) {
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .statusBarsPadding()
                .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
        ) {
            if (onBack != null) {
                Surface(
                    onClick = onBack,
                    shape = CircleShape,
                    color = tokens.subtleSurface,
                    border = BorderStroke(1.dp, tokens.navPillBorder),
                    modifier = Modifier.size(44.dp),
                ) {
                    Box(contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                        Icon(
                            imageVector = Icons.AutoMirrored.Rounded.ArrowBack,
                            contentDescription = backContentDescription,
                            tint = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp),
                        )
                    }
                }
                Spacer(Modifier.width(10.dp))
            }
            if (iconType != null) {
                Webino3DGlassIcon(type = iconType, size = 38.dp)
                Spacer(Modifier.width(10.dp))
            }
            Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                Text(
                    text = title,
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    maxLines = 1,
                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                )
                if (!subtitle.isNullOrBlank()) {
                    Text(
                        text = subtitle,
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                        color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                        maxLines = 1,
                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                    )
                }
            }
            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically, content = actions)
        }
    }
}

data class FloatingNavItemData(
    val label: String,
    val testTag: String,
    val iconType: Webino3DIconType,
    val selectedIcon: ImageVector,
    val unselectedIcon: ImageVector,
    val selected: Boolean,
    val onClick: () -> Unit,
)

@Composable
fun Modifier.softEnter(delayMs: Int = 0): Modifier {
    val progress = remember { Animatable(0f) }
    LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
        delay(delayMs.toLong())
        progress.animateTo(1f, tween(durationMillis = 420, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing))
    }
    return this.graphicsLayer {
        alpha = progress.value
        translationY = (1f - progress.value) * 28.dp.toPx()
    }
}

@Composable
fun WebinoFloatingGlassBottomBar(
    items: List<FloatingNavItemData>,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
) {
    val tokens = WebinoThemeTokens.colors
    val barShape = RoundedCornerShape(38.dp)
    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .navigationBarsPadding()
            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 12.dp),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
    ) {
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .widthIn(max = 540.dp)
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .shadow(
                    elevation = if (tokens.isDark) 14.dp else 22.dp,
                    shape = barShape,
                    ambientColor = GrapePurple.copy(alpha = 0.40f),
                    spotColor = GrapePurple.copy(alpha = 0.40f),
                )
                .clip(barShape)
                .background(
                    if (tokens.isDark) {
                        Brush.linearGradient(listOf(tokens.navPillContainer, tokens.navPillContainer))
                    } else {
                        Brush.linearGradient(listOf(Color(0xD6FFFFFF), Color(0x99FFFFFF)))
                    }
                )
                .border(
                    width = 1.3.dp,
                    brush = Brush.linearGradient(listOf(Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.95f), tokens.navPillBorder)),
                    shape = barShape,
                ),
        ) {
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .height(86.dp) // افزایش ارتفاع نوار پایین
                    .padding(horizontal = 8.dp),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceEvenly,
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            ) {
                items.forEach { item ->
                    val iconSize by animateDpAsState(
                        targetValue = if (item.selected) 46.dp else 30.dp,
                        animationSpec = spring(dampingRatio = 0.65f, stiffness = 380f),
                        label = "navIconSize",
                    )
                    val labelColor by animateColorAsState(
                        targetValue = if (item.selected) GrapePurple else MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                        animationSpec = tween(220),
                        label = "navLabelColor",
                    )
                    Surface(
                        onClick = item.onClick,
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp),
                        color = Color.Transparent,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .weight(1f)
                            .minimumInteractiveComponentSize()
                            .testTag(item.testTag),
                    ) {
                        Column(
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(vertical = 4.dp), // کاهش پدینگ برای جا شدن بهتر
                            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
                        ) {
                            Box(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .size(48.dp)
                                    .then(
                                        if (item.selected) {
                                            Modifier
                                                .shadow(
                                                    elevation = 10.dp,
                                                    shape = CircleShape,
                                                    ambientColor = GrapePurple.copy(alpha = 0.55f),
                                                    spotColor = GrapePurple.copy(alpha = 0.55f),
                                                )
                                                .background(Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.92f), CircleShape)
                                                .border(2.dp, Color.White, CircleShape)
                                        } else {
                                            Modifier
                                        }
                                    ),
                                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
                            ) {
                                Webino3DGlassIcon(
                                    type = item.iconType,
                                    size = iconSize,
                                    elevated = item.selected,
                                    contentDescription = item.label,
                                )
                            }
                            Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp)) // افزایش فاصله برای خوانایی
                            Text(
                                text = item.label,
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                                fontWeight = if (item.selected) FontWeight.ExtraBold else FontWeight.Medium,
                                color = labelColor,
                                maxLines = 1,
                                overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun StatusBadge(
    text: String,
    color: Color,
    icon: ImageVector? = null,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
) {
    val tokens = WebinoThemeTokens.colors
    Surface(
        modifier = modifier,
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(50),
        color = color.copy(alpha = if (tokens.isDark) 0.18f else 0.12f),
        border = BorderStroke(1.dp, color.copy(alpha = if (tokens.isDark) 0.42f else 0.30f)),
    ) {
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 5.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
        ) {
            if (icon != null) {
                Icon(
                    imageVector = icon,
                    contentDescription = null,
                    tint = color,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(14.dp),
                )
                Spacer(Modifier.width(5.dp))
            }
            Text(
                text = text,
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                color = color,
                maxLines = 1,
                overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun SettingToggleRow(
    title: String,
    description: String,
    checked: Boolean,
    onCheckedChange: (Boolean) -> Unit,
    icon: ImageVector? = null,
    testTag: String? = null,
) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .minimumInteractiveComponentSize()
            .padding(vertical = 8.dp)
            .then(if (testTag != null) Modifier.testTag(testTag) else Modifier),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
    ) {
        if (icon != null) {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .size(40.dp)
                    .background(
                        Brush.linearGradient(
                            listOf(ElectricViolet.copy(alpha = 0.16f), SkyBlue.copy(alpha = 0.14f))
                        ),
                        RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                    ),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
            ) {
                Icon(icon, contentDescription = null, tint = ElectricViolet, modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp))
            }
            Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
        }
        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
            Text(
                text = title,
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleSmall,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface,
            )
            if (description.isNotBlank()) {
                Spacer(Modifier.height(2.dp))
                Text(
                    text = description,
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                    color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                )
            }
        }
        Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
        Switch(
            checked = checked,
            onCheckedChange = onCheckedChange,
            colors = SwitchDefaults.colors(
                checkedThumbColor = Color.White,
                checkedTrackColor = ElectricViolet,
                uncheckedThumbColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                uncheckedTrackColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surfaceVariant,
            ),
        )
    }
}

@Composable
fun EmptyStateCard(
    icon: ImageVector,
    title: String,
    subtitle: String,
    actionText: String? = null,
    onAction: (() -> Unit)? = null,
    icon3DType: Webino3DIconType = Webino3DIconType.PROJECTS,
) {
    GlassCard(contentPadding = PaddingValues(28.dp)) {
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
        ) {
            Webino3DGlassIcon(
                type = icon3DType,
                size = 64.dp,
                elevated = true,
            )
            Spacer(Modifier.height(16.dp))
            Text(
                text = title,
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
                color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface,
            )
            Spacer(Modifier.height(6.dp))
            Text(
                text = subtitle,
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
            )
            if (actionText != null && onAction != null) {
                Spacer(Modifier.height(18.dp))
                GradientPrimaryButton(
                    text = actionText,
                    icon = icon,
                    onClick = onAction,
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun SkeletonBox(modifier: Modifier = Modifier) {
    val transition = rememberInfiniteTransition(label = "skeleton")
    val alpha by transition.animateFloat(
        initialValue = 0.25f,
        targetValue = 0.6f,
        animationSpec = infiniteRepeatable(tween(800), RepeatMode.Reverse),
        label = "skeletonAlpha",
    )
    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
            .background(MaterialTheme.colorScheme.outline.copy(alpha = alpha)),
    )
}

@Composable
fun ColorSwatchRow(
    selectedHex: String,
    onSelectHex: (String) -> Unit,
    presets: List<String> = listOf(
        "#7C3AED", "#0EA5E9", "#06B6D4", "#10B981", "#F43F5E", "#F97316", "#EC4899", "#0B1026",
    ),
) {
    val tokens = WebinoThemeTokens.colors
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
    ) {
        presets.forEach { hex ->
            val color = runCatching { Color(android.graphics.Color.parseColor(hex)) }.getOrDefault(ElectricViolet)
            val isSelected = selectedHex.equals(hex, ignoreCase = true)
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .size(36.dp)
                    .shadow(if (isSelected) 6.dp else 1.dp, CircleShape)
                    .clip(CircleShape)
                    .background(color)
                    .border(
                        width = if (isSelected) 3.dp else 1.5.dp,
                        color = if (isSelected) {
                            if (tokens.isDark) Color.White else MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface
                        } else {
                            Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.65f)
                        },
                        shape = CircleShape,
                    )
                    .clickable { onSelectHex(hex) },
            )
        }
    }
}

fun localizeNumber(value: Number, language: AppLanguage, usePersianDigits: Boolean): String =
    JalaliCalendar.localizeDigits(value.toString(), usePersianDigits && language == AppLanguage.FA)

fun localizeNumber(value: String, language: AppLanguage, usePersianDigits: Boolean): String =
    JalaliCalendar.localizeDigits(value, usePersianDigits && language == AppLanguage.FA)

fun statusColorFor(statusName: String): Color = when (statusName) {
    "SUCCEEDED" -> SuccessEmerald
    "FAILED", "CANCELLED" -> ErrorCoral
    "RUNNING", "QUEUED" -> WarningAmber
    else -> CyanAccent
}package com.example.ui.navigation

import kotlinx.serialization.Serializable

sealed interface WebinoRoute {
    @Serializable
    data object Splash : WebinoRoute

    @Serializable
    data object Dashboard : WebinoRoute

    @Serializable
    data object Projects : WebinoRoute

    @Serializable
    data object Outputs : WebinoRoute

    @Serializable
    data object Settings : WebinoRoute

    @Serializable
    data class CreateProject(val presetTemplateId: String? = null) : WebinoRoute

    @Serializable
    data class ProjectStudio(val projectId: Long, val initialTab: Int = 0) : WebinoRoute

    @Serializable
    data class LivePreview(val projectId: Long) : WebinoRoute

    @Serializable
    data class BuildManager(val projectId: Long, val taskId: Long = 0L) : WebinoRoute

    @Serializable
    data object SigningManager : WebinoRoute

    @Serializable
    data object BuildHistory : WebinoRoute

    @Serializable
    data object HelpCenter : WebinoRoute

    @Serializable
    data object About : WebinoRoute
}

@Composable
fun AboutScreen(
    language: AppLanguage,
    usePersianDigits: Boolean,
    onBack: () -> Unit,
    onNotify: (String) -> Unit,
    onContactSupportClick: (() -> Unit)? = null,
    onRateMyketClick: (() -> Unit)? = null,
    onOtherAppsClick: (() -> Unit)? = null,
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val tokens = WebinoThemeTokens.colors
    val isFa = language == AppLanguage.FA

    var showHeaderCard by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var showIntroCard by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var showFeaturesCard by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var showOrgCard by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var showStoreCard by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
        showHeaderCard = true
        delay(55)
        showIntroCard = true
        delay(55)
        showFeaturesCard = true
        delay(55)
        showOrgCard = true
        delay(55)
        showStoreCard = true
    }

    val features = remember(isFa) {
        if (isFa) {
            listOf(
                "تبدیل وب‌سایت به اپلیکیشن اندرویدی",
                "پیش‌نمایش وب‌سایت پیش از ساخت",
                "شخصی‌سازی نام، آیکون و صفحه شروع برنامه",
                "تنظیمات پیشرفته WebView",
                "مدیریت پروژه‌ها و خروجی‌ها",
                "مدیریت نسخه و امضای دیجیتال",
                "پشتیبانی از قالب‌های متنوع",
                "محیط کاملاً فارسی و راست‌چین",
            )
        } else {
            listOf(
                "Convert websites into standalone Android apps",
                "Live interactive website preview before compilation",
                "Customize app name, icon, splash screen and colors",
                "Comprehensive categorized WebView engine settings",
                "Complete project and APK/AAB output management",
                "Versioning and digital keystore signing management",
                "Commercial presets and tailored templates",
                "Native Persian RTL and localized calendar support",
            )
        }
    }

    val noEmailMsg = appString(R.string.about_no_email_app)
    val noStoreMsg = appString(R.string.about_no_store_browser)
    val supportBtnLabel = appString(R.string.about_support_btn)

    Scaffold(
        containerColor = Color.Transparent,
        topBar = {
            WebinoScreenHeader(
                title = appString(R.string.about_screen_title),
                subtitle = appString(R.string.about_brand_display),
                iconType = Webino3DIconType.HELP,
                onBack = onBack,
                backContentDescription = appString(R.string.back_cd),
            )
        },
    ) { padding ->
        LazyColumn(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .padding(padding)
                .testTag("about_list"),
            contentPadding = PaddingValues(start = 16.dp, end = 16.dp, top = 14.dp, bottom = 28.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp),
        ) {
            // 1. Official Brand Hero Card
            item {
                AnimatedVisibility(
                    visible = showHeaderCard,
                    enter = fadeIn(tween(300)) + slideInVertically(tween(300)) { it / 8 },
                ) {
                    GlassCard(
                        borderColor = ElectricViolet.copy(alpha = 0.35f),
                        contentPadding = PaddingValues(20.dp),
                    ) {
                        Column(
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                        ) {
                            Surface(
                                shape = RoundedCornerShape(30.dp),
                                color = tokens.glassContainerStrong,
                                border = BorderStroke(
                                    width = 1.5.dp,
                                    brush = Brush.linearGradient(
                                        listOf(ElectricViolet.copy(alpha = 0.45f), SkyBlue.copy(alpha = 0.45f))
                                    ),
                                ),
                                modifier = Modifier.shadow(
                                    elevation = 16.dp,
                                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(30.dp),
                                    ambientColor = ElectricViolet.copy(alpha = 0.25f),
                                    spotColor = SkyBlue.copy(alpha = 0.25f),
                                ),
                            ) {
                                Image(
                                    painter = painterResource(R.drawable.img_webino_icon),
                                    contentDescription = appString(R.string.about_brand_display),
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .padding(6.dp)
                                        .size(84.dp)
                                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(24.dp)),
                                    contentScale = ContentScale.Crop,
                                )
                            }
                            Spacer(Modifier.height(14.dp))
                            Text(
                                text = appString(R.string.about_brand_display),
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.headlineSmall,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
                            )
                            Spacer(Modifier.height(6.dp))
                            Text(
                                text = appString(R.string.brand_tagline),
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                                color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                                textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
                            )
                            Spacer(Modifier.height(12.dp))
                            Row(
                                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                            ) {
                                val versionText = if (isFa) {
                                    "نسخه ${localizeNumber(BuildConfig.VERSION_NAME, language, usePersianDigits)}"
                                } else {
                                    "v${BuildConfig.VERSION_NAME}"
                                }
                                StatusBadge(
                                    text = versionText,
                                    color = ElectricViolet,
                                    icon = Icons.Rounded.Verified,
                                )
                                CompositionLocalProvider(LocalLayoutDirection provides LayoutDirection.Ltr) {
                                    StatusBadge(
                                        text = BuildConfig.APPLICATION_ID,
                                        color = SkyBlue,
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            // 2. Introduction Card
            item {
                AnimatedVisibility(
                    visible = showIntroCard,
                    enter = fadeIn(tween(320)) + slideInVertically(tween(320)) { it / 8 },
                ) {
                    GlassCard {
                        Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                            Webino3DGlassIcon(type = Webino3DIconType.PREVIEW, size = 38.dp)
                            Spacer(Modifier.width(10.dp))
                            Text(
                                text = appString(R.string.about_intro_title),
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            )
                        }
                        Spacer(Modifier.height(10.dp))
                        Text(
                            text = appString(R.string.about_intro_body),
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                            color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                        )
                    }
                }
            }

            // 3. Verified Core Features List
            item {
                AnimatedVisibility(
                    visible = showFeaturesCard,
                    enter = fadeIn(tween(340)) + slideInVertically(tween(340)) { it / 8 },
                ) {
                    GlassCard(borderColor = CyanAccent.copy(alpha = 0.28f)) {
                        Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                            Webino3DGlassIcon(type = Webino3DIconType.BUILD, size = 38.dp)
                            Spacer(Modifier.width(10.dp))
                            Text(
                                text = appString(R.string.about_features_title),
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            )
                        }
                        Spacer(Modifier.height(10.dp))
                        Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                            features.forEach { feature ->
                                Row(
                                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                                ) {
                                    Box(
                                        modifier = Modifier
                                            .size(26.dp)
                                            .background(SuccessEmerald.copy(alpha = 0.14f), CircleShape),
                                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
                                    ) {
                                        Icon(
                                            imageVector = Icons.Rounded.CheckCircle,
                                            contentDescription = null,
                                            tint = SuccessEmerald,
                                            modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp),
                                        )
                                    }
                                    Spacer(Modifier.width(10.dp))
                                    Text(
                                        text = feature,
                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                                        color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface,
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            // 4. Official Developer, Management & Hidden-Email Support Card
            item {
                AnimatedVisibility(
                    visible = showOrgCard,
                    enter = fadeIn(tween(360)) + slideInVertically(tween(360)) { it / 8 },
                ) {
                    GlassCard(borderColor = SkyBlue.copy(alpha = 0.32f)) {
                        Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                            Webino3DGlassIcon(type = Webino3DIconType.SIGNING, size = 38.dp)
                            Spacer(Modifier.width(10.dp))
                            Text(
                                text = appString(R.string.about_org_title),
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            )
                        }
                        Spacer(Modifier.height(12.dp))
                        InfoBadgeRow(
                            icon = Icons.Rounded.Code,
                            label = appString(R.string.about_developer_label),
                            value = appString(R.string.about_developer_value),
                            accent = ElectricViolet,
                        )
                        Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
                        InfoBadgeRow(
                            icon = Icons.Rounded.Badge,
                            label = appString(R.string.about_manager_label),
                            value = appString(R.string.about_manager_value),
                            accent = SkyBlue,
                        )
                        Spacer(Modifier.height(14.dp))
                        GradientPrimaryButton(
                            text = supportBtnLabel,
                            icon = Icons.Rounded.Email,
                            onClick = {
                                if (onContactSupportClick != null) {
                                    onContactSupportClick()
                                } else {
                                    ExternalIntentHelper.openSupportEmail(
                                        context = context,
                                        chooserTitle = supportBtnLabel,
                                        noEmailClientMessage = noEmailMsg,
                                        onError = onNotify,
                                    )
                                }
                            },
                            testTag = "about_support_email_btn",
                        )
                    }
                }
            }

            // 5. Myket Rating & Other Apps Card
            item {
                AnimatedVisibility(
                    visible = showStoreCard,
                    enter = fadeIn(tween(380)) + slideInVertically(tween(380)) { it / 8 },
                ) {
                    GlassCard(borderColor = MintGreen.copy(alpha = 0.35f)) {
                        Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                            Webino3DGlassIcon(type = Webino3DIconType.OUTPUTS, size = 38.dp)
                            Spacer(Modifier.width(10.dp))
                            Text(
                                text = appString(R.string.about_myket_section_title),
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            )
                        }
                        Spacer(Modifier.height(14.dp))
                        WebinoSecondaryButton(
                            text = appString(R.string.about_myket_review_btn),
                            icon = Icons.Rounded.StarRate,
                            accent = SoftOrange,
                            onClick = {
                                if (onRateMyketClick != null) {
                                    onRateMyketClick()
                                } else {
                                    ExternalIntentHelper.openMyketReview(
                                        context = context,
                                        noTargetMessage = noStoreMsg,
                                        onError = onNotify,
                                    )
                                }
                            },
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .testTag("about_myket_review_btn"),
                        )
                        Spacer(Modifier.height(10.dp))
                        WebinoSecondaryButton(
                            text = appString(R.string.about_myket_dev_btn),
                            icon = Icons.Rounded.Apps,
                            accent = ElectricViolet,
                            onClick = {
                                if (onOtherAppsClick != null) {
                                    onOtherAppsClick()
                                } else {
                                    ExternalIntentHelper.openMyketDeveloperApps(
                                        context = context,
                                        noTargetMessage = noStoreMsg,
                                        onError = onNotify,
                                    )
                                }
                            },
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .testTag("about_myket_dev_btn"),
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun InfoBadgeRow(
    icon: androidx.compose.ui.graphics.vector.ImageVector,
    label: String,
    value: String,
    accent: Color,
) {
    val tokens = WebinoThemeTokens.colors
    Surface(
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(15.dp),
        color = tokens.subtleSurface,
        border = BorderStroke(1.dp, tokens.navPillBorder),
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
    ) {
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
        ) {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .size(34.dp)
                    .background(accent.copy(alpha = 0.14f), RoundedCornerShape(10.dp)),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
            ) {
                Icon(icon, contentDescription = null, tint = accent, modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp))
            }
            Spacer(Modifier.width(10.dp))
            Column {
                Text(
                    text = label,
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                    color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                )
                Text(
                    text = value,
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleSmall,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface,
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun BuildHistoryScreen(
    prefs: UserPreferences,
    tasks: List<BuildTaskEntity>,
    projects: List<FullProjectBundle>,
    observeLogs: (Long) -> Flow<List<BuildLogEntity>>,
    loadReport: suspend (Long) -> String,
    onBack: () -> Unit,
    onNotify: (String) -> Unit,
) {
    val isFa = prefs.language == AppLanguage.FA
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    var expandedId by remember { mutableStateOf<Long?>(null) }
    val nameById = remember(projects) { projects.associate { it.project.id to it.project.name } }

    Scaffold(
        containerColor = Color.Transparent,
        topBar = {
            WebinoScreenHeader(
                title = if (isFa) "تاریخچه‌ی ساخت" else "Build history",
                subtitle = if (isFa) "لاگ هر ساخت را ببینید و برای پشتیبانی بفرستید" else "Review logs and send them to support",
                iconType = Webino3DIconType.HISTORY,
                onBack = onBack,
            )
        },
    ) { padding ->
        if (tasks.isEmpty()) {
            Column(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize().padding(padding).padding(16.dp)) {
                EmptyStateCard(
                    icon = Icons.Rounded.Share,
                    title = if (isFa) "هنوز ساختی انجام نشده" else "No builds yet",
                    subtitle = if (isFa) "بعد از اولین ساخت، لاگ آن اینجا نمایش داده می‌شود." else "Logs of your builds will appear here.",
                    icon3DType = Webino3DIconType.HISTORY,
                )
            }
        } else {
            LazyColumn(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize().padding(padding),
                contentPadding = PaddingValues(start = 16.dp, end = 16.dp, top = 12.dp, bottom = 24.dp),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp),
            ) {
                items(tasks, key = { it.id }) { task ->
                    val expanded = expandedId == task.id
                    HistoryTaskCard(
                        task = task,
                        projectName = nameById[task.projectId] ?: (if (isFa) "پروژه‌ی حذف‌شده" else "Deleted project"),
                        prefs = prefs,
                        expanded = expanded,
                        onToggle = { expandedId = if (expanded) null else task.id },
                        observeLogs = observeLogs,
                        onShare = {
                            scope.launch {
                                val text = loadReport(task.id)
                                if (text.isBlank()) onNotify(if (isFa) "گزارشی برای این ساخت پیدا نشد." else "No report found.")
                                else shareReportFile(context, task.id, text, isFa) { onNotify(it) }
                            }
                        },
                        onCopy = {
                            scope.launch {
                                val text = loadReport(task.id)
                                val cm = context.getSystemService(Context.CLIPBOARD_SERVICE) as ClipboardManager
                                cm.setPrimaryClip(ClipData.newPlainText("webino-build-log", text))
                                onNotify(if (isFa) "لاگ کپی شد." else "Log copied.")
                            }
                        },
                        onEmail = {
                            scope.launch {
                                val text = loadReport(task.id)
                                val intent = ExternalIntentHelper.buildSupportEmailIntent(
                                    "WEBINO build #${task.id} (${task.status})",
                                ).apply {
                                    // mailto: bodies have a size limit; send the head and keep the full log shareable as a file.
                                    putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_TEXT, text.take(8000))
                                }
                                runCatching { context.startActivity(Intent.createChooser(intent, null)) }
                                    .onFailure { onNotify(if (isFa) "برنامه‌ی ایمیل پیدا نشد." else "No email app found.") }
                            }
                        },
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun HistoryTaskCard(
    task: BuildTaskEntity,
    projectName: String,
    prefs: UserPreferences,
    expanded: Boolean,
    onToggle: () -> Unit,
    observeLogs: (Long) -> Flow<List<BuildLogEntity>>,
    onShare: () -> Unit,
    onCopy: () -> Unit,
    onEmail: () -> Unit,
) {
    val isFa = prefs.language == AppLanguage.FA
    val status = runCatching { BuildStatus.valueOf(task.status) }.getOrNull()
    val statusText = if (isFa) (status?.titleFa ?: task.status) else (status?.name ?: task.status)
    val whenText = JalaliCalendar.formatDateTime(
        millis = task.startedAt,
        useJalali = prefs.calendarType == CalendarType.JALALI,
        isPersian = isFa,
        usePersianDigits = prefs.usePersianDigits,
    )
    GlassCard(
        onClick = onToggle,
        borderColor = statusColorFor(task.status).copy(alpha = 0.35f),
        modifier = Modifier.testTag("history_task_${task.id}"),
    ) {
        Row(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
            Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                Text(
                    text = projectName,
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleSmall,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    maxLines = 1,
                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                )
                Text(
                    text = "${task.outputFormat} • $whenText",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                    color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                )
            }
            Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
            StatusBadge(text = statusText, color = statusColorFor(task.status))
            Spacer(Modifier.width(4.dp))
            Icon(
                imageVector = if (expanded) Icons.Rounded.ExpandLess else Icons.Rounded.ExpandMore,
                contentDescription = null,
                tint = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
            )
        }
        if (!task.errorMessage.isNullOrBlank()) {
            Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
            Text(
                text = task.errorMessage,
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                color = ErrorCoral,
                maxLines = if (expanded) Int.MAX_VALUE else 2,
                overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
            )
        }
        AnimatedVisibility(visible = expanded) {
            val logs by remember(task.id) { observeLogs(task.id) }.collectAsState(initial = emptyList())
            Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 12.dp)) {
                Text(
                    text = if (isFa) {
                        "لاگ (${localizeNumber(logs.size, prefs.language, prefs.usePersianDigits)} خط)"
                    } else {
                        "Log (${logs.size} lines)"
                    },
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                )
                Spacer(Modifier.height(6.dp))
                GlassCard(contentPadding = PaddingValues(10.dp)) {
                    if (logs.isEmpty()) {
                        Text(
                            text = if (isFa) "لاگی ثبت نشده است." else "No log lines.",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                            color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                        )
                    } else {
                        // Show the last lines inline; the shared report always contains the full log.
                        logs.takeLast(40).forEach { l ->
                            Text(
                                text = "[${l.level}] ${l.message}",
                                fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace,
                                fontSize = 11.sp,
                                color = when (l.level) {
                                    "ERROR" -> ErrorCoral
                                    "WARN" -> WarningAmber
                                    else -> MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface
                                },
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
                Spacer(Modifier.height(10.dp))
                Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp), modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
                    WebinoSecondaryButton(
                        text = if (isFa) "اشتراک‌گذاری" else "Share",
                        icon = Icons.Rounded.Share,
                        onClick = onShare,
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f).testTag("history_share_${task.id}"),
                        accent = CyanAccent,
                    )
                    WebinoSecondaryButton(
                        text = if (isFa) "کپی" else "Copy",
                        icon = Icons.Rounded.ContentCopy,
                        onClick = onCopy,
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                    )
                    WebinoSecondaryButton(
                        text = if (isFa) "پشتیبانی" else "Support",
                        icon = Icons.Rounded.Email,
                        onClick = onEmail,
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

private fun shareReportFile(
    context: Context,
    taskId: Long,
    text: String,
    isFa: Boolean,
    notify: (String) -> Unit,
) {
    runCatching {
        val dir = File(context.cacheDir, "shared_logs").apply { mkdirs() }
        val file = File(dir, "webino-build-$taskId.txt")
        file.writeText(text, Charsets.UTF_8)
        val uri = FileProvider.getUriForFile(context, "${context.packageName}.fileprovider", file)
        val send = Intent(Intent.ACTION_SEND).apply {
            type = "text/plain"
            putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_STREAM, uri)
            putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_SUBJECT, "WEBINO build #$taskId")
            clipData = ClipData.newRawUri("webino-build-log", uri)
            addFlags(Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION)
        }
        context.startActivity(Intent.createChooser(send, if (isFa) "ارسال لاگ ساخت" else "Share build log"))
    }.onFailure {
        notify(if (isFa) "اشتراک‌گذاری لاگ ممکن نشد." else "Could not share the log.")
    }
}

import androidx.compose.foundation.BorderStroke
