@Composable
fun LivePreviewScreen(
    bundle: FullProjectBundle?,
    clearCacheOnExit: Boolean = false,
    onBack: () -> Unit,
) {
    if (bundle == null) return
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val lifecycleOwner = LocalLifecycleOwner.current
    val tokens = WebinoThemeTokens.colors
    
    // باگ بحرانی رفع شد: استفاده از remember به جای rememberSaveable برای جلوگیری از کرش در چرخش صفحه
    val savedWebBundle = remember(bundle.project.id) { Bundle() }

    var previewState by remember { mutableStateOf(WebPreviewState(currentUrl = bundle.project.websiteUrl)) }
    var isFullscreen by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var isLandscapeFrame by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var customVideoView by remember { mutableStateOf<View?>(null) }
    var popupWebView by remember { mutableStateOf<WebView?>(null) }
    var showDownloadsDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var pendingPermResult by remember { mutableStateOf<((Boolean) -> Unit)?>(null) }

    val webCfg = remember(bundle) {
        val w = bundle.webSettings
        val s = bundle.settings
        val startUrl = buildString {
            append(bundle.project.websiteUrl.trimEnd('/'))
            val path = s?.startPagePath.orEmpty().trim()
            if (path.isNotEmpty()) {
                if (!path.startsWith("/")) append("/")
                append(path)
            }
        }
        WebViewEngineConfig(
            initialUrl = startUrl,
            navigationRulesRaw = w?.navigationRulesRaw.orEmpty(),
            javaScriptEnabled = w?.javaScriptEnabled ?: true,
            domStorageEnabled = w?.domStorageEnabled ?: true,
            webSqlEnabled = w?.webSqlEnabled ?: false,
            clipboardAccessEnabled = w?.clipboardAccessEnabled ?: true,
            cookiesEnabled = w?.cookiesEnabled ?: true,
            thirdPartyCookiesEnabled = w?.thirdPartyCookiesEnabled ?: false,
            fileUploadEnabled = w?.fileUploadEnabled ?: true,
            downloadManagerEnabled = w?.downloadManagerEnabled ?: true,
            cameraAllowed = w?.cameraPermission ?: false,
            microphoneAllowed = w?.microphonePermission ?: false,
            geolocationAllowed = w?.geolocationEnabled ?: false,
            webRtcAllowed = w?.webRtcEnabled ?: false,
            fullscreenVideoAllowed = w?.fullscreenVideoEnabled ?: true,
            multipleWindowsAllowed = w?.multipleWindowsEnabled ?: false,
            popupsAllowed = w?.popupsEnabled ?: false,
            popupBehavior = runCatching { PopupBehaviorMode.valueOf(w?.popupBehavior ?: "") }
                .getOrDefault(PopupBehaviorMode.SAME_PAGE),
            backBehavior = runCatching { BackNavigationBehavior.valueOf(s?.backBehavior ?: "") }
                .getOrDefault(BackNavigationBehavior.HISTORY_THEN_EXIT),
            customUserAgent = w?.customUserAgent.orEmpty(),
            cacheMode = runCatching { CacheModeOption.valueOf(w?.cacheMode ?: "") }.getOrDefault(CacheModeOption.DEFAULT),
            zoomEnabled = w?.zoomEnabled ?: false,
            mixedContentMode = runCatching { MixedContentOption.valueOf(w?.mixedContentMode ?: "") }
                .getOrDefault(MixedContentOption.NEVER_ALLOW),
            sslPolicy = runCatching { SslPolicy.valueOf(w?.sslPolicy ?: "") }.getOrDefault(SslPolicy.STRICT_BLOCK),
            httpsOnly = w?.httpsOnly ?: true,
            externalLinkMode = runCatching { ExternalLinkMode.valueOf(s?.externalLinkMode ?: "") }
                .getOrDefault(ExternalLinkMode.ASK_OR_ALLOWLIST),
            enableDeepLinks = s?.enableDeepLinks ?: false,
            deepLinkScheme = s?.deepLinkScheme ?: "webino",
            enableAppLinks = s?.enableAppLinks ?: false,
            appLinkHost = s?.appLinkHost.orEmpty(),
        )
    }

    val permLauncher = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(ActivityResultContracts.RequestMultiplePermissions()) { results ->
        val granted = results.values.any { it }
        pendingPermResult?.invoke(granted)
        pendingPermResult = null
    }

    val wiredController = remember {
        WebViewManager(
            context = context,
            onStateChange = { previewState = it },
            onOsPermissionPrompt = { perms, resultCb ->
                pendingPermResult = resultCb
                permLauncher.launch(perms.toTypedArray())
            },
            onShowCustomVideoView = { v -> customVideoView = v },
            onShowPopupWebView = { p -> popupWebView = p },
        )
    }

    val fileLauncher = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(ActivityResultContracts.StartActivityForResult()) { res ->
        wiredController.completeUploadResult(res.resultCode, res.data)
    }

    val cameraLauncher = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(ActivityResultContracts.TakePicture()) { ok ->
        wiredController.completeCameraCapture(ok)
    }

    val activeUploadOptions = previewState.pendingUploadOptions
    val offerCameraChoice = remember(activeUploadOptions, webCfg.cameraAllowed) {
        activeUploadOptions != null && (
            activeUploadOptions.captureEnabled ||
                (webCfg.cameraAllowed && activeUploadOptions.acceptMimeTypes.any {
                    it.startsWith("image", ignoreCase = true) || it == "*/*" || it.equals(".jpg", true) || it.equals(".png", true)
                })
            )
    }

    LaunchedEffect(activeUploadOptions, offerCameraChoice) {
        val opts = activeUploadOptions ?: return@LaunchedEffect
        if (!offerCameraChoice) {
            runCatching { fileLauncher.launch(opts.chooserIntent) }.onFailure {
                wiredController.cancelUpload()
            }
        }
    }

    LaunchedEffect(previewState.activeDownloads) {
        if (previewState.activeDownloads.any { it.status == DownloadItemStatus.RUNNING || it.status == DownloadItemStatus.PENDING }) {
            while (true) {
                delay(1200)
                wiredController.refreshDownloads()
                val stillActive = wiredController.getCurrentState().activeDownloads.any {
                    it.status == DownloadItemStatus.RUNNING || it.status == DownloadItemStatus.PENDING
                }
                if (!stillActive) break
            }
        }
    }

    DisposableEffect(lifecycleOwner, clearCacheOnExit, wiredController) {
        val observer = LifecycleEventObserver { _, event ->
            when (event) {
                Lifecycle.Event.ON_RESUME -> wiredController.onResume()
                Lifecycle.Event.ON_PAUSE -> {
                    wiredController.saveWebViewState(savedWebBundle)
                    wiredController.onPause()
                }
                else -> Unit
            }
        }
        lifecycleOwner.lifecycle.addObserver(observer)
        onDispose {
            lifecycleOwner.lifecycle.removeObserver(observer)
            wiredController.saveWebViewState(savedWebBundle)
            if (clearCacheOnExit) wiredController.clearSessionCache()
            wiredController.destroy()
        }
    }

    BackHandler {
        wiredController.handleBackPress(onExitScreen = onBack)
    }

    Scaffold(
        containerColor = Color.Transparent,
        topBar = {
            if (!isFullscreen) {
                WebinoScreenHeader(
                    title = appString(R.string.preview_title),
                    subtitle = bundle.settings?.appName ?: bundle.project.name,
                    iconType = Webino3DIconType.PREVIEW,
                    onBack = onBack,
                    backContentDescription = appString(R.string.back_cd),
                    actions = {
                        if (previewState.activeDownloads.isNotEmpty()) {
                            IconButton(
                                onClick = {
                                    wiredController.refreshDownloads()
                                    showDownloadsDialog = true
                                },
                                modifier = Modifier.testTag("preview_downloads_btn"),
                            ) {
                                BadgedBox(
                                    badge = {
                                        Badge {
                                            Text(previewState.activeDownloads.size.toString())
                                        }
                                    }
                                ) {
                                    Icon(Icons.Rounded.Download, contentDescription = "لیست دانلودها", tint = SkyBlue)
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        IconButton(onClick = { isLandscapeFrame = !isLandscapeFrame }) {
                            Icon(Icons.Rounded.ScreenRotation, contentDescription = "چرخش")
                        }
                        IconButton(onClick = { isFullscreen = true }) {
                            Icon(Icons.Rounded.Fullscreen, contentDescription = "تمام‌صفحه")
                        }
                    },
                )
            }
        }
    ) { padding ->
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .padding(padding)
                .padding(if (isFullscreen) 0.dp else 12.dp),
            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
        ) {
            if (!isFullscreen) {
                GlassCard(
                    contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 6.dp),
                ) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                    ) {
                        Row(
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                        ) {
                            Box(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .size(28.dp)
                                    .background(
                                        when {
                                            !previewState.isOnline -> ErrorCoral.copy(alpha = 0.14f)
                                            previewState.currentUrl.startsWith("https") -> SuccessEmerald.copy(alpha = 0.14f)
                                            else -> WarningAmber.copy(alpha = 0.14f)
                                        },
                                        CircleShape,
                                    ),
                                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
                            ) {
                                Icon(
                                    if (previewState.isOnline) Icons.Rounded.Https else Icons.Rounded.WifiOff,
                                    contentDescription = null,
                                    tint = when {
                                        !previewState.isOnline -> ErrorCoral
                                        previewState.currentUrl.startsWith("https") -> SuccessEmerald
                                        else -> WarningAmber
                                    },
                                    modifier = Modifier.size(15.dp),
                                )
                            }
                            Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
                            Text(
                                text = previewState.currentUrl,
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall.copy(
                                    fontFamily = JetBrainsMonoFamily,
                                    textDirection = TextDirection.Ltr,
                                ),
                                maxLines = 1,
                                overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                            )
                        }
                        Row {
                            IconButton(
                                onClick = { wiredController.goBack() },
                                enabled = previewState.canGoBack,
                                modifier = Modifier.testTag("preview_back_btn"),
                            ) {
                                Icon(Icons.AutoMirrored.Rounded.ArrowBack, contentDescription = appString(R.string.preview_back_cd))
                            }
                            IconButton(
                                onClick = { wiredController.goForward() },
                                enabled = previewState.canGoForward,
                                modifier = Modifier.testTag("preview_forward_btn"),
                            ) {
                                Icon(Icons.AutoMirrored.Rounded.ArrowForward, contentDescription = appString(R.string.preview_forward_cd))
                            }
                            IconButton(
                                onClick = { wiredController.reload() },
                                modifier = Modifier.testTag("preview_refresh_btn"),
                            ) {
                                Icon(Icons.Rounded.Refresh, contentDescription = appString(R.string.preview_refresh_cd), tint = ElectricViolet)
                            }
                            IconButton(
                                onClick = { wiredController.openCurrentInExternalBrowser() },
                                modifier = Modifier.testTag("preview_external_btn"),
                            ) {
                                Icon(Icons.Rounded.OpenInBrowser, contentDescription = appString(R.string.preview_open_external_cd), tint = SkyBlue)
                            }
                            IconButton(
                                onClick = { wiredController.requestClearSiteDataConfirmation() },
                                modifier = Modifier.testTag("preview_clear_cache_btn"),
                            ) {
                                Icon(Icons.Rounded.CleaningServices, contentDescription = appString(R.string.preview_clear_cache_cd))
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                Spacer(Modifier.height(10.dp))
            }

            val frameModifier = if (isFullscreen) {
                Modifier.fillMaxSize()
            } else if (isLandscapeFrame) {
                Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .aspectRatio(16f / 10f)
            } else {
                Modifier
                    .fillMaxHeight()
                    .aspectRatio(9f / 17f)
            }

            val frameShape = RoundedCornerShape(if (isFullscreen) 0.dp else 28.dp)
            Box(
                modifier = frameModifier
                    .shadow(
                        elevation = if (isFullscreen) 0.dp else 14.dp,
                        shape = frameShape,
                        ambientColor = tokens.cardShadowTint,
                        spotColor = tokens.cardShadowTint,
                    )
                    .clip(frameShape)
                    .border(
                        width = if (isFullscreen) 0.dp else 3.dp,
                        color = if (tokens.isDark) Color(0xFF2B3867) else Color(0xFFCBD5E1),
                        shape = frameShape,
                    )
                    .background(if (tokens.isDark) NavyVoid else Color.White),
            ) {
                Column(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
                    if (previewState.isLoading) {
                        LinearProgressIndicator(
                            progress = { (previewState.progress / 100f).coerceIn(0.05f, 1f) },
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                            color = ElectricViolet,
                        )
                    }
                    Box(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                        AndroidView(
                            factory = { ctx ->
                                WebView(ctx).also { wv ->
                                    wiredController.attach(wv, webCfg, savedWebBundle.takeIf { !it.isEmpty })
                                }
                            },
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                        )

                        val errInfo = previewState.errorInfo
                        if (errInfo != null || previewState.errorDescription != null) {
                            Box(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxSize()
                                    .background(MaterialTheme.colorScheme.background.copy(alpha = 0.94f))
                                    .padding(20.dp)
                                    .testTag("preview_error_overlay"),
                                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
                            ) {
                                GlassCard(borderColor = ErrorCoral) {
                                    Text(
                                        text = errInfo?.titleFa ?: appString(R.string.preview_error_title),
                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                                        color = ErrorCoral,
                                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                    )
                                    Spacer(Modifier.height(6.dp))
                                    Text(
                                        text = errInfo?.messageFa ?: previewState.errorDescription.orEmpty(),
                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                    )
                                    if (errInfo != null && errInfo.technicalDetail.isNotBlank()) {
                                        Spacer(Modifier.height(6.dp))
                                        Text(
                                            text = errInfo.technicalDetail,
                                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(fontFamily = JetBrainsMonoFamily),
                                            color = WarningAmber,
                                        )
                                    }
                                    Spacer(Modifier.height(14.dp))
                                    Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                                        Button(
                                            onClick = { wiredController.reload() },
                                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                                        ) {
                                            Text(appString(R.string.preview_retry_btn))
                                        }
                                        if (previewState.canGoBack) {
                                            OutlinedButton(
                                                onClick = { wiredController.goBack() },
                                                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                                            ) {
                                                Text("صفحه قبل")
                                            }
                                        }
                                    }
                                    Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
                                    OutlinedButton(
                                        onClick = {
                                            runCatching {
                                                context.startActivity(
                                                    Intent(Settings.ACTION_WIRELESS_SETTINGS).addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
                                                )
                                            }
                                        },
                                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                    ) {
                                        Text("بررسی تنظیمات اتصال اینترنت")
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }

                        popupWebView?.let { pWeb ->
                            Column(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxSize()
                                    .background(MaterialTheme.colorScheme.background)
                                    .testTag("preview_internal_popup"),
                            ) {
                                Surface(color = tokens.glassContainerStrong, border = BorderStroke(1.dp, tokens.navPillBorder)) {
                                    Row(
                                        modifier = Modifier
                                            .fillMaxWidth()
                                            .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 4.dp),
                                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                                    ) {
                                        Text("پنجره داخلی", style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleSmall, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                                        IconButton(onClick = { wiredController.closeInternalPopup() }) {
                                            Icon(Icons.Rounded.Close, contentDescription = "بستن پنجره")
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                                AndroidView(
                                    factory = { pWeb },
                                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1f).fillMaxWidth(),
                                )
                            }
                        }

                        previewState.blockedNotice?.let { note ->
                            Surface(
                                color = WarningAmber.copy(alpha = 0.94f),
                                shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .align(Alignment.BottomCenter)
                                    .padding(12.dp),
                            ) {
                                Text(
                                    text = note,
                                    color = Color.Black,
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium,
                                    modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 6.dp),
                                )
                            }
                        }

                        customVideoView?.let { vid ->
                            AndroidView(
                                factory = { ctx ->
                                    FrameLayout(ctx).apply {
                                        addView(vid)
                                    }
                                },
                                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }

                if (isFullscreen) {
                    IconButton(
                        onClick = { isFullscreen = false },
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .align(Alignment.TopEnd)
                            .padding(12.dp)
                            .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.55f), RoundedCornerShape(50)),
                    ) {
                        Icon(Icons.Rounded.FullscreenExit, contentDescription = "خروج", tint = Color.White)
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    previewState.sslWarningUrl?.let { badUrl ->
        AlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { wiredController.resolveSslDecision(false) },
            title = { Text(appString(R.string.preview_ssl_title)) },
            text = { Text("گواهی امنیتی آدرس زیر معتبر نیست:\n$badUrl\nبرای حفظ امنیت، اتصال مسدود شود؟") },
            confirmButton = {
                TextButton(onClick = { wiredController.resolveSslDecision(false) }) {
                    Text("مسدود کن (امن)", color = SuccessEmerald)
                }
            },
            dismissButton = {
                TextButton(onClick = { wiredController.resolveSslDecision(true) }) {
                    Text("عبور موقت", color = ErrorCoral)
                }
            },
        )
    }

    previewState.pendingRationale?.let { rationale ->
        AlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { wiredController.resolveRationale(false) },
            title = { Text(rationale.titleFa) },
            text = { Text(rationale.messageFa) },
            confirmButton = {
                TextButton(onClick = { wiredController.resolveRationale(true) }) {
                    Text("موافقم و ادامه")
                }
            },
            dismissButton = {
                TextButton(onClick = { wiredController.resolveRationale(false) }) {
                    Text("انصراف", color = ErrorCoral)
                }
            },
        )
    }

    previewState.pendingExternalConfirm?.let { confirmExt ->
        AlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { wiredController.resolveExternalNavigation(false) },
            title = { Text("باز کردن لینک خارجی") },
            text = { Text(confirmExt.reasonFa) },
            confirmButton = {
                TextButton(onClick = { wiredController.resolveExternalNavigation(true) }) {
                    Text("باز کردن با برنامه دیگر")
                }
            },
            dismissButton = {
                TextButton(onClick = { wiredController.resolveExternalNavigation(false) }) {
                    Text("انصراف")
                }
            },
        )
    }

    if (offerCameraChoice && activeUploadOptions != null) {
        AlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { wiredController.cancelUpload() },
            title = { Text("انتخاب منبع بارگذاری فایل") },
            text = { Text("فایل یا تصویر را از گالری انتخاب کنید یا عکس جدید بگیرید.") },
            confirmButton = {
                TextButton(
                    onClick = {
                        runCatching { fileLauncher.launch(activeUploadOptions.chooserIntent) }
                            .onFailure { wiredController.cancelUpload() }
                    }
                ) {
                    Icon(Icons.Rounded.FolderOpen, contentDescription = null, modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp))
                    Spacer(Modifier.width(4.dp))
                    Text("انتخاب فایل")
                }
            },
            dismissButton = {
                Row {
                    TextButton(
                        onClick = {
                            wiredController.requestCameraForUpload { granted ->
                                if (granted) {
                                    val pair = wiredController.prepareCameraCapture()
                                    if (pair != null) {
                                        runCatching { cameraLauncher.launch(pair.second) }
                                            .onFailure { wiredController.completeCameraCapture(false) }
                                    } else {
                                        wiredController.completeCameraCapture(false)
                                    }
                                } else {
                                    wiredController.cancelUpload()
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    ) {
                        Icon(Icons.Rounded.CameraAlt, contentDescription = null, modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp))
                        Spacer(Modifier.width(4.dp))
                        Text("دوربین")
                    }
                    TextButton(onClick = { wiredController.cancelUpload() }) {
                        Text("لغو", color = ErrorCoral)
                    }
                }
            },
        )
    }

    if (previewState.confirmClearSiteData) {
        AlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { wiredController.confirmClearSiteData(false) },
            title = { Text("پاکسازی کوکی و حافظه سایت") },
            text = { Text("تمام کوکی‌های ورود، حافظه LocalStorage و کش موقت این سایت پاک می‌شود. ادامه می‌دهید؟") },
            confirmButton = {
                TextButton(onClick = { wiredController.confirmClearSiteData(true) }) {
                    Text("پاکسازی کن", color = ErrorCoral)
                }
            },
            dismissButton = {
                TextButton(onClick = { wiredController.confirmClearSiteData(false) }) {
                    Text("انصراف")
                }
            },
        )
    }

    if (previewState.confirmExitPrompt) {
        AlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { wiredController.dismissExitConfirm(false, onBack) },
            title = { Text("خروج از پیش‌نمایش") },
            text = { Text("آیا مطمئن هستید که می‌خواهید پیش‌نمایش وب‌سایت را ببندید؟") },
            confirmButton = {
                TextButton(onClick = { wiredController.dismissExitConfirm(true, onBack) }) {
                    Text("بله، خروج")
                }
            },
            dismissButton = {
                TextButton(onClick = { wiredController.dismissExitConfirm(false, onBack) }) {
                    Text("ادامه مشاهده")
                }
            },
        )
    }

    previewState.pendingJsDialog?.let { jsDlg ->
        AlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { wiredController.resolveJsDialog(false) },
            title = { Text(jsDlg.titleFa) },
            text = { Text(jsDlg.message) },
            confirmButton = {
                TextButton(onClick = { wiredController.resolveJsDialog(true) }) {
                    Text("تأیید")
                }
            },
            dismissButton = if (jsDlg.isConfirm) {
                {
                    TextButton(onClick = { wiredController.resolveJsDialog(false) }) {
                        Text("لغو", color = ErrorCoral)
                    }
                }
            } else {
                null
            },
        )
    }

    if (showDownloadsDialog) {
        AlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { showDownloadsDialog = false },
            title = { Text("مدیریت دانلودها") },
            text = {
                LazyColumn(
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().height(260.dp),
                ) {
                    items(previewState.activeDownloads) { dl ->
                        GlassCard {
                            Text(dl.fileName, style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleSmall, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                            Text(
                                "${dl.status.titleFa} • ${dl.progressPercent}%",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                            )
                            if (dl.status == DownloadItemStatus.RUNNING || dl.status == DownloadItemStatus.PENDING) {
                                Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp))
                                LinearProgressIndicator(
                                    progress = { (dl.progressPercent / 100f).coerceIn(0.05f, 1f) },
                                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                )
                            }
                            Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                                if (dl.status == DownloadItemStatus.SUCCESSFUL) {
                                    TextButton(onClick = { wiredController.openDownload(dl) }) {
                                        Text("باز کردن فایل")
                                    }
                                }
                                if (dl.status == DownloadItemStatus.RUNNING || dl.status == DownloadItemStatus.PENDING || dl.status == DownloadItemStatus.PAUSED) {
                                    TextButton(onClick = { wiredController.cancelDownload(dl.downloadId) }) {
                                        Text("لغو", color = ErrorCoral)
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            },
            confirmButton = {
                TextButton(onClick = { showDownloadsDialog = false }) {
                    Text("بستن")
                }
            },
            dismissButton = {
                TextButton(onClick = { wiredController.refreshDownloads() }) {
                    Text("بروزرسانی")
                }
            },
        )
    }
}package com.example.ui.screens

import androidx.compose.animation.core.animateDpAsState
import androidx.compose.animation.core.animateFloatAsState
import androidx.compose.foundation.ExperimentalFoundationApi
import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.border
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Arrangement
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Box
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Column
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Row
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Spacer
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.fillMaxSize

private data class OnboardingPage(
    val icon: Webino3DIconType,
    val titleFa: String,
    val titleEn: String,
    val bodyFa: String,
    val bodyEn: String,
    val from: Color,
    val to: Color,
)

private val pages = listOf(
    OnboardingPage(
        Webino3DIconType.HOME,

    ),
)

@OptIn(ExperimentalFoundationApi::class)
@Composable
fun OnboardingScreen(
    language: AppLanguage,
    usePersianDigits: Boolean,
    onFinished: () -> Unit,
) {
    val isFa = language == AppLanguage.FA
    val pagerState = rememberPagerState(pageCount = { pages.size })
    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    val isLast = pagerState.currentPage == pages.lastIndex

    WebinoAmbientBackground {
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .safeDrawingPadding()
                .testTag("onboarding_root"),
        ) {
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 4.dp),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = localizeNumber(pagerState.currentPage + 1, language, usePersianDigits) +
                        " / " + localizeNumber(pages.size, language, usePersianDigits),
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge,
                    color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                    modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 8.dp),
                )
                TextButton(onClick = onFinished, modifier = Modifier.testTag("onboarding_skip")) {
                    Text(if (isFa) "رد کردن" else "Skip")
                }
            }

            HorizontalPager(
                state = pagerState,
                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f).fillMaxWidth(),
            ) { index ->
                val page = pages[index]
                Column(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize().padding(horizontal = 28.dp),
                    horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
                ) {
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .size(188.dp)
                            .shadow(24.dp, CircleShape, ambientColor = page.to.copy(alpha = 0.5f), spotColor = page.to.copy(alpha = 0.5f))
                            .clip(CircleShape)
                            .background(Brush.linearGradient(listOf(page.from, page.to)))
                            .border(
                                1.5.dp,
                                Brush.linearGradient(listOf(Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.9f), Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.1f))),
                                CircleShape,
                            ),
                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
                    ) {
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .size(124.dp)
                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(38.dp))
                                .background(Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.30f))
                                .border(1.dp, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.75f), RoundedCornerShape(38.dp)),
                            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
                        ) {
                            Webino3DGlassIcon(type = page.icon, size = 96.dp)
                        }
                    }
                    Spacer(Modifier.height(36.dp))
                    Text(
                        text = if (isFa) page.titleFa else page.titleEn,
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.headlineSmall,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold,
                        textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
                    )
                    Spacer(Modifier.height(14.dp))
                    Text(
                        text = if (isFa) page.bodyFa else page.bodyEn,
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyLarge,
                        color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                        textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
                    )
                }
            }

            Row(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(vertical = 14.dp),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            ) {
                pages.forEachIndexed { i, _ ->
                    val selected = i == pagerState.currentPage
                    val w by animateDpAsState(if (selected) 26.dp else 8.dp, label = "dotW")
                    val a by animateFloatAsState(if (selected) 1f else 0.35f, label = "dotA")
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .padding(horizontal = 4.dp)
                            .height(8.dp)
                            .size(width = w, height = 8.dp)
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(50))
                            .background(GrapePurple.copy(alpha = a)),
                    )
                }
            }

            GradientPrimaryButton(
                text = when {
                    isLast -> if (isFa) "شروع کنیم" else "Get started"
                    else -> if (isFa) "بعدی" else "Next"
                },
                icon = if (isLast) Icons.Rounded.RocketLaunch else if (isFa) Icons.Rounded.ArrowBack else Icons.Rounded.ArrowForward,
                onClick = {
                    if (isLast) onFinished()
                    else scope.launch { pagerState.animateScrollToPage(pagerState.currentPage + 1) }
                },
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .padding(horizontal = 24.dp)
                    .padding(bottom = 20.dp),
                testTag = "onboarding_next",
            )
        }
    }
}package com.example.ui.screens

@Composable
fun OutputsScreen(
    prefs: UserPreferences,
    outputs: List<BuildResultEntity>,
    onVerifyOutput: (Long) -> Unit,
    onDeleteOutput: (Long) -> Unit,
    onNotify: (String) -> Unit,
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val isFa = prefs.language == AppLanguage.FA
    val tokens = WebinoThemeTokens.colors
    var pendingSaveFile by remember { mutableStateOf<BuildResultEntity?>(null) }
    var deleteCandidate by remember { mutableStateOf<BuildResultEntity?>(null) }

    val safSaveLauncher = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(
        ActivityResultContracts.CreateDocument("application/octet-stream")
    ) { destUri ->
        val item = pendingSaveFile
        if (destUri != null && item != null) {
            runCatching {
                val src = File(item.filePath)
                context.contentResolver.openOutputStream(destUri)?.use { out ->
                    src.inputStream().use { it.copyTo(out) }
                }
            }.onSuccess {
                onNotify("فایل در مسیر انتخابی ذخیره شد.")
            }.onFailure {
                onNotify("خطا در ذخیره فایل در حافظه دستگاه.")
            }
        }
        pendingSaveFile = null
    }

    LazyColumn(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
        contentPadding = PaddingValues(start = 16.dp, end = 16.dp, top = 12.dp, bottom = 24.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp),
    ) {
        item {
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .statusBarsPadding(),
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
            ) {
                Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically, modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                    Webino3DGlassIcon(type = Webino3DIconType.OUTPUTS, size = 40.dp)
                    Spacer(Modifier.width(10.dp))
                    Column {
                        Text(
                            text = appString(R.string.outputs_title),
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.headlineSmall,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        )
                        Text(
                            text = if (isFa) "فایل‌های امضاشده و آماده نصب" else "Signed builds ready for distribution",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                            color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                        )
                    }
                }
                if (outputs.isNotEmpty()) {
                    StatusBadge(
                        text = if (isFa) "${localizeNumber(outputs.size, prefs.language, prefs.usePersianDigits)} خروجی" else "${outputs.size} files",
                        color = MintGreen,
                    )
                }
            }
        }

        if (outputs.isEmpty()) {
            item {
                EmptyStateCard(
                    icon = Icons.Rounded.Android,
                    icon3DType = Webino3DIconType.OUTPUTS,
                    title = appString(R.string.outputs_empty_title),
                    subtitle = appString(R.string.outputs_empty_sub),
                )
            }
        } else {
            items(outputs, key = { it.id }) { item ->
                val isApk = item.format != BuildOutputFormat.AAB.name
                GlassCard(
                    borderColor = if (item.isVerified) SuccessEmerald.copy(alpha = 0.35f) else WarningAmber.copy(alpha = 0.35f),
                ) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    ) {
                        Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically, modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                            Webino3DGlassIcon(
                                type = if (isApk) Webino3DIconType.OUTPUTS else Webino3DIconType.BUILD,
                                size = 42.dp,
                            )
                            Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
                            Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                                Text(
                                    text = "${item.appName} • ${item.format}",
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                )
                                Text(
                                    text = "${item.packageName} • v${item.versionName}",
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall.copy(
                                        fontFamily = JetBrainsMonoFamily,
                                        textDirection = TextDirection.Ltr,
                                    ),
                                    color = SkyBlue,
                                    maxLines = 1,
                                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                                )
                            }
                        }
                        Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
                        StatusBadge(
                            text = if (item.isVerified) "امضا و تأییدشده" else "نیاز به بررسی",
                            color = if (item.isVerified) SuccessEmerald else WarningAmber,
                            icon = Icons.Rounded.Verified,
                        )
                    }
                    Spacer(Modifier.height(10.dp))
                    Text(
                        text = "${JalaliCalendar.formatDateTime(item.createdAt, prefs.calendarType == CalendarType.JALALI, isFa, prefs.usePersianDigits)} • ${ValidationUtils.formatFileSize(item.fileSizeBytes, isFa, prefs.usePersianDigits)}",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                        color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                    )
                    if (item.validationSummary.isNotBlank()) {
                        Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp))
                        Text(
                            text = item.validationSummary,
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                            color = SuccessEmerald,
                        )
                    }
                    Spacer(Modifier.height(10.dp))
                    Surface(
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
                        color = tokens.subtleSurface,
                    ) {
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .padding(horizontal = 4.dp, vertical = 2.dp),
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                        ) {
                            if (isApk) {
                                IconButton(onClick = { installOrOpenOutput(context, item.filePath, onNotify) }) {
                                    Icon(Icons.Rounded.InstallMobile, contentDescription = "نصب APK", tint = SuccessEmerald)
                                }
                            }
                            IconButton(onClick = { shareOutput(context, item.filePath, item.appName, onNotify) }) {
                                Icon(Icons.Rounded.Share, contentDescription = "اشتراک‌گذاری", tint = SkyBlue)
                            }
                            IconButton(
                                onClick = {
                                    pendingSaveFile = item
                                    val ext = if (isApk) "apk" else "aab"
                                    safSaveLauncher.launch("${item.packageName}_v${item.versionName}.$ext")
                                }
                            ) {
                                Icon(Icons.Rounded.SaveAlt, contentDescription = "ذخیره با SAF", tint = ElectricViolet)
                            }
                            IconButton(onClick = { onVerifyOutput(item.id) }) {
                                Icon(Icons.Rounded.VerifiedUser, contentDescription = "بررسی امضا")
                            }
                            IconButton(onClick = { deleteCandidate = item }) {
                                Icon(Icons.Rounded.Delete, contentDescription = "حذف", tint = ErrorCoral)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        item { Spacer(Modifier.height(20.dp)) }
    }

    deleteCandidate?.let { target ->
        AlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { deleteCandidate = null },
            title = { Text("حذف فایل خروجی") },
            text = { Text("فایل ${target.appName} (${target.format}) حذف شود؟") },
            confirmButton = {
                TextButton(
                    onClick = {
                        onDeleteOutput(target.id)
                        deleteCandidate = null
                    }
                ) { Text("حذف", color = ErrorCoral) }
            },
            dismissButton = {
                TextButton(onClick = { deleteCandidate = null }) { Text("انصراف") }
            },
        )
    }
}

private fun installOrOpenOutput(context: Context, filePath: String, onNotify: (String) -> Unit) {
    val file = File(filePath)
    if (!file.exists()) {
        onNotify("فایل خروجی یافت نشد.")
        return
    }

    // بررسی مجوز نصب برنامه‌های ناشناس برای اندروید 8 (API 26) به بالا
    if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
        if (!context.packageManager.canRequestPackageInstalls()) {
            onNotify("برای نصب مستقیم، لطفاً به برنامه اجازه نصب برنامه‌های ناشناس را بدهید.")
            val intent = Intent(Settings.ACTION_MANAGE_UNKNOWN_APP_SOURCES).apply {
                data = Uri.parse("package:${context.packageName}")
                addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
            }
            context.startActivity(intent)
            return
        }
    }

    runCatching {
        val uri = FileProvider.getUriForFile(context, "${context.packageName}.fileprovider", file)
        val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW).apply {
            setDataAndType(uri, "application/vnd.android.package-archive")
            addFlags(Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION or Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
        }
        context.startActivity(intent)
    }.onFailure {
        onNotify("امکان اجرای مستقیم نصب‌کننده در این محیط وجود ندارد؛ از دکمه ذخیره یا اشتراک‌گذاری استفاده کنید.")
    }
}

private fun shareOutput(context: Context, filePath: String, appName: String, onNotify: (String) -> Unit) {
    val file = File(filePath)
    if (!file.exists()) {
        onNotify("فایل یافت نشد.")
        return
    }
    runCatching {
        val uri = FileProvider.getUriForFile(context, "${context.packageName}.fileprovider", file)
        val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_SEND).apply {
            type = "application/octet-stream"
            putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_STREAM, uri)
            putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_SUBJECT, appName)
            addFlags(Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION)
        }
        context.startActivity(Intent.createChooser(intent, appName))
    }.onFailure {
        onNotify("خطا در اشتراک‌گذاری فایل.")
    }
}

@Composable
fun SigningManagerScreen(
    keys: List<SigningKeyMetadataEntity>,
    onBack: () -> Unit,
    onCreateKey: (alias: String, password: String, cn: String, org: String, country: String, years: Int) -> Unit,
    onImportKey: (Uri, String) -> Unit,
    onSetDefault: (Long) -> Unit,
    onChangePassword: (Long, String) -> Unit,
    onVerifyKey: (Long) -> Unit,
    onDeleteKey: (Long) -> Unit,
    onNotify: (String) -> Unit,
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val tokens = WebinoThemeTokens.colors
    var showCreateDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var importUriPending by remember { mutableStateOf<Uri?>(null) }
    var passwordChangeKey by remember { mutableStateOf<SigningKeyMetadataEntity?>(null) }
    var backupKeyPending by remember { mutableStateOf<SigningKeyMetadataEntity?>(null) }
    var deleteKeyCandidate by remember { mutableStateOf<SigningKeyMetadataEntity?>(null) }

    val importPicker = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(ActivityResultContracts.OpenDocument()) { uri ->
        if (uri != null) importUriPending = uri
    }

    val backupSaver = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(ActivityResultContracts.CreateDocument("application/x-pkcs12")) { destUri ->
        val key = backupKeyPending
        if (destUri != null && key != null) {
            runCatching {
                val src = File(key.keystorePath)
                context.contentResolver.openOutputStream(destUri)?.use { out ->
                    src.inputStream().use { it.copyTo(out) }
                }
            }.onSuccess { onNotify("پشتیبان رمزنگاری‌شده Keystore ذخیره شد.") }
                .onFailure { onNotify("خطا در تهیه نسخه پشتیبان.") }
        }
        backupKeyPending = null
    }

    Scaffold(
        containerColor = Color.Transparent,
        topBar = {
            WebinoScreenHeader(
                title = "مدیریت امضای دیجیتال و Keystore",
                subtitle = "کلیدهای امضای PKCS12 / RSA-2048",
                iconType = Webino3DIconType.SIGNING,
                onBack = onBack,
            )
        }
    ) { padding ->
        LazyColumn(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .padding(padding),
            contentPadding = PaddingValues(16.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp),
        ) {
            item {
                GlassCard(borderColor = WarningAmber.copy(alpha = 0.45f)) {
                    Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                        Icon(Icons.Rounded.Warning, contentDescription = null, tint = WarningAmber)
                        Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
                        Text(
                            text = "الزام امضای سازگار برای بروزرسانی اپلیکیشن",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleSmall,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            color = WarningAmber,
                        )
                    }
                    Spacer(Modifier.height(6.dp))
                    Text(
                        text = "سیستم‌عامل اندروید و فروشگاه‌هایی مثل مایکت، کافه‌بازار و گوگل‌پلی تنها زمانی اجازه نصب یا انتشار آپدیت نسخه جدید را می‌دهند که با همان کلید قبلی (گواهی یکسان) امضا شده باشد. از کلید خود حتماً نسخه پشتیبان تهیه کنید.",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                        color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                    )
                }
            }
            item {
                Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)) {
                    OutlinedButton(
                        onClick = { showCreateDialog = true },
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                        border = BorderStroke(1.dp, ElectricViolet.copy(alpha = 0.45f)),
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f).height(48.dp),
                    ) {
                        Icon(Icons.Rounded.Add, contentDescription = null, tint = ElectricViolet)
                        Spacer(Modifier.width(6.dp))
                        Text("ساخت کلید جدید")
                    }
                    OutlinedButton(
                        onClick = { importPicker.launch(arrayOf("*/*")) },
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                        border = BorderStroke(1.dp, SkyBlue.copy(alpha = 0.45f)),
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f).height(48.dp),
                    ) {
                        Icon(Icons.Rounded.FileOpen, contentDescription = null, tint = SkyBlue)
                        Spacer(Modifier.width(6.dp))
                        Text("وارد کردن Keystore")
                    }
                }
            }

            items(keys, key = { it.id }) { key ->
                GlassCard(borderColor = if (key.isDefault) SkyBlue.copy(alpha = 0.6f) else tokens.navPillBorder) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    ) {
                        Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically, modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                            Webino3DGlassIcon(type = Webino3DIconType.SIGNING, size = 38.dp)
                            Spacer(Modifier.width(10.dp))
                            Column {
                                Text(key.alias, style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                                Text("${key.commonName} (${key.keystoreType})", style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall, color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant)
                            }
                        }
                        if (key.isDefault) {
                            StatusBadge(text = "کلید پیش‌فرض", color = SuccessEmerald)
                        } else {
                            TextButton(onClick = { onSetDefault(key.id) }) { Text("انتخاب پیش‌فرض") }
                        }
                    }
                    Spacer(Modifier.height(10.dp))
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .background(tokens.subtleSurface, RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                            .padding(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 6.dp),
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            text = "SHA-256: ${key.sha256Fingerprint}",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(
                                fontFamily = JetBrainsMonoFamily,
                                textDirection = TextDirection.Ltr,
                            ),
                            color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                        )
                    }
                    Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                    ) {
                        IconButton(onClick = { onVerifyKey(key.id) }) {
                            Icon(Icons.Rounded.VerifiedUser, contentDescription = "بررسی اعتبار امضا", tint = SuccessEmerald)
                        }
                        IconButton(onClick = { passwordChangeKey = key }) {
                            Icon(Icons.Rounded.LockReset, contentDescription = "تغییر رمز")
                        }
                        IconButton(
                            onClick = {
                                backupKeyPending = key
                                backupSaver.launch("${key.alias}_backup.p12")
                            }
                        ) {
                            Icon(Icons.Rounded.Backup, contentDescription = "پشتیبان‌گیری امن", tint = CyanAccent)
                        }
                        if (!key.isDefault) {
                            IconButton(onClick = { deleteKeyCandidate = key }) {
                                Icon(Icons.Rounded.Delete, contentDescription = "حذف", tint = ErrorCoral)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    deleteKeyCandidate?.let { target ->
        AlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { deleteKeyCandidate = null },
            title = { Text("تأیید حذف کلید امضا") },
            text = {
                Text(
                    "آیا از حذف کلید «${target.alias}» اطمینان دارید؟ در صورت نداشتن نسخه پشتیبان، دیگر امکان امضای آپدیت برنامه‌های قبلی با این کلید وجود نخواهد داشت."
                )
            },
            confirmButton = {
                TextButton(
                    onClick = {
                        onDeleteKey(target.id)
                        deleteKeyCandidate = null
                    }
                ) { Text("حذف نهایی", color = ErrorCoral) }
            },
            dismissButton = {
                TextButton(onClick = { deleteKeyCandidate = null }) { Text("انصراف") }
            },
        )
    }

    if (showCreateDialog) {
        var alias by remember { mutableStateOf("my_release_key") }
        var password by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
        var cn by remember { mutableStateOf("My Company") }
        var org by remember { mutableStateOf("Webino Apps") }
        AlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { showCreateDialog = false },
            title = { Text("ایجاد کلید امضای RSA 2048") },
            text = {
                Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                    OutlinedTextField(value = alias, onValueChange = { alias = it }, label = { Text("نام مستعار (Alias)") }, singleLine = true)
                    OutlinedTextField(
                        value = password,
                        onValueChange = { password = it },
                        label = { Text("رمز عبور Keystore (حداقل ۶ کاراکتر)") },
                        visualTransformation = PasswordVisualTransformation(),
                        singleLine = true,
                    )
                    OutlinedTextField(value = cn, onValueChange = { cn = it }, label = { Text("نام صادرکننده (CN)") }, singleLine = true)
                    OutlinedTextField(value = org, onValueChange = { org = it }, label = { Text("سازمان (O)") }, singleLine = true)
                }
            },
            confirmButton = {
                TextButton(
                    onClick = {
                        if (password.length >= 6) {
                            onCreateKey(alias, password, cn, org, "IR", 25)
                            showCreateDialog = false
                        } else {
                            onNotify("رمز باید حداقل ۶ کاراکتر باشد.")
                        }
                    }
                ) { Text("ایجاد کلید") }
            },
            dismissButton = { TextButton(onClick = { showCreateDialog = false }) { Text("لغو") } },
        )
    }

    importUriPending?.let { uri ->
        var pwd by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
        AlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { importUriPending = null },
            title = { Text("رمز Keystore را وارد کنید") },
            text = {
                OutlinedTextField(
                    value = pwd,
                    onValueChange = { pwd = it },
                    label = { Text("رمز فایل Keystore") },
                    visualTransformation = PasswordVisualTransformation(),
                    singleLine = true,
                )
            },
            confirmButton = {
                TextButton(
                    onClick = {
                        onImportKey(uri, pwd)
                        importUriPending = null
                    }
                ) { Text("وارد کردن") }
            },
            dismissButton = { TextButton(onClick = { importUriPending = null }) { Text("لغو") } },
        )
    }

    passwordChangeKey?.let { key ->
        var newPwd by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
        AlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { passwordChangeKey = null },
            title = { Text("تغییر رمز کلید ${key.alias}") },
            text = {
                OutlinedTextField(
                    value = newPwd,
                    onValueChange = { newPwd = it },
                    label = { Text("رمز جدید (حداقل ۶ کاراکتر)") },
                    visualTransformation = PasswordVisualTransformation(),
                    singleLine = true,
                )
            },
            confirmButton = {
                TextButton(
                    onClick = {
                        if (newPwd.length >= 6) {
                            onChangePassword(key.id, newPwd)
                            passwordChangeKey = null
                        } else {
                            onNotify("رمز جدید باید حداقل ۶ کاراکتر باشد.")
                        }
                    }
                ) { Text("ذخیره رمز") }
            },
            dismissButton = { TextButton(onClick = { passwordChangeKey = null }) { Text("لغو") } },
        )
    }
}package com.example.ui.screens

import androidx.activity.compose.rememberLauncherForActivityResult
import androidx.activity.result.contract.ActivityResultContracts
import androidx.compose.foundation.BorderStroke
import androidx.compose.foundation.Image
import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.border
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Arrangement
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Box
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Column
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.PaddingValues
