data class CreateProjectFormState(
    val urlInput: String = "",
    val appName: String = "",
    val packageName: String = "",
    val versionName: String = "1.0.0",
    val versionCode: String = "1",
    val description: String = "",
    val selectedTemplate: AppTemplateType = AppTemplateType.STORE,
    val allowInsecureHttp: Boolean = false,
    val isInspecting: Boolean = false,
    val inspectedSite: SiteMetadata? = null,
    val errorMessage: String? = null,
)

data class StorageStats(
    val outputsBytes: Long = 0L,
    val cacheBytes: Long = 0L,
    val mediaBytes: Long = 0L,
)

class WebinoViewModel(application: Application) : AndroidViewModel(application) {

    private val repo = (application as WebinoApplication).repository
    private val inspectBuildEnvUseCase = InspectBuildEnvironmentUseCase(repo)
    private val startBuildUseCase = StartBuildUseCase(repo)
    private val cancelBuildUseCase = CancelBuildUseCase(repo)
    private val retryBuildUseCase = RetryBuildUseCase(repo)

    val preferences: StateFlow<UserPreferences> = repo.preferences.stateIn(
        viewModelScope,
        SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(5000),
        UserPreferences(),
    )

    val projects: StateFlow<List<FullProjectBundle>> = repo.allProjects.stateIn(
        viewModelScope,
        SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(5000),
        emptyList(),
    )

    val buildResults: StateFlow<List<BuildResultEntity>> = repo.allBuildResults.stateIn(
        viewModelScope,
        SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(5000),
        emptyList(),
    )

    val latestBuildTask: StateFlow<BuildTaskEntity?> = repo.latestBuildTask.stateIn(
        viewModelScope,
        SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(5000),
        null,
    )

    val recentBuildTasks: StateFlow<List<BuildTaskEntity>> = repo.recentBuildTasks.stateIn(
        viewModelScope,
        SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(5000),
        emptyList(),
    )

    val allBuildTasks: StateFlow<List<BuildTaskEntity>> = repo.allBuildTasks.stateIn(
        viewModelScope,
        SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(5000),
        emptyList(),
    )

    val signingKeys: StateFlow<List<SigningKeyMetadataEntity>> = repo.signingKeys.stateIn(
        viewModelScope,
        SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(5000),
        emptyList(),
    )

    val templates: StateFlow<List<AppTemplateEntity>> = repo.templates.stateIn(
        viewModelScope,
        SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(5000),
        emptyList(),
    )

    val recentWebsites: StateFlow<List<RecentWebsiteEntity>> = repo.recentWebsites.stateIn(
        viewModelScope,
        SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(5000),
        emptyList(),
    )

    private val _environmentReport = MutableStateFlow(repo.inspectEnvironment())
    val environmentReport: StateFlow<EnvironmentCheckReport> = _environmentReport.asStateFlow()

    private val _storageStats = MutableStateFlow(StorageStats())
    val storageStats: StateFlow<StorageStats> = _storageStats.asStateFlow()

    private val _createForm = MutableStateFlow(CreateProjectFormState())
    val createForm: StateFlow<CreateProjectFormState> = _createForm.asStateFlow()

    /** Picks the snackbar text matching the user's chosen app language. */
    private fun tr(fa: String, en: String): String =
        if (preferences.value.language == AppLanguage.FA) fa else en

    private val _snackbarMessage = MutableStateFlow<String?>(null)
    val snackbarMessage: StateFlow<String?> = _snackbarMessage.asStateFlow()

    private val _hasCompletedSplash = MutableStateFlow(false)
    val hasCompletedSplash: StateFlow<Boolean> = _hasCompletedSplash.asStateFlow()

    init {
        refreshSystemStats()
    }

    fun refreshSystemStats() {
        _environmentReport.value = inspectBuildEnvUseCase()
        val (outB, cacheB, mediaB) = repo.calculateStorageBreakdown()
        _storageStats.value = StorageStats(outB, cacheB, mediaB)
    }

    fun recheckEnvironment() {
        _environmentReport.value = _environmentReport.value.copy(
            status = com.example.domain.model.BuildEngineStatus.CHECKING
        )
        viewModelScope.launch {
            kotlinx.coroutines.delay(180)
            refreshSystemStats()
            val status = _environmentReport.value.status
            _snackbarMessage.value = tr("وضعیت موتور Build: ${status.titleFa}", "Build engine: ${status.name}")
        }
    }

    suspend fun exportProjectJson(projectId: Long): String? = repo.exportProjectJson(projectId)

    fun importProjectJson(rawJson: String, onImported: ((Long) -> Unit)? = null) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            repo.importProjectJson(rawJson)
                .onSuccess { newId ->
                    refreshSystemStats()
                    _snackbarMessage.value = tr("تنظیمات پروژه با موفقیت وارد شد.", "Project settings imported successfully.")
                    onImported?.invoke(newId)
                }
                .onFailure { err ->
                    _snackbarMessage.value = err.localizedMessage ?: tr("فایل JSON نامعتبر است.", "Invalid JSON file.")
                }
        }
    }

    fun consumeSnackbar() {
        _snackbarMessage.value = null
    }

    fun postMessage(msg: String) {
        _snackbarMessage.value = msg
    }

    fun markSplashCompleted() {
        _hasCompletedSplash.value = true
    }

    fun observeProject(projectId: Long): Flow<FullProjectBundle?> = repo.observeProject(projectId)
    fun observeBuildTask(taskId: Long): Flow<BuildTaskEntity?> = repo.observeBuildTask(taskId)
    fun observeBuildLogs(taskId: Long): Flow<List<BuildLogEntity>> = repo.observeBuildLogs(taskId)

    fun resetCreateForm(presetTemplateId: String? = null) {
        val tmpl = AppTemplateType.entries.firstOrNull { it.id == presetTemplateId } ?: AppTemplateType.STORE
        _createForm.value = CreateProjectFormState(selectedTemplate = tmpl)
    }

    fun updateCreateForm(transform: (CreateProjectFormState) -> CreateProjectFormState) {
        _createForm.value = transform(_createForm.value)
    }

    fun inspectSiteForCreateForm() {
        val raw = _createForm.value.urlInput.trim()
        if (!ValidationUtils.isValidWebUrl(raw)) {
            _createForm.value = _createForm.value.copy(errorMessage = "لطفاً آدرس وبسایت معتبر وارد کنید (مثلاً https://site.ir)")
            return
        }
        viewModelScope.launch {
            _createForm.value = _createForm.value.copy(isInspecting = true, errorMessage = null)
            val meta = repo.inspectWebsite(raw)
            val current = _createForm.value
            val suggestedName = current.appName.ifBlank { meta.title.take(28) }
            val suggestedPkg = current.packageName.ifBlank {
                ValidationUtils.suggestPackageName(meta.finalUrl, suggestedName)
            }
            val suggestedDesc = current.description.ifBlank { meta.description }
            _createForm.value = current.copy(
                urlInput = meta.finalUrl,
                appName = suggestedName,
                packageName = suggestedPkg,
                description = suggestedDesc,
                isInspecting = false,
                inspectedSite = meta,
                errorMessage = if (!meta.isReachable) meta.errorMessage else null,
            )
        }
    }

    fun createProjectFromForm(onCreated: (Long) -> Unit) {
        val f = _createForm.value
        val normalizedUrl = ValidationUtils.normalizeUrl(f.urlInput)
        if (!ValidationUtils.isValidWebUrl(normalizedUrl)) {
            _createForm.value = f.copy(errorMessage = "آدرس وب‌سایت معتبر نیست.")
            return
        }
        if (!ValidationUtils.isHttpsUrl(normalizedUrl) && !f.allowInsecureHttp) {
            _createForm.value = f.copy(
                errorMessage = "آدرس‌های غیرامن HTTP به‌صورت پیش‌فرض پذیرفته نمی‌شوند. از HTTPS استفاده کنید یا مجوز موقت HTTP را فعال نمایید."
            )
            return
        }
        if (f.appName.trim().isEmpty()) {
            _createForm.value = f.copy(errorMessage = "نام اپلیکیشن نمی‌تواند خالی باشد.")
            return
        }
        val pkg = f.packageName.trim().ifBlank { ValidationUtils.suggestPackageName(normalizedUrl, f.appName) }
        if (!ValidationUtils.isValidPackageName(pkg)) {
            _createForm.value = f.copy(errorMessage = "نام بسته (Package Name) نامعتبر است. مثال: ir.mysite.app")
            return
        }
        val verCode = f.versionCode.toIntOrNull()?.coerceAtLeast(1) ?: 1
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val id = repo.createProject(
                name = f.appName.trim(),
                websiteUrl = normalizedUrl,
                packageName = pkg,
                versionName = f.versionName.trim().ifBlank { "1.0.0" },
                versionCode = verCode,
                description = f.description.trim(),
                template = f.selectedTemplate,
                faviconUrl = f.inspectedSite?.faviconUrl,
                themeColorOverride = f.inspectedSite?.themeColorHex,
            )
            refreshSystemStats()
            onCreated(id)
        }
    }

    fun saveProjectChanges(
        project: ProjectEntity,
        settings: ProjectSettingsEntity,
        webSettings: WebSettingsEntity,
        changeNote: String,
    ) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            repo.updateProjectBundle(project, settings, webSettings, changeNote)
            _snackbarMessage.value = tr("تغییرات پروژه ذخیره شد.", "Project changes saved.")
        }
    }

    fun duplicateProject(projectId: Long) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            repo.duplicateProject(projectId)
            _snackbarMessage.value = tr("نسخه کپی پروژه ایجاد شد.", "Project copy created.")
        }
    }

    fun deleteProject(projectId: Long) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            repo.deleteProject(projectId)
            refreshSystemStats()
            _snackbarMessage.value = tr("پروژه حذف شد.", "Project deleted.")
        }
    }

    fun importProjectImage(projectId: Long, uri: Uri, isSplash: Boolean, onSaved: (String) -> Unit) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            repo.applyProjectImage(projectId, uri, isSplash)
                .onSuccess { savedPath ->
                    onSaved(savedPath)
                    _snackbarMessage.value = if (isSplash) {
                        tr("تصویر Splash ذخیره و اعمال شد.", "Splash image saved and applied.")
                    } else {
                        tr("آیکون برنامه ذخیره و اعمال شد.", "App icon saved and applied.")
                    }
                }
                .onFailure { e ->
                    val reason = e.localizedMessage ?: e.javaClass.simpleName
                    _snackbarMessage.value = tr("خطا در پردازش تصویر: $reason", "Could not process the image: $reason")
                }
        }
    }

    fun startBuild(
        projectId: Long,
        outputFormat: BuildOutputFormat,
        signingKeyId: Long,
        onTaskCreated: (Long) -> Unit,
    ) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            startBuildUseCase(projectId, outputFormat, signingKeyId)
                .onSuccess { taskId ->
                    onTaskCreated(taskId)
                }
                .onFailure { err ->
                    _snackbarMessage.value = err.message ?: tr("خطا در اعتبارسنجی مشخصات پیش از ساخت.", "Pre-build validation failed.")
                }
        }
    }

    fun retryBuild(
        taskId: Long,
        onTaskCreated: (Long) -> Unit,
    ) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            retryBuildUseCase(taskId)
                .onSuccess { newTaskId ->
                    onTaskCreated(newTaskId)
                }
                .onFailure { err ->
                    _snackbarMessage.value = err.message ?: tr("خطا در شروع مجدد ساخت.", "Could not restart the build.")
                }
        }
    }

    fun cancelBuild(taskId: Long) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            cancelBuildUseCase(taskId)
            refreshSystemStats()
            _snackbarMessage.value = tr("فرایند ساخت لغو شد.", "Build cancelled.")
        }
    }

    fun verifyOutput(resultId: Long) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val summary = repo.verifyOutput(resultId)
            _snackbarMessage.value = summary
        }
    }

    fun deleteOutput(resultId: Long) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            repo.deleteOutput(resultId)
            refreshSystemStats()
            _snackbarMessage.value = tr("فایل خروجی حذف شد.", "Output file deleted.")
        }
    }

    fun createSigningKey(
        alias: String,
        password: String,
        commonName: String,
        organization: String,
        country: String,
        validityYears: Int,
    ) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            runCatching {
                repo.createSigningKey(alias, password, commonName, organization, country, validityYears)
            }.onSuccess {
                _snackbarMessage.value = tr("کلید امضای دیجیتال جدید ایجاد و فعال شد.", "New signing key created and set as default.")
            }.onFailure {
                _snackbarMessage.value = it.localizedMessage ?: tr("خطا در ایجاد کلید امضا", "Could not create the signing key.")
            }
        }
    }

    fun importSigningKey(uri: Uri, password: String) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            runCatching {
                repo.importSigningKey(uri, password)
            }.onSuccess {
                _snackbarMessage.value = tr("فایل Keystore با موفقیت وارد شد.", "Keystore imported successfully.")
            }.onFailure {
                _snackbarMessage.value = it.localizedMessage ?: tr("خطا در خواندن Keystore", "Could not read the keystore.")
            }
        }
    }

    fun setDefaultSigningKey(keyId: Long) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            repo.setDefaultSigningKey(keyId)
            _snackbarMessage.value = tr("کلید پیشفرض امضا تغییر کرد.", "Default signing key changed.")
        }
    }

    fun changeSigningPassword(keyId: Long, newPassword: String) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            runCatching {
                repo.changeSigningPassword(keyId, newPassword)
            }.onSuccess {
                _snackbarMessage.value = tr("رمز کلید امضا با موفقیت تغییر یافت.", "Signing key password changed.")
            }.onFailure {
                _snackbarMessage.value = tr("تغییر رمز ناموفق بود.", "Password change failed.")
            }
        }
    }

    fun verifySigningKey(keyId: Long) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val ok = repo.verifySigningKey(keyId)
            _snackbarMessage.value = if (ok) tr("گواهی امضا کاملاً معتبر و سالم است.", "The signing certificate is valid and intact.") else tr("اعتبارسنجی گواهی ناموفق بود.", "Certificate validation failed.")
        }
    }

    fun deleteSigningKey(keyId: Long) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            repo.deleteSigningKey(keyId)
            _snackbarMessage.value = tr("کلید امضا حذف شد.", "Signing key deleted.")
        }
    }

    fun clearBuildCache() {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val freed = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { repo.clearTempBuildCache() }
            refreshSystemStats()
            val lang = preferences.value.language
            val persianDigits = preferences.value.usePersianDigits
            val formatted = ValidationUtils.formatFileSize(freed, lang == AppLanguage.FA, persianDigits)
            _snackbarMessage.value = tr("حافظه موقت ساخت پاکسازی شد ($formatted).", "Build cache cleared ($formatted).")
        }
    }

    fun setLanguage(lang: AppLanguage) = viewModelScope.launch { repo.setLanguage(lang) }
    fun setThemeMode(mode: AppThemeMode) = viewModelScope.launch { repo.setThemeMode(mode) }
    fun setFontChoice(font: AppFontChoice) = viewModelScope.launch { repo.setFontChoice(font) }
    fun setCalendarType(cal: CalendarType) = viewModelScope.launch { repo.setCalendarType(cal) }
    fun setPersianDigits(enabled: Boolean) = viewModelScope.launch { repo.setPersianDigits(enabled) }
    fun setClearCacheOnExit(enabled: Boolean) = viewModelScope.launch { repo.setClearCacheOnExit(enabled) }
    fun setRedactLogs(enabled: Boolean) = viewModelScope.launch { repo.setRedactLogs(enabled) }
    fun setOnboardingCompleted(done: Boolean) = viewModelScope.launch { repo.setOnboardingCompleted(done) }
    suspend fun buildSupportReport(taskId: Long, isFa: Boolean): String = repo.buildSupportReport(taskId, isFa)
    fun observeBuildHistoryLogs(taskId: Long): Flow<List<BuildLogEntity>> = repo.observeBuildLogs(taskId)
}
package com.example.webviewengine

import android.content.Context
import android.net.ConnectivityManager
import android.net.Network
import android.net.NetworkCapabilities
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.Looper

/**
 * Observes device network availability and triggers main-thread reconnect callbacks
 * when connectivity is restored after an offline error.
 */
class WebConnectivityManager(private val context: Context) {

    private var networkCallback: ConnectivityManager.NetworkCallback? = null
    private val mainHandler = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())

    fun isNetworkAvailable(): Boolean = runCatching {
        val cm = context.getSystemService(Context.CONNECTIVITY_SERVICE) as? ConnectivityManager ?: return@runCatching true
        val active = cm.activeNetwork ?: return@runCatching false
        val caps = cm.getNetworkCapabilities(active) ?: return@runCatching false
        caps.hasCapability(NetworkCapabilities.NET_CAPABILITY_INTERNET)
    }.getOrDefault(true)

    fun startReconnectMonitor(onNetworkRestored: () -> Unit) {
        stopReconnectMonitor()
        runCatching {
            val cm = context.getSystemService(Context.CONNECTIVITY_SERVICE) as? ConnectivityManager ?: return
            val cb = object : ConnectivityManager.NetworkCallback() {
                override fun onAvailable(network: Network) {
                    mainHandler.post { onNetworkRestored() }
                }
            }
            cm.registerDefaultNetworkCallback(cb)
            networkCallback = cb
        }
    }

    fun stopReconnectMonitor() {
        runCatching {
            val cb = networkCallback ?: return
            val cm = context.getSystemService(Context.CONNECTIVITY_SERVICE) as? ConnectivityManager ?: return
            cm.unregisterNetworkCallback(cb)
        }
        networkCallback = null
        mainHandler.removeCallbacksAndMessages(null)
    }
}
package com.example.webviewengine

import android.webkit.CookieManager
import android.webkit.WebView

/**
 * Manages HTTP/HTTPS Cookies, SameSite & Third-Party Cookie policies,
 * cross-launch session persistence via flush(), and user-confirmed cookie clearing.
 * Never writes cookies or login session values into logs.
 */
object WebCookieManager {

    fun applyPolicy(webView: WebView, cookiesEnabled: Boolean, thirdPartyCookiesEnabled: Boolean) {
        runCatching {
            CookieManager.getInstance()?.apply {
                setAcceptCookie(cookiesEnabled)
                setAcceptThirdPartyCookies(webView, cookiesEnabled && thirdPartyCookiesEnabled)
            }
        }
    }

    fun hasSessionCookies(url: String): Boolean = runCatching {
        !CookieManager.getInstance()?.getCookie(url).isNullOrBlank()
    }.getOrDefault(false)

    fun flush() {
        runCatching { CookieManager.getInstance()?.flush() }
    }

    fun clearAll(onCleared: (() -> Unit)? = null) {
        runCatching {
            val cm = CookieManager.getInstance()
            cm?.removeAllCookies {
                runCatching { cm.flush() }
                onCleared?.invoke()
            }
        }
    }
}
package com.example.webviewengine

import android.app.DownloadManager
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.net.Uri
import android.os.Environment
import android.webkit.URLUtil
import androidx.core.content.FileProvider
import java.io.File
import java.util.Locale

enum class DownloadItemStatus(val titleFa: String) {
    PENDING("در صف دانلود"),
    RUNNING("در حال دانلود"),
    PAUSED("متوقف‌شده"),
    SUCCESSFUL("تکمیل شد"),
    FAILED("ناموفق"),
    CANCELLED("لغو شده"),
}

data class WebDownloadItem(
    val downloadId: Long,
    val url: String,
    val fileName: String,
    val mimeType: String,
    val totalBytes: Long = -1L,
    val downloadedBytes: Long = 0L,
    val progressPercent: Int = 0,
    val status: DownloadItemStatus = DownloadItemStatus.PENDING,
    val localUri: String? = null,
    val errorReason: String? = null,
)

/**
 * Real Android DownloadManager & SAF integration with:
 * - URL & MIME validation (blocks unsafe auto-executable payloads)
 * - Path-traversal-safe filename sanitization & duplicate filename avoidance
 * - Real progress polling, cancel support, and safe document viewing
 */
class WebDownloadManager(
    private val context: Context,
) {
    private val blockedExtensions = setOf("apk", "xapk", "apks", "dex", "jar", "so", "exe", "bat", "sh")

    fun validateAndSanitize(
        url: String,
        contentDisposition: String?,
        mimetype: String?,
    ): Triple<Uri, String, String>? {
        if (url.isBlank()) return null
        val uri = runCatching { Uri.parse(url) }.getOrNull() ?: return null
        val scheme = uri.scheme?.lowercase(Locale.US).orEmpty()
        if (scheme != "https" && scheme != "http") return null

        val guessed = URLUtil.guessFileName(url, contentDisposition, mimetype)
        val cleanName = guessed
            .replace("..", "_")
            .replace(Regex("[^a-zA-Z0-9._-]"), "_")
            .trim('_')
            .take(96)
            .ifBlank { "download.bin" }

        val ext = cleanName.substringAfterLast('.', "").lowercase(Locale.US)
        if (ext in blockedExtensions) return null

        val safeMime = mimetype?.takeIf { it.contains('/') } ?: when (ext) {
            "pdf" -> "application/pdf"
            "png" -> "image/png"
            "jpg", "jpeg" -> "image/jpeg"
            "webp" -> "image/webp"
            "mp4" -> "video/mp4"
            "mp3" -> "audio/mpeg"
            else -> "application/octet-stream"
        }

        return Triple(uri, makeUniqueFilename(cleanName), safeMime)
    }

    fun makeUniqueFilename(desiredName: String): String {
        val downloadsDir = Environment.getExternalStoragePublicDirectory(Environment.DIRECTORY_DOWNLOADS)
        if (downloadsDir == null || !downloadsDir.exists()) return desiredName
        if (!File(downloadsDir, desiredName).exists()) return desiredName

        val base = desiredName.substringBeforeLast('.', desiredName)
        val ext = desiredName.substringAfterLast('.', "")
        for (i in 1..99) {
            val candidate = if (ext.isNotEmpty()) "${base}_($i).$ext" else "${base}_($i)"
            if (!File(downloadsDir, candidate).exists()) return candidate
        }
        return "${System.currentTimeMillis()}_$desiredName"
    }

    fun handleDownload(
        url: String,
        contentDisposition: String?,
        mimetype: String?,
        contentLength: Long = -1L,
        enabled: Boolean,
        onExternalFallback: (Uri) -> Unit,
    ): WebDownloadItem? {
        if (!enabled) return null
        val (uri, uniqueName, safeMime) = validateAndSanitize(url, contentDisposition, mimetype) ?: return null

        val enqueuedId = runCatching {
            val dm = context.getSystemService(Context.DOWNLOAD_SERVICE) as? DownloadManager ?: return@runCatching -1L
            val request = DownloadManager.Request(uri).apply {
                setMimeType(safeMime)
                setTitle(uniqueName)
                setDescription("دانلود از وبینو")
                setNotificationVisibility(DownloadManager.Request.VISIBILITY_VISIBLE_NOTIFY_COMPLETED)
                // Without the session cookie, downloads from logged-in sites return a login page instead of the file.
                runCatching { android.webkit.CookieManager.getInstance().getCookie(uri.toString()) }
                    .getOrNull()
                    ?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
                    ?.let { addRequestHeader("Cookie", it) }
                runCatching { android.webkit.WebSettings.getDefaultUserAgent(context) }
                    .getOrNull()
                    ?.let { addRequestHeader("User-Agent", it) }
                setDestinationInExternalPublicDir(Environment.DIRECTORY_DOWNLOADS, uniqueName)
            }
            dm.enqueue(request)
        }.getOrDefault(-1L)

        if (enqueuedId < 0L) {
            onExternalFallback(uri)
            return null
        }

        return WebDownloadItem(
            downloadId = enqueuedId,
            url = url,
            fileName = uniqueName,
            mimeType = safeMime,
            totalBytes = contentLength,
            downloadedBytes = 0L,
            progressPercent = 0,
            status = DownloadItemStatus.RUNNING,
        )
    }

    fun refreshDownloadStatus(item: WebDownloadItem): WebDownloadItem {
        val dm = context.getSystemService(Context.DOWNLOAD_SERVICE) as? DownloadManager ?: return item
        val cursor = runCatching { dm.query(DownloadManager.Query().setFilterById(item.downloadId)) }.getOrNull()
            ?: return item
        cursor.use { c ->
            if (!c.moveToFirst()) return item
            val statusIdx = c.getColumnIndex(DownloadManager.COLUMN_STATUS)
            val dlIdx = c.getColumnIndex(DownloadManager.COLUMN_BYTES_DOWNLOADED_SO_FAR)
            val totalIdx = c.getColumnIndex(DownloadManager.COLUMN_TOTAL_SIZE_BYTES)
            val uriIdx = c.getColumnIndex(DownloadManager.COLUMN_LOCAL_URI)

            val rawStatus = if (statusIdx >= 0) c.getInt(statusIdx) else DownloadManager.STATUS_RUNNING
            val dlBytes = if (dlIdx >= 0) c.getLong(dlIdx) else item.downloadedBytes
            val total = if (totalIdx >= 0) c.getLong(totalIdx) else item.totalBytes
            val localUri = if (uriIdx >= 0) c.getString(uriIdx) else item.localUri

            val mappedStatus = when (rawStatus) {
                DownloadManager.STATUS_PENDING -> DownloadItemStatus.PENDING
                DownloadManager.STATUS_RUNNING -> DownloadItemStatus.RUNNING
                DownloadManager.STATUS_PAUSED -> DownloadItemStatus.PAUSED
                DownloadManager.STATUS_SUCCESSFUL -> DownloadItemStatus.SUCCESSFUL
                DownloadManager.STATUS_FAILED -> DownloadItemStatus.FAILED
                else -> item.status
            }
            val pct = when {
                mappedStatus == DownloadItemStatus.SUCCESSFUL -> 100
                total > 0L -> ((dlBytes * 100L) / total).toInt().coerceIn(0, 100)
                else -> item.progressPercent
            }
            return item.copy(
                downloadedBytes = dlBytes,
                totalBytes = total,
                progressPercent = pct,
                status = mappedStatus,
                localUri = localUri,
                errorReason = if (mappedStatus == DownloadItemStatus.FAILED) "خطا در دریافت فایل از سرور." else null,
            )
        }
    }

    fun cancelDownload(downloadId: Long): Boolean = runCatching {
        val dm = context.getSystemService(Context.DOWNLOAD_SERVICE) as? DownloadManager ?: return false
        dm.remove(downloadId) > 0
    }.getOrDefault(false)

    fun openDownloadedFile(item: WebDownloadItem): Boolean {
        val rawUri = item.localUri ?: return false
        val parsed = runCatching { Uri.parse(rawUri) }.getOrNull() ?: return false
        val viewUri = if (parsed.scheme == "file" && parsed.path != null) {
            runCatching {
                FileProvider.getUriForFile(context, "${context.packageName}.fileprovider", File(parsed.path!!))
            }.getOrDefault(parsed)
        } else {
            parsed
        }
        return runCatching {
            val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW).apply {
                setDataAndType(viewUri, item.mimeType)
                addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK or Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION)
            }
            context.startActivity(Intent.createChooser(intent, item.fileName).addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK))
            true
        }.getOrDefault(false)
    }
}
package com.example.webviewengine

import android.content.Context
import android.net.http.SslError
import android.webkit.SslErrorHandler
import com.example.domain.model.SslPolicy

enum class WebErrorCategory(val titleFa: String) {
    NO_INTERNET("عدم اتصال به اینترنت"),
    DNS_FAILURE("خطا در شناسایی دامنه (DNS)"),
    TIMEOUT("اتمام زمان پاسخگویی سرور"),
    HTTP_NOT_FOUND("صفحه مورد نظر پیدا نشد (۴۰۴)"),
    HTTP_SERVER_ERROR("خطای داخلی سرور وب‌سایت"),
    HTTP_ERROR("خطای پروتکل HTTP"),
    SSL_ERROR("خطای امنیتی گواهی SSL"),
    FILE_LOAD_ERROR("خطا در بارگذاری فایل یا منبع"),
    WEBVIEW_ERROR("خطای موتور نمایش صفحه"),
}

data class WebErrorInfo(
    val category: WebErrorCategory,
    val titleFa: String,
    val messageFa: String,
    val technicalDetail: String,
    val canRetry: Boolean = true,
)

sealed interface SslResolution {
    data object CancelledStrictly : SslResolution
    data class RequireExplicitUserWarning(val url: String) : SslResolution
}

/**
 * Classifies WebView network, DNS, Timeout, HTTP, File, and SSL failures into
 * clear Persian diagnostics without leaking sensitive URL tokens or cookies.
 */
class WebErrorHandler(private val context: Context) {

    private val connectivityManager = WebConnectivityManager(context)
    private var pendingSslHandler: SslErrorHandler? = null

    fun classifyNetworkError(errorCode: Int, rawDescription: String?, failingUrl: String?): WebErrorInfo {
        val desc = rawDescription.orEmpty()
        val offline = !connectivityManager.isNetworkAvailable()
        val safeUrl = WebSecurityManager.sanitizeUrlForLog(failingUrl)

        return when {
            desc.contains("INTERNET_DISCONNECTED", ignoreCase = true) -> WebErrorInfo(
                category = WebErrorCategory.NO_INTERNET,
                titleFa = WebErrorCategory.NO_INTERNET.titleFa,
                messageFa = "اتصال اینترنت دستگاه برقرار نیست. پس از اتصال به Wi-Fi یا داده همراه، صفحه به‌صورت خودکار یا با دکمه تلاش مجدد بارگذاری می‌شود.",
                technicalDetail = "OFFLINE ($errorCode) • $safeUrl",
            )
            desc.contains("NAME_NOT_RESOLVED", ignoreCase = true) || errorCode == -2 -> WebErrorInfo(
                category = WebErrorCategory.DNS_FAILURE,
                titleFa = WebErrorCategory.DNS_FAILURE.titleFa,
                messageFa = "آدرس سرور وب‌سایت پیدا نشد. نشانی سایت یا DNS شبکه خود را بررسی کنید.",
                technicalDetail = "DNS ($errorCode) • $safeUrl",
            )
            desc.contains("TIMED_OUT", ignoreCase = true) || errorCode == -8 -> WebErrorInfo(
                category = WebErrorCategory.TIMEOUT,
                titleFa = WebErrorCategory.TIMEOUT.titleFa,
                messageFa = "پاسخگویی سرور بیش از حد طول کشید. کیفیت اینترنت را بررسی و دوباره تلاش کنید.",
                technicalDetail = "TIMEOUT ($errorCode) • $safeUrl",
            )
            desc.contains("FILE", ignoreCase = true) || errorCode == -13 || errorCode == -14 -> WebErrorInfo(
                category = WebErrorCategory.FILE_LOAD_ERROR,
                titleFa = WebErrorCategory.FILE_LOAD_ERROR.titleFa,
                messageFa = "بارگذاری فایل یا منبع درخواستی امکان‌پذیر نیست.",
                technicalDetail = "FILE ($errorCode) • $safeUrl",
            )
            offline -> WebErrorInfo(
                category = WebErrorCategory.NO_INTERNET,
                titleFa = WebErrorCategory.NO_INTERNET.titleFa,
                messageFa = "اتصال اینترنت دستگاه برقرار نیست. پس از اتصال به Wi-Fi یا داده همراه، صفحه به‌صورت خودکار یا با دکمه تلاش مجدد بارگذاری می‌شود.",
                technicalDetail = "OFFLINE ($errorCode) • $safeUrl",
            )
            else -> WebErrorInfo(
                category = WebErrorCategory.WEBVIEW_ERROR,
                titleFa = WebErrorCategory.WEBVIEW_ERROR.titleFa,
                messageFa = "بارگذاری صفحه با خطا مواجه شد. می‌توانید دوباره تلاش کنید یا به صفحه قبل برگردید.",
                technicalDetail = "${desc.ifBlank { "WEBVIEW_ERR" }} ($errorCode) • $safeUrl",
            )
        }
    }

    fun classifyHttpError(statusCode: Int, failingUrl: String?): WebErrorInfo {
        val safeUrl = WebSecurityManager.sanitizeUrlForLog(failingUrl)
        return when {
            statusCode == 404 -> WebErrorInfo(
                category = WebErrorCategory.HTTP_NOT_FOUND,
                titleFa = WebErrorCategory.HTTP_NOT_FOUND.titleFa,
                messageFa = "صفحه یا فایل درخواستی روی سرور وب‌سایت یافت نشد.",
                technicalDetail = "HTTP 404 • $safeUrl",
            )
            statusCode >= 500 -> WebErrorInfo(
                category = WebErrorCategory.HTTP_SERVER_ERROR,
                titleFa = WebErrorCategory.HTTP_SERVER_ERROR.titleFa,
                messageFa = "سرور وب‌سایت موقتاً با خطای داخلی ($statusCode) مواجه شده است.",
                technicalDetail = "HTTP $statusCode • $safeUrl",
            )
            else -> WebErrorInfo(
                category = WebErrorCategory.HTTP_ERROR,
                titleFa = WebErrorCategory.HTTP_ERROR.titleFa,
                messageFa = "درخواست وب‌سایت با کد وضعیت HTTP $statusCode رد شد.",
                technicalDetail = "HTTP $statusCode • $safeUrl",
            )
        }
    }

    fun formatNetworkError(rawDescription: String?): String =
        classifyNetworkError(-1, rawDescription, null).messageFa

    fun handleSslError(
        handler: SslErrorHandler?,
        error: SslError?,
        fallbackUrl: String,
        policy: SslPolicy,
    ): SslResolution? {
        if (handler == null) return null
        if (policy == SslPolicy.STRICT_BLOCK) {
            handler.cancel()
            pendingSslHandler = null
            return SslResolution.CancelledStrictly
        }
        // Never silently proceed on SSL errors
        pendingSslHandler = handler
        return SslResolution.RequireExplicitUserWarning(error?.url ?: fallbackUrl)
    }

    fun resolvePendingSsl(proceed: Boolean) {
        if (proceed) pendingSslHandler?.proceed() else pendingSslHandler?.cancel()
        pendingSslHandler = null
    }

    fun isNetworkAvailable(): Boolean = connectivityManager.isNetworkAvailable()

    fun startReconnectMonitor(onNetworkRestored: () -> Unit) =
        connectivityManager.startReconnectMonitor(onNetworkRestored)

    fun stopReconnectMonitor() =
        connectivityManager.stopReconnectMonitor()
}
package com.example.webviewengine

import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.net.Uri
import android.os.Build
import java.util.Locale

/**
 * Safely dispatches external links and native intents (tel, mailto, smsto, WhatsApp,
 * Telegram, Maps, validated intent://, and system browser) without requesting prohibited
 * SMS/Call/Package permissions.
 */
class WebExternalLinkHandler(
    private val context: Context,
) {
    fun isNativeAppUri(uri: Uri): Boolean {
        val scheme = uri.scheme?.lowercase(Locale.US).orEmpty()
        val host = uri.host?.lowercase(Locale.US).orEmpty()
        return scheme in setOf("tel", "mailto", "sms", "smsto", "geo", "whatsapp", "tg", "intent") ||
            host.contains("wa.me") ||
            host.contains("api.whatsapp.com") ||
            host.contains("t.me") ||
            host.contains("telegram.me") ||
            host.contains("maps.google") ||
            host.contains("neshan.org") ||
            host.contains("balad.ir")
    }

    fun openExternal(uri: Uri): Boolean {
        val scheme = uri.scheme?.lowercase(Locale.US).orEmpty()
        if (WebSecurityManager.isDangerousScheme(scheme)) return false

        if (scheme == "intent") {
            val (parsedIntent, fallbackUrl) = WebSecurityManager.parseSafeIntentUri(uri.toString())
            if (parsedIntent != null) {
                val started = runCatching {
                    context.startActivity(parsedIntent)
                    true
                }.getOrDefault(false)
                if (started) return true
            }
            if (!fallbackUrl.isNullOrBlank()) {
                return openExternal(Uri.parse(fallbackUrl))
            }
            return false
        }

        val intent = when (scheme) {
            "tel" -> Intent(Intent.ACTION_DIAL, uri)
            "mailto" -> Intent(Intent.ACTION_SENDTO, uri)
            "sms", "smsto" -> Intent(Intent.ACTION_SENDTO, uri)
            else -> Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, uri)
        }.apply {
            addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
        }

        return runCatching {
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.R || intent.resolveActivity(context.packageManager) != null) {
                context.startActivity(intent)
                true
            } else {
                false
            }
        }.getOrDefault(false)
    }

    fun shareText(text: String, title: String): Boolean = runCatching {
        val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_SEND).apply {
            type = "text/plain"
            putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_SUBJECT, title)
            putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_TEXT, text)
            addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
        }
        val chooser = Intent.createChooser(intent, title).apply {
            addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
        }
        context.startActivity(chooser)
        true
    }.getOrDefault(false)
}
package com.example.webviewengine

import android.view.View
import android.webkit.WebChromeClient

/**
 * Manages HTML5 fullscreen video/media custom view enter, exit, and Back-button cleanup.
 */
class WebFullscreenManager(
    private val onShowCustomVideoView: (View?) -> Unit,
) {
    private var activeCallback: WebChromeClient.CustomViewCallback? = null
    private var isFullscreenActive: Boolean = false

    fun enterFullscreen(
        customView: View?,
        callback: WebChromeClient.CustomViewCallback?,
        allowed: Boolean,
    ): Boolean {
        if (!allowed || customView == null) {
            runCatching { callback?.onCustomViewHidden() }
            return false
        }
        activeCallback = callback
        isFullscreenActive = true
        onShowCustomVideoView(customView)
        return true
    }

    fun exitFullscreen(): Boolean {
        val wasActive = isFullscreenActive
        isFullscreenActive = false
        onShowCustomVideoView(null)
        runCatching { activeCallback?.onCustomViewHidden() }
        activeCallback = null
        return wasActive
    }

    fun isInFullscreen(): Boolean = isFullscreenActive
}
package com.example.webviewengine

import android.content.Context
import android.net.Uri
import com.example.domain.model.ExternalLinkMode
import java.util.Locale

sealed interface NavigationDecision {
    data object AllowInWebView : NavigationDecision
    data class OpenExternal(val uri: Uri, val categoryLabelFa: String = "لینک خارجی") : NavigationDecision
    data class ConfirmExternal(val uri: Uri, val reasonFa: String) : NavigationDecision
    data class BlockWithNotice(val reason: String) : NavigationDecision
}

/**
 * Evaluates URL navigation rules, HTTPS-Only enforcement, domain allowlists/blocklists,
 * special native schemes (tel, mailto, smsto, WhatsApp, Telegram, Maps, validated intent://),
 * Deep Links / App Links, and Iranian payment gateways.
 */
class WebNavigationHandler(
    private val context: Context,
) {
    private val externalLinkHandler = WebExternalLinkHandler(context)
    private var allowedOriginHost: String = ""
    private var customAllowlist: Set<String> = emptySet()
    private var customBlocklist: Set<String> = emptySet()

    fun updateRules(initialUrl: String, navigationRulesRaw: String = "") {
        allowedOriginHost = runCatching { Uri.parse(initialUrl).host?.lowercase(Locale.US) }
            .getOrNull()
            .orEmpty()
        val lines = navigationRulesRaw.split(',', '\n', ';')
            .map { it.trim().lowercase(Locale.US) }
            .filter { it.isNotEmpty() && !it.startsWith("#") }
        customBlocklist = lines.filter { it.startsWith("!") }
            .map { it.removePrefix("!").trim() }
            .filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
            .toSet()
        customAllowlist = lines.filter { !it.startsWith("!") }.toSet()
    }

    fun getAllowedOriginHost(): String = allowedOriginHost

    fun evaluate(uri: Uri, config: WebViewEngineConfig): NavigationDecision {
        val scheme = uri.scheme?.lowercase(Locale.US).orEmpty()
        val host = uri.host?.lowercase(Locale.US).orEmpty()

        if (WebSecurityManager.isDangerousScheme(scheme)) {
            return NavigationDecision.BlockWithNotice("طرح URI غیرمجاز ($scheme:) مسدود شد.")
        }

        if (scheme == "tel") return NavigationDecision.OpenExternal(uri, "تماس تلفنی")
        if (scheme == "mailto") return NavigationDecision.OpenExternal(uri, "ارسال ایمیل")
        if (scheme == "sms" || scheme == "smsto") return NavigationDecision.OpenExternal(uri, "پیامک")
        if (scheme == "geo" || host.contains("maps.google") || host.contains("neshan.org") || host.contains("balad.ir")) {
            return NavigationDecision.OpenExternal(uri, "مسیریاب و نقشه")
        }
        if (scheme == "whatsapp" || host.contains("wa.me") || host.contains("api.whatsapp.com")) {
            return NavigationDecision.OpenExternal(uri, "واتساپ")
        }
        if (scheme == "tg" || host.contains("t.me") || host.contains("telegram.me")) {
            return NavigationDecision.OpenExternal(uri, "تلگرام")
        }
        if (scheme == "intent") {
            val (parsed, fallback) = WebSecurityManager.parseSafeIntentUri(uri.toString())
            if (parsed == null && fallback == null) {
                return NavigationDecision.BlockWithNotice("ساختار لینک intent:// معتبر نیست و مسدود شد.")
            }
            return NavigationDecision.ConfirmExternal(uri, "این لینک درخواست باز کردن یک اپلیکیشن دیگر در گوشی را دارد. اجازه می‌دهید؟")
        }

        if (config.httpsOnly && scheme == "http") {
            return NavigationDecision.BlockWithNotice("اتصال ناامن HTTP به دلیل فعال بودن HTTPS-Only مسدود شد.")
        }

        if (scheme != "https" && scheme != "http") {
            return NavigationDecision.ConfirmExternal(
                uri = uri,
                reasonFa = "طرح ناشناس ($scheme:) درخواست اجرای برنامه خارجی دارد. ادامه می‌دهید؟",
            )
        }

        val isBlocked = customBlocklist.any { rule -> host == rule || host.endsWith(".$rule") }
        if (isBlocked) {
            return NavigationDecision.BlockWithNotice("دسترسی به دامنه $host طبق قوانین پروژه مسدود شده است.")
        }

        val isSameHost = allowedOriginHost.isNotEmpty() &&
            (host == allowedOriginHost || host.endsWith(".$allowedOriginHost"))
        val isAllowlisted = customAllowlist.any { rule -> host == rule || host.endsWith(".$rule") }
        val isPaymentGateway = isPaymentGatewayHost(host)

        if (isSameHost || isAllowlisted || isPaymentGateway) {
            return NavigationDecision.AllowInWebView
        }

        return when (config.externalLinkMode) {
            ExternalLinkMode.OPEN_IN_SYSTEM_BROWSER -> NavigationDecision.OpenExternal(uri, "مرورگر خارجی")
            ExternalLinkMode.KEEP_IN_APP -> NavigationDecision.AllowInWebView
            ExternalLinkMode.ASK_OR_ALLOWLIST -> NavigationDecision.ConfirmExternal(
                uri = uri,
                reasonFa = "لینک به دامنه خارجی ($host) اشاره دارد. در مرورگر خارجی باز شود یا همینجا؟",
            )
        }
    }

    fun isOriginTrusted(url: String?, requireHttps: Boolean = true): Boolean =
        WebSecurityManager.isOriginTrusted(url, allowedOriginHost, requireHttps)

    fun openExternal(uri: Uri): Boolean = externalLinkHandler.openExternal(uri)

    fun shareText(text: String, title: String): Boolean = externalLinkHandler.shareText(text, title)

    private fun isPaymentGatewayHost(host: String): Boolean =
        host.contains("shaparak.ir") ||
            host.contains("zarinpal.com") ||
            host.contains("idpay.ir") ||
            host.contains("behpardakht.com") ||
            host.contains("sadadpsp.ir") ||
            host.contains("pep.co.ir")
}
package com.example.webviewengine

import android.Manifest
import android.content.Context
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.location.LocationManager
import android.os.Build
import android.webkit.GeolocationPermissions
import android.webkit.PermissionRequest
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat

data class PermissionRationaleRequest(
    val titleFa: String,
    val messageFa: String,
    val origin: String,
    val androidPermissions: List<String>,
    val onConfirmGrant: () -> Unit,
    val onUserDecline: () -> Unit,
)

/**
 * Coordinates Android OS runtime permissions and WebView permission prompts
 * (Camera, Microphone, WebRTC, GPS Geolocation) with:
 * - Origin verification
 * - Device Location service availability check
 * - Mandatory Persian rationale explanation before OS dialog
 * - Graceful deny on rejection or mid-session revocation
 */
class WebPermissionManager(
    private val context: Context,
    private val onRationaleRequired: (PermissionRationaleRequest) -> Unit,
    private val onOsPermissionPrompt: (List<String>, (Boolean) -> Unit) -> Unit,
) {
    private var activeWebRequest: PermissionRequest? = null

    fun hasPermission(permission: String): Boolean =
        ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(context, permission) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED

    fun isLocationServiceEnabled(): Boolean = runCatching {
        val lm = context.getSystemService(Context.LOCATION_SERVICE) as? LocationManager ?: return@runCatching false
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.P) {
            lm.isLocationEnabled
        } else {
            lm.isProviderEnabled(LocationManager.GPS_PROVIDER) || lm.isProviderEnabled(LocationManager.NETWORK_PROVIDER)
        }
    }.getOrDefault(false)

    fun handleGeolocationPrompt(
        origin: String?,
        callback: GeolocationPermissions.Callback?,
        config: WebViewEngineConfig,
        allowedOriginHost: String,
        onStatusNotice: (String) -> Unit = {},
    ) {
        if (!config.geolocationAllowed || callback == null) {
            callback?.invoke(origin, false, false)
            return
        }
        val cleanOrigin = origin.orEmpty()
        val trusted = allowedOriginHost.isBlank() ||
            WebSecurityManager.isOriginTrusted(cleanOrigin, allowedOriginHost, config.httpsOnly)
        if (!trusted) {
            callback.invoke(origin, false, false)
            onStatusNotice("درخواست موقعیت مکانی از دامنه غیرمعتبر مسدود شد.")
            return
        }
        if (!isLocationServiceEnabled()) {
            callback.invoke(origin, false, false)
            onStatusNotice("سرویس مکان‌یاب (GPS) گوشی خاموش است.")
            return
        }
        if (hasPermission(Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION) || hasPermission(Manifest.permission.ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION)) {
            callback.invoke(origin, true, false)
            return
        }

        val perms = listOf(Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION, Manifest.permission.ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION)
        onRationaleRequired(
            PermissionRationaleRequest(
                titleFa = "دسترسی به موقعیت مکانی (GPS)",
                messageFa = "وب‌سایت «$cleanOrigin» برای نمایش نقشه یا ارسال خدمات محلی درخواست دسترسی موقت به موقعیت مکانی دارد.",
                origin = cleanOrigin,
                androidPermissions = perms,
                onConfirmGrant = {
                    onOsPermissionPrompt(perms) { granted ->
                        callback.invoke(origin, granted, false)
                        if (!granted) onStatusNotice("دسترسی موقعیت مکانی توسط کاربر رد شد.")
                    }
                },
                onUserDecline = {
                    callback.invoke(origin, false, false)
                },
            )
        )
    }

    fun handleWebPermissionRequest(
        request: PermissionRequest?,
        config: WebViewEngineConfig,
        allowedOriginHost: String,
        onStatusNotice: (String) -> Unit = {},
    ) {
        if (request == null) return
        val originStr = request.origin?.toString().orEmpty()
        val trusted = allowedOriginHost.isBlank() ||
            WebSecurityManager.isOriginTrusted(originStr, allowedOriginHost, config.httpsOnly)
        if (!trusted) {
            request.deny()
            onStatusNotice("درخواست دسترسی سخت‌افزاری از مبدأ غیرمجاز رد شد.")
            return
        }

        val allowedResources = request.resources.filter { res ->
            (res == PermissionRequest.RESOURCE_VIDEO_CAPTURE && config.cameraAllowed) ||
                (res == PermissionRequest.RESOURCE_AUDIO_CAPTURE && (config.microphoneAllowed || config.webRtcAllowed))
        }
        if (allowedResources.isEmpty()) {
            request.deny()
            onStatusNotice("دسترسی دوربین یا میکروفون در تنظیمات این پروژه غیرفعال است.")
            return
        }

        val neededOsPerms = buildList {
            if (PermissionRequest.RESOURCE_VIDEO_CAPTURE in allowedResources && !hasPermission(Manifest.permission.CAMERA)) {
                add(Manifest.permission.CAMERA)
            }
            if (PermissionRequest.RESOURCE_AUDIO_CAPTURE in allowedResources && !hasPermission(Manifest.permission.RECORD_AUDIO)) {
                add(Manifest.permission.RECORD_AUDIO)
            }
        }

        if (neededOsPerms.isEmpty()) {
            request.grant(allowedResources.toTypedArray())
            return
        }

        activeWebRequest = request
        val featureDesc = buildList {
            if (Manifest.permission.CAMERA in neededOsPerms) add("دوربین")
            if (Manifest.permission.RECORD_AUDIO in neededOsPerms) add("میکروفون")
        }.joinToString(" و ")

        onRationaleRequired(
            PermissionRationaleRequest(
                titleFa = "درخواست دسترسی به $featureDesc",
                messageFa = "وب‌سایت «$originStr» برای تماس تصویری، ضبط صدا یا ثبت تصویر به $featureDesc نیاز دارد. این دسترسی فقط در همین نشست استفاده می‌شود.",
                origin = originStr,
                androidPermissions = neededOsPerms,
                onConfirmGrant = {
                    onOsPermissionPrompt(neededOsPerms) { granted ->
                        if (granted && activeWebRequest == request) {
                            request.grant(allowedResources.toTypedArray())
                        } else {
                            runCatching { request.deny() }
                            onStatusNotice("مجوز $featureDesc صادر نشد و وب‌سایت بدون خطا ادامه می‌دهد.")
                        }
                        if (activeWebRequest == request) activeWebRequest = null
                    }
                },
                onUserDecline = {
                    runCatching { request.deny() }
                    if (activeWebRequest == request) activeWebRequest = null
                },
            )
        )
    }

    fun handleWebPermissionCanceled(request: PermissionRequest?) {
        if (request == activeWebRequest) {
            activeWebRequest = null
        }
    }

    fun requestCameraForUpload(
        origin: String,
        onResult: (Boolean) -> Unit,
    ) {
        if (hasPermission(Manifest.permission.CAMERA)) {
            onResult(true)
            return
        }
        onRationaleRequired(
            PermissionRationaleRequest(
                titleFa = "استفاده از دوربین برای ارسال تصویر",
                messageFa = "برای ثبت عکس جدید جهت بارگذاری در فرم وب‌سایت، مجوز دوربین مورد نیاز است.",
                origin = origin,
                androidPermissions = listOf(Manifest.permission.CAMERA),
                onConfirmGrant = {
                    onOsPermissionPrompt(listOf(Manifest.permission.CAMERA), onResult)
                },
                onUserDecline = { onResult(false) },
            )
        )
    }
}
package com.example.webviewengine

import android.content.Context
import android.os.Message
import android.webkit.WebResourceRequest
import android.webkit.WebView
import android.webkit.WebViewClient
import com.example.domain.model.PopupBehaviorMode

sealed interface PopupAction {
    data object Blocked : PopupAction
    data class OpenInSameWebView(val resultMsg: Message) : PopupAction
    data class OpenInternalModal(val popupWebView: WebView) : PopupAction
    data class ResolveInExternalBrowser(val tempTransportView: WebView) : PopupAction
}

/**
 * Coordinates window.open / target="_blank" / onCreateWindow behavior according to the
 * project's PopupBehaviorMode while blocking ungestured unwanted pop-ups.
 */
class WebPopupHandler(
    private val context: Context,
) {
    fun handleCreateWindow(
        mainWebView: WebView,
        config: WebViewEngineConfig,
        isUserGesture: Boolean,
        resultMsg: Message?,
        onPopupUrlForExternal: (String) -> Unit,
        onShowInternalPopup: (WebView) -> Unit,
        onCloseInternalPopup: () -> Unit,
    ): Boolean {
        if (resultMsg == null) return false
        if (!config.multipleWindowsAllowed) return false
        if (!isUserGesture && !config.popupsAllowed) return false

        val transport = resultMsg.obj as? WebView.WebViewTransport ?: return false

        return when (config.popupBehavior) {
            PopupBehaviorMode.SAME_PAGE -> {
                transport.webView = mainWebView
                resultMsg.sendToTarget()
                true
            }
            PopupBehaviorMode.INTERNAL_WINDOW -> {
                val popupView = WebView(context).also { wv ->
                    WebViewFactory.configure(wv, config)
                    WebCookieManager.applyPolicy(wv, config.cookiesEnabled, config.thirdPartyCookiesEnabled)
                    wv.webViewClient = object : WebViewClient() {
                        override fun shouldOverrideUrlLoading(view: WebView?, request: WebResourceRequest?): Boolean {
                            val u = request?.url ?: return false
                            val scheme = u.scheme?.lowercase().orEmpty()
                            if (WebSecurityManager.isDangerousScheme(scheme)) return true
                            if (config.httpsOnly && scheme == "http") return true
                            return false
                        }
                    }
                    wv.webChromeClient = object : android.webkit.WebChromeClient() {
                        override fun onCloseWindow(window: WebView?) {
                            onCloseInternalPopup()
                        }
                    }
                }
                transport.webView = popupView
                resultMsg.sendToTarget()
                onShowInternalPopup(popupView)
                true
            }
            PopupBehaviorMode.EXTERNAL_BROWSER -> {
                val bridgeView = WebView(context).apply {
                    webViewClient = object : WebViewClient() {
                        override fun shouldOverrideUrlLoading(view: WebView?, request: WebResourceRequest?): Boolean {
                            request?.url?.toString()?.let(onPopupUrlForExternal)
                            runCatching { view?.destroy() }
                            return true
                        }
                    }
                }
                transport.webView = bridgeView
                resultMsg.sendToTarget()
                true
            }
        }
    }
}
package com.example.webviewengine

import android.content.Intent
import android.net.Uri
import android.webkit.WebSettings
import java.util.Locale

/**
 * Central security gatekeeper for WEBINO's WebView engine:
 * - Blocks dangerous URI schemes (javascript:, file:, data:, vbscript:)
 * - Validates Origin trust before granting JS bridge or sensitive capabilities
 * - Sanitizes intent:// URIs against arbitrary component/selector hijacking
 * - Redacts sensitive query params or tokens before logging technical errors
 */
object WebSecurityManager {

    private val blockedSchemes = setOf("javascript", "file", "data", "vbscript", "blob")
    private val sensitiveQueryKeys = setOf("token", "password", "passwd", "pass", "secret", "auth", "code", "session", "cookie", "key")

    fun isDangerousScheme(scheme: String?): Boolean =
        scheme?.lowercase(Locale.US) in blockedSchemes

    fun isOriginTrusted(url: String?, allowedOriginHost: String, requireHttps: Boolean): Boolean {
        if (url.isNullOrBlank() || allowedOriginHost.isBlank()) return false
        val uri = runCatching { Uri.parse(url) }.getOrNull() ?: return false
        val scheme = uri.scheme?.lowercase(Locale.US).orEmpty()
        if (requireHttps && scheme != "https") return false
        if (scheme != "https" && scheme != "http") return false
        val host = uri.host?.lowercase(Locale.US).orEmpty()
        return host == allowedOriginHost || host.endsWith(".$allowedOriginHost")
    }

    fun isHttpAllowed(scheme: String, httpsOnly: Boolean): Boolean =
        !(httpsOnly && scheme.equals("http", ignoreCase = true))

    fun enforceSafeSettings(settings: WebSettings) {
        settings.allowFileAccess = false
        @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
        settings.allowFileAccessFromFileURLs = false
        @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
        settings.allowUniversalAccessFromFileURLs = false
    }

    /**
     * Parses an Android intent:// URI safely, removing embedded selectors/explicit dangerous
     * components and validating an optional browser_fallback_url.
     */
    fun parseSafeIntentUri(rawUri: String): Pair<Intent?, String?> {
        val parsed = runCatching { Intent.parseUri(rawUri, Intent.URI_INTENT_SCHEME) }.getOrNull()
            ?: return null to null

        val fallback = parsed.getStringExtra("browser_fallback_url")?.takeIf { fb ->
            val u = runCatching { Uri.parse(fb) }.getOrNull()
            val s = u?.scheme?.lowercase(Locale.US)
            s == "https" || s == "http"
        }

        // Prevent targeted internal component hijacking and explicit selector abuse
        parsed.component = null
        parsed.selector = null
        // A hostile page must not be able to smuggle URI-grant flags into the intent (intent redirection).
        parsed.flags = parsed.flags and (
            Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION or
                Intent.FLAG_GRANT_WRITE_URI_PERMISSION or
                Intent.FLAG_GRANT_PERSISTABLE_URI_PERMISSION or
                Intent.FLAG_GRANT_PREFIX_URI_PERMISSION
            ).inv()
        parsed.addCategory(Intent.CATEGORY_BROWSABLE)
        parsed.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
        return parsed to fallback
    }

    fun sanitizeUrlForLog(rawUrl: String?): String {
        if (rawUrl.isNullOrBlank()) return ""
        val uri = runCatching { Uri.parse(rawUrl) }.getOrNull() ?: return rawUrl.take(120)
        val hostPart = listOfNotNull(uri.scheme, uri.host).joinToString("://")
        val pathPart = uri.path.orEmpty()
        val query = uri.query
        if (query.isNullOrBlank()) return "$hostPart$pathPart"
        val safeParams = query.split("&").map { pair ->
            val key = pair.substringBefore("=").lowercase(Locale.US)
            if (sensitiveQueryKeys.any { key.contains(it) }) {
                "${pair.substringBefore("=")}=***"
            } else {
                pair
            }
        }.joinToString("&")
        return "$hostPart$pathPart?$safeParams"
    }
}
package com.example.webviewengine

import android.webkit.WebStorage
import android.webkit.WebView
import com.example.domain.model.CacheModeOption

/**
 * Manages DOM Storage, LocalStorage, SessionStorage, IndexedDB/WebSQL availability,
 * cache mode policy, and user-confirmed clearing of cached site data.
 */
object WebStorageManager {

    fun applyStorageAndCache(
        webView: WebView,
        domStorageEnabled: Boolean,
        webSqlEnabled: Boolean,
        cacheMode: CacheModeOption,
    ) {
        webView.settings.apply {
            this.domStorageEnabled = domStorageEnabled
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            this.databaseEnabled = webSqlEnabled || domStorageEnabled
            this.cacheMode = cacheMode.androidConst
        }
    }

    fun clearCacheOnly(webView: WebView?, includeDiskFiles: Boolean = true) {
        runCatching { webView?.clearCache(includeDiskFiles) }
    }

    fun clearAllSiteData(webView: WebView?) {
        runCatching {
            webView?.clearCache(true)
            webView?.clearFormData()
            webView?.clearHistory()
            WebStorage.getInstance().deleteAllData()
            WebCookieManager.clearAll()
        }
    }
}
package com.example.webviewengine

import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.net.Uri
import android.provider.MediaStore
import android.webkit.ValueCallback
import android.webkit.WebChromeClient
import androidx.core.content.FileProvider
import java.io.File

data class UploadRequestOptions(
    val acceptMimeTypes: List<String>,
    val allowMultiple: Boolean,
    val captureEnabled: Boolean,
    val chooserIntent: Intent,
)

/**
 * Coordinates WebView HTML5 file chooser upload requests:
 * - Single & multiple file selection
 * - Images, PDF, and specific MIME type filters
 * - Optional camera photo capture via FileProvider
 * - Clean cancellation & Activity rotation safety
 */
class WebUploadManager(
    private val onFileChooserRequest: (UploadRequestOptions, ValueCallback<Array<Uri>>) -> Unit,
) {
    private var pendingCallback: ValueCallback<Array<Uri>>? = null
    private var pendingCameraOutputUri: Uri? = null

    fun hasPendingChooser(): Boolean = pendingCallback != null

    fun startChooser(
        filePathCallback: ValueCallback<Array<Uri>>?,
        params: WebChromeClient.FileChooserParams?,
        enabled: Boolean,
    ): Boolean {
        if (!enabled || filePathCallback == null || params == null) {
            filePathCallback?.onReceiveValue(null)
            return false
        }
        pendingCallback?.onReceiveValue(null)
        pendingCallback = filePathCallback
        pendingCameraOutputUri = null

        return runCatching {
            val rawAccept = params.acceptTypes
                ?.flatMap { it.split(',') }
                ?.map { it.trim() }
                ?.filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
                .orEmpty()
            val allowMultiple = params.mode == WebChromeClient.FileChooserParams.MODE_OPEN_MULTIPLE
            val primaryMime = when {
                rawAccept.size == 1 -> normalizeMimeType(rawAccept.first())
                rawAccept.any { it.startsWith("image/") || it.equals(".jpg", true) || it.equals(".png", true) } &&
                    rawAccept.all { it.startsWith("image/") || it.startsWith(".") } -> "image/*"
                else -> "*/*"
            }

            val contentIntent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_GET_CONTENT).apply {
                addCategory(Intent.CATEGORY_OPENABLE)
                type = primaryMime
                if (rawAccept.size > 1) {
                    putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_MIME_TYPES, rawAccept.map(::normalizeMimeType).toTypedArray())
                }
                if (allowMultiple) {
                    putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_ALLOW_MULTIPLE, true)
                }
            }
            val chooser = Intent.createChooser(contentIntent, "انتخاب فایل برای ارسال")

            val options = UploadRequestOptions(
                acceptMimeTypes = rawAccept.ifEmpty { listOf("*/*") },
                allowMultiple = allowMultiple,
                captureEnabled = params.isCaptureEnabled,
                chooserIntent = chooser,
            )
            onFileChooserRequest(options) { uris ->
                complete(uris)
            }
            true
        }.getOrElse {
            pendingCallback = null
            false
        }
    }

    fun prepareCameraCaptureIntent(context: Context): Pair<Intent, Uri>? = runCatching {
        val dir = File(context.cacheDir, "camera_uploads").apply { mkdirs() }
        val photoFile = File(dir, "capture_${System.currentTimeMillis()}.jpg")
        val uri = FileProvider.getUriForFile(context, "${context.packageName}.fileprovider", photoFile)
        pendingCameraOutputUri = uri
        val intent = Intent(MediaStore.ACTION_IMAGE_CAPTURE).apply {
            putExtra(MediaStore.EXTRA_OUTPUT, uri)
            addFlags(Intent.FLAG_GRANT_WRITE_URI_PERMISSION or Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION)
        }
        intent to uri
    }.getOrNull()

    fun completeWithCameraSuccess(success: Boolean) {
        val uri = pendingCameraOutputUri
        pendingCameraOutputUri = null
        if (success && uri != null) {
            complete(arrayOf(uri))
        } else {
            complete(null)
        }
    }

    fun parseActivityResult(resultCode: Int, data: Intent?): Array<Uri>? {
        val clip = data?.clipData
        if (resultCode == android.app.Activity.RESULT_OK && clip != null && clip.itemCount > 0) {
            return Array(clip.itemCount) { idx -> clip.getItemAt(idx).uri }
        }
        return WebChromeClient.FileChooserParams.parseResult(resultCode, data)
    }

    fun complete(uris: Array<Uri>?) {
        pendingCallback?.onReceiveValue(uris)
        pendingCallback = null
        pendingCameraOutputUri = null
    }

    fun cancelPending() {
        pendingCallback?.onReceiveValue(null)
        pendingCallback = null
        pendingCameraOutputUri = null
    }

    private fun normalizeMimeType(raw: String): String = when (raw.lowercase()) {
        ".pdf" -> "application/pdf"
        ".jpg", ".jpeg" -> "image/jpeg"
        ".png" -> "image/png"
        ".webp" -> "image/webp"
        ".mp4" -> "video/mp4"
        ".mp3" -> "audio/mpeg"
        else -> if (raw.contains('/')) raw else "*/*"
    }
}
package com.example.webviewengine

import android.content.Context
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.os.Build
import android.webkit.WebSettings
import android.webkit.WebView
import com.example.domain.model.MixedContentOption

data class WebViewCapabilityReport(
    val androidSdkInt: Int,
    val webViewPackageName: String,
    val webViewVersionName: String,
    val supportsDomStorage: Boolean = true,
    val supportsIndexedDb: Boolean = true,
    val supportsCookies: Boolean = true,
    val supportsThirdPartyCookies: Boolean = true,
    val supportsSameSiteStrict: Boolean,
    val supportsWebRtc: Boolean = true,
    val supportsFileApi: Boolean = true,
    val supportsClipboardApi: Boolean = true,
    val supportsGeolocation: Boolean = true,
    val supportsWebShareApi: Boolean,
    val supportsWebNotifications: Boolean = false,
    val supportsWebSql: Boolean,
    val notesFa: List<String>,
)

/**
 * Inspects device WebView runtime capabilities and builds hardened WebSettings
 * tailored to each project's configuration and Android OS level.
 */
object WebViewConfiguration {

    fun inspectCapabilities(context: Context): WebViewCapabilityReport {
        val sdk = Build.VERSION.SDK_INT
        val pkgInfo = runCatching { WebView.getCurrentWebViewPackage() }.getOrNull()
        val pkgName = pkgInfo?.packageName ?: "com.google.android.webview"
        val versionName = pkgInfo?.versionName ?: "unknown"
        val majorVersion = versionName.substringBefore('.').toIntOrNull() ?: 0
        val hasCamera = context.packageManager.hasSystemFeature(PackageManager.FEATURE_CAMERA_ANY)
        val hasMic = context.packageManager.hasSystemFeature(PackageManager.FEATURE_MICROPHONE)

        val notes = buildList {
            if (!hasCamera) add("این دستگاه فاقد دوربین سخت‌افزاری است.")
            if (!hasMic) add("این دستگاه فاقد میکروفون سخت‌افزاری است.")
            add("Web Notifications استاندارد مرورگر در Android WebView پشتیبانی کامل ندارد و برای پوش‌نوتیفیکیشن از سرویس بومی استفاده می‌شود.")
            if (sdk >= 33) {
                add("WebSQL در نسخه‌های جدید WebView منسوخ شده و استفاده از DOM Storage / IndexedDB توصیه می‌شود.")
            }
            add("پخش ویدئوهای دارای DRM اختصاصی (Widevine L1) یا کدک‌های غیراستاندارد ممکن است در برخی وب‌سایت‌ها با محدودیت WebView مواجه شود.")
        }

        return WebViewCapabilityReport(
            androidSdkInt = sdk,
            webViewPackageName = pkgName,
            webViewVersionName = versionName,
            supportsSameSiteStrict = majorVersion == 0 || majorVersion >= 80 || sdk >= 29,
            supportsWebShareApi = majorVersion == 0 || majorVersion >= 89,
            supportsWebNotifications = false,
            supportsWebSql = sdk < 33,
            notesFa = notes,
        )
    }

    @Suppress("SetJavaScriptEnabled", "DEPRECATION")
    fun applySettings(settings: WebSettings, config: WebViewEngineConfig) {
        settings.javaScriptEnabled = config.javaScriptEnabled
        settings.domStorageEnabled = config.domStorageEnabled
        settings.databaseEnabled = config.webSqlEnabled || config.domStorageEnabled

        // Strict file-access hardening against local file exfiltration
        settings.allowFileAccess = false
        settings.allowContentAccess = true
        settings.allowFileAccessFromFileURLs = false
        settings.allowUniversalAccessFromFileURLs = false

        settings.loadWithOverviewMode = true
        settings.useWideViewPort = true
        settings.setSupportZoom(config.zoomEnabled)
        settings.builtInZoomControls = config.zoomEnabled
        settings.displayZoomControls = false

        settings.setGeolocationEnabled(config.geolocationAllowed)
        settings.javaScriptCanOpenWindowsAutomatically = config.popupsAllowed
        settings.setSupportMultipleWindows(config.multipleWindowsAllowed)
        settings.mediaPlaybackRequiresUserGesture = !config.webRtcAllowed
        settings.cacheMode = config.cacheMode.androidConst

        settings.mixedContentMode = when (config.mixedContentMode) {
            MixedContentOption.NEVER_ALLOW -> WebSettings.MIXED_CONTENT_NEVER_ALLOW
            MixedContentOption.COMPATIBILITY -> WebSettings.MIXED_CONTENT_COMPATIBILITY_MODE
        }

        if (config.customUserAgent.isNotBlank()) {
            settings.userAgentString = config.customUserAgent.trim()
        }
    }
}
