data class JsDialogRequest(
    val titleFa: String,
    val message: String,
    val isConfirm: Boolean,
)

data class WebViewEngineConfig(
    val initialUrl: String,
    val navigationRulesRaw: String = "",
    val javaScriptEnabled: Boolean = true,
    val domStorageEnabled: Boolean = true,
    val webSqlEnabled: Boolean = false,
    val clipboardAccessEnabled: Boolean = true,
    val cookiesEnabled: Boolean = true,
    val thirdPartyCookiesEnabled: Boolean = false,
    val fileUploadEnabled: Boolean = true,
    val downloadManagerEnabled: Boolean = true,
    val cameraAllowed: Boolean = false,
    val microphoneAllowed: Boolean = false,
    val geolocationAllowed: Boolean = false,
    val webRtcAllowed: Boolean = false,
    val fullscreenVideoAllowed: Boolean = true,
    val multipleWindowsAllowed: Boolean = false,
    val popupsAllowed: Boolean = false,
    val popupBehavior: PopupBehaviorMode = PopupBehaviorMode.SAME_PAGE,
    val backBehavior: BackNavigationBehavior = BackNavigationBehavior.HISTORY_THEN_EXIT,
    val customUserAgent: String = "",
    val cacheMode: CacheModeOption = CacheModeOption.DEFAULT,
    val zoomEnabled: Boolean = false,
    val mixedContentMode: MixedContentOption = MixedContentOption.NEVER_ALLOW,
    val sslPolicy: SslPolicy = SslPolicy.STRICT_BLOCK,
    val httpsOnly: Boolean = true,
    val externalLinkMode: ExternalLinkMode = ExternalLinkMode.ASK_OR_ALLOWLIST,
    val enableDeepLinks: Boolean = false,
    val deepLinkScheme: String = "webino",
    val enableAppLinks: Boolean = false,
    val appLinkHost: String = "",
)

data class WebPreviewState(
    val currentUrl: String = "",
    val pageTitle: String = "",
    val progress: Int = 0,
    val isLoading: Boolean = false,
    val canGoBack: Boolean = false,
    val canGoForward: Boolean = false,
    val errorDescription: String? = null,
    val errorInfo: WebErrorInfo? = null,
    val sslWarningUrl: String? = null,
    val blockedNotice: String? = null,
    val hasFullscreenVideo: Boolean = false,
    val hasInternalPopup: Boolean = false,
    val hasSessionCookies: Boolean = false,
    val isOnline: Boolean = true,
    val pendingRationale: PermissionRationaleRequest? = null,
    val pendingExternalConfirm: NavigationDecision.ConfirmExternal? = null,
    val pendingUploadOptions: UploadRequestOptions? = null,
    val pendingJsDialog: JsDialogRequest? = null,
    val activeDownloads: List<WebDownloadItem> = emptyList(),
    val capabilityReport: WebViewCapabilityReport? = null,
    val confirmClearSiteData: Boolean = false,
    val confirmExitPrompt: Boolean = false,
)

typealias WebViewEngineController = WebViewManager

/**
 * Orchestrating facade for WEBINO's 16-module WebView architecture.
 * Coordinates WebViewConfiguration, WebViewFactory, WebViewLifecycleManager, WebNavigationHandler,
 * WebExternalLinkHandler, WebPermissionManager, WebUploadManager, WebDownloadManager,
 * WebCookieManager, WebStorageManager, WebErrorHandler, WebPopupHandler, WebFullscreenManager,
 * WebConnectivityManager, and WebSecurityManager.
 */
class WebViewManager(
    private val context: Context,
    private val onStateChange: (WebPreviewState) -> Unit,
    private val onOsPermissionPrompt: (List<String>, (Boolean) -> Unit) -> Unit,
    private val onShowCustomVideoView: (View?) -> Unit,
    private val onShowPopupWebView: (WebView?) -> Unit = {},
) {
    private val lifecycleManager = WebViewLifecycleManager()
    private val navigationHandler = WebNavigationHandler(context)
    private val externalLinkHandler = WebExternalLinkHandler(context)
    private val downloadManager = WebDownloadManager(context)
    private val errorHandler = WebErrorHandler(context)
    private val popupHandler = WebPopupHandler(context)
    private val fullscreenManager = WebFullscreenManager(onShowCustomVideoView)

    private var uploadCallbackSink: ValueCallback<Array<Uri>>? = null
    private val uploadManager = WebUploadManager { options, cb ->
        uploadCallbackSink = cb
        updateState { it.copy(pendingUploadOptions = options) }
    }

    private val permissionManager = WebPermissionManager(
        context = context,
        onRationaleRequired = { rationale ->
            updateState { it.copy(pendingRationale = rationale) }
        },
        onOsPermissionPrompt = onOsPermissionPrompt,
    )

    private var webView: WebView? = null
    private var popupWebView: WebView? = null
    private var pendingJsResult: JsResult? = null
    private var currentConfig = WebViewEngineConfig("")
    private var state = WebPreviewState()

    private fun updateState(transform: (WebPreviewState) -> WebPreviewState) {
        state = transform(state)
        onStateChange(state)
    }

    fun getCurrentState(): WebPreviewState = state

    fun getCapabilityReport(): WebViewCapabilityReport =
        WebViewConfiguration.inspectCapabilities(context)

    fun attach(
        view: WebView,
        config: WebViewEngineConfig,
        savedBundle: Bundle? = null,
    ) {
        this.webView = view
        this.currentConfig = config
        navigationHandler.updateRules(config.initialUrl, config.navigationRulesRaw)

        val capabilities = WebViewConfiguration.inspectCapabilities(context)
        val online = errorHandler.isNetworkAvailable()
        updateState {
            it.copy(
                capabilityReport = capabilities,
                isOnline = online,
                currentUrl = it.currentUrl.ifBlank { config.initialUrl },
            )
        }

        WebViewFactory.configure(
            view = view,
            config = config,
            allowedOriginHost = navigationHandler.getAllowedOriginHost(),
            onShareRequested = { text ->
                externalLinkHandler.shareText(text, state.pageTitle.ifBlank { "WEBINO" })
            },
        )
        WebCookieManager.applyPolicy(view, config.cookiesEnabled, config.thirdPartyCookiesEnabled)

        view.webViewClient = object : WebViewClient() {
            override fun onPageStarted(wv: WebView?, url: String?, favicon: Bitmap?) {
                errorHandler.stopReconnectMonitor()
                updateState {
                    it.copy(
                        currentUrl = url ?: it.currentUrl,
                        isLoading = true,
                        errorDescription = null,
                        errorInfo = null,
                        blockedNotice = null,
                        canGoBack = wv?.canGoBack() ?: false,
                        canGoForward = wv?.canGoForward() ?: false,
                        isOnline = errorHandler.isNetworkAvailable(),
                    )
                }
            }

            override fun onPageFinished(wv: WebView?, url: String?) {
                WebCookieManager.flush()
                val current = url ?: state.currentUrl
                updateState {
                    it.copy(
                        currentUrl = current,
                        isLoading = false,
                        progress = 100,
                        canGoBack = wv?.canGoBack() ?: false,
                        canGoForward = wv?.canGoForward() ?: false,
                        hasSessionCookies = WebCookieManager.hasSessionCookies(current),
                    )
                }
            }

            override fun onReceivedError(wv: WebView?, req: WebResourceRequest?, error: WebResourceError?) {
                if (req?.isForMainFrame == true) {
                    val info = errorHandler.classifyNetworkError(
                        errorCode = error?.errorCode ?: -1,
                        rawDescription = error?.description?.toString(),
                        failingUrl = req.url?.toString(),
                    )
                    updateState {
                        it.copy(
                            isLoading = false,
                            errorDescription = info.messageFa,
                            errorInfo = info,
                            isOnline = errorHandler.isNetworkAvailable(),
                            canGoBack = wv?.canGoBack() ?: false,
                            canGoForward = wv?.canGoForward() ?: false,
                        )
                    }
                    errorHandler.startReconnectMonitor {
                        wv?.post {
                            updateState { s -> s.copy(isOnline = true) }
                            reload()
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            override fun onReceivedHttpError(
                wv: WebView?,
                request: WebResourceRequest?,
                errorResponse: WebResourceResponse?,
            ) {
                if (request?.isForMainFrame == true && (errorResponse?.statusCode ?: 200) >= 400) {
                    val info = errorHandler.classifyHttpError(
                        statusCode = errorResponse?.statusCode ?: 500,
                        failingUrl = request.url?.toString(),
                    )
                    updateState {
                        it.copy(
                            isLoading = false,
                            errorDescription = info.messageFa,
                            errorInfo = info,
                            canGoBack = wv?.canGoBack() ?: false,
                            canGoForward = wv?.canGoForward() ?: false,
                        )
                    }
                }
            }

            override fun onReceivedSslError(wv: WebView?, handler: SslErrorHandler?, error: SslError?) {
                val res = errorHandler.handleSslError(
                    handler = handler,
                    error = error,
                    fallbackUrl = state.currentUrl,
                    policy = config.sslPolicy,
                ) ?: return
                when (res) {
                    SslResolution.CancelledStrictly -> {
                        val sslErr = WebErrorInfo(
                            category = WebErrorCategory.SSL_ERROR,
                            titleFa = WebErrorCategory.SSL_ERROR.titleFa,
                            messageFa = "گواهی امنیتی SSL این صفحه نامعتبر است و اتصال برای حفاظت از اطلاعات مسدود شد.",
                            technicalDetail = "SSL_ERROR • ${WebSecurityManager.sanitizeUrlForLog(error?.url ?: state.currentUrl)}",
                        )
                        updateState {
                            it.copy(
                                isLoading = false,
                                errorDescription = sslErr.messageFa,
                                errorInfo = sslErr,
                            )
                        }
                    }
                    is SslResolution.RequireExplicitUserWarning -> updateState {
                        it.copy(
                            isLoading = false,
                            sslWarningUrl = res.url,
                        )
                    }
                }
            }

            override fun shouldOverrideUrlLoading(wv: WebView?, req: WebResourceRequest?): Boolean {
                val uri = req?.url ?: return false
                return when (val decision = navigationHandler.evaluate(uri, config)) {
                    NavigationDecision.AllowInWebView -> false
                    is NavigationDecision.OpenExternal -> {
                        val opened = navigationHandler.openExternal(decision.uri)
                        if (!opened) {
                            updateState { it.copy(blockedNotice = "برنامه‌ای برای باز کردن ${decision.categoryLabelFa} روی دستگاه یافت نشد.") }
                        }
                        true
                    }
                    is NavigationDecision.ConfirmExternal -> {
                        updateState { it.copy(pendingExternalConfirm = decision) }
                        true
                    }
                    is NavigationDecision.BlockWithNotice -> {
                        updateState { it.copy(blockedNotice = decision.reason) }
                        true
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        view.webChromeClient = object : WebChromeClient() {
            override fun onProgressChanged(wv: WebView?, newProgress: Int) {
                updateState { it.copy(progress = newProgress, isLoading = newProgress < 100) }
            }

            override fun onReceivedTitle(wv: WebView?, title: String?) {
                if (!title.isNullOrBlank()) {
                    updateState { it.copy(pageTitle = title) }
                }
            }

            override fun onShowCustomView(customView: View?, callback: CustomViewCallback?) {
                val entered = fullscreenManager.enterFullscreen(customView, callback, config.fullscreenVideoAllowed)
                if (entered) {
                    updateState { it.copy(hasFullscreenVideo = true) }
                }
            }

            override fun onHideCustomView() {
                fullscreenManager.exitFullscreen()
                updateState { it.copy(hasFullscreenVideo = false) }
            }

            override fun onShowFileChooser(
                wv: WebView?,
                cb: ValueCallback<Array<Uri>>?,
                params: FileChooserParams?,
            ): Boolean = uploadManager.startChooser(cb, params, config.fileUploadEnabled)

            override fun onGeolocationPermissionsShowPrompt(
                origin: String?,
                callback: GeolocationPermissions.Callback?,
            ) {
                permissionManager.handleGeolocationPrompt(
                    origin = origin,
                    callback = callback,
                    config = config,
                    allowedOriginHost = navigationHandler.getAllowedOriginHost(),
                    onStatusNotice = { notice -> updateState { it.copy(blockedNotice = notice) } },
                )
            }

            override fun onPermissionRequest(request: PermissionRequest?) {
                permissionManager.handleWebPermissionRequest(
                    request = request,
                    config = config,
                    allowedOriginHost = navigationHandler.getAllowedOriginHost(),
                    onStatusNotice = { notice -> updateState { it.copy(blockedNotice = notice) } },
                )
            }

            override fun onPermissionRequestCanceled(request: PermissionRequest?) {
                permissionManager.handleWebPermissionCanceled(request)
            }

            override fun onCreateWindow(
                wv: WebView?,
                isDialog: Boolean,
                isUserGesture: Boolean,
                resultMsg: Message?,
            ): Boolean {
                val handled = popupHandler.handleCreateWindow(
                    mainWebView = view,
                    config = config,
                    isUserGesture = isUserGesture,
                    resultMsg = resultMsg,
                    onPopupUrlForExternal = { popupUrl ->
                        runCatching { Uri.parse(popupUrl) }.getOrNull()?.let { externalLinkHandler.openExternal(it) }
                    },
                    onShowInternalPopup = { pView ->
                        popupWebView = pView
                        onShowPopupWebView(pView)
                        updateState { it.copy(hasInternalPopup = true) }
                    },
                    onCloseInternalPopup = {
                        closeInternalPopup()
                    },
                )
                if (!handled && resultMsg != null) {
                    updateState { it.copy(blockedNotice = "باز شدن پنجره یا پاپ‌آپ طبق تنظیمات پروژه مسدود شد.") }
                }
                return handled
            }

            override fun onCloseWindow(window: WebView?) {
                if (window == popupWebView) {
                    closeInternalPopup()
                }
            }

            override fun onJsAlert(view: WebView?, url: String?, message: String?, result: JsResult?): Boolean {
                pendingJsResult?.cancel()
                pendingJsResult = result
                updateState {
                    it.copy(
                        pendingJsDialog = JsDialogRequest(
                            titleFa = state.pageTitle.ifBlank { "پیام صفحه" },
                            message = message.orEmpty(),
                            isConfirm = false,
                        )
                    )
                }
                return true
            }

            override fun onJsConfirm(view: WebView?, url: String?, message: String?, result: JsResult?): Boolean {
                pendingJsResult?.cancel()
                pendingJsResult = result
                updateState {
                    it.copy(
                        pendingJsDialog = JsDialogRequest(
                            titleFa = state.pageTitle.ifBlank { "تأییدیه" },
                            message = message.orEmpty(),
                            isConfirm = true,
                        )
                    )
                }
                return true
            }
        }

        view.setDownloadListener(DownloadListener { url, _, contentDisposition, mimetype, contentLength ->
            if (!config.downloadManagerEnabled) {
                updateState { it.copy(blockedNotice = "مدیریت دانلود در تنظیمات این پروژه غیرفعال است.") }
                return@DownloadListener
            }
            val item = downloadManager.handleDownload(
                url = url,
                contentDisposition = contentDisposition,
                mimetype = mimetype,
                contentLength = contentLength,
                enabled = true,
                onExternalFallback = { uri -> navigationHandler.openExternal(uri) },
            )
            if (item != null) {
                updateState {
                    it.copy(
                        activeDownloads = (listOf(item) + it.activeDownloads).take(15),
                        blockedNotice = "دانلود آغاز شد: ${item.fileName}",
                    )
                }
            } else {
                updateState { it.copy(blockedNotice = "این فایل به دلایل امنیتی یا خطای لینک مسدود شد.") }
            }
        })

        val restored = if (savedBundle != null) {
            lifecycleManager.restoreState(view, savedBundle)
        } else {
            lifecycleManager.restoreState(view)
        }
        if (!restored && config.initialUrl.isNotBlank()) {
            loadUrl(config.initialUrl)
        }
    }

    fun loadUrl(url: String) {
        val scheme = runCatching { Uri.parse(url).scheme?.lowercase() }.getOrNull().orEmpty()
        if (WebSecurityManager.isDangerousScheme(scheme)) {
            updateState { it.copy(blockedNotice = "طرح آدرس غیرمجاز مسدود شد.") }
            return
        }
        if (currentConfig.httpsOnly && url.startsWith("http://", ignoreCase = true)) {
            updateState { it.copy(blockedNotice = "حالت HTTPS-Only فعال است و آدرس HTTP مسدود شد.") }
            return
        }
        webView?.loadUrl(url)
    }

    fun reload() {
        updateState { it.copy(errorDescription = null, errorInfo = null, blockedNotice = null) }
        webView?.reload()
    }

    fun zoomIn() {
        webView?.zoomIn()
    }

    fun zoomOut() {
        webView?.zoomOut()
    }

    fun goBack(): Boolean {
        if (fullscreenManager.isInFullscreen()) {
            fullscreenManager.exitFullscreen()
            updateState { it.copy(hasFullscreenVideo = false) }
            return true
        }
        val popup = popupWebView
        if (popup != null) {
            if (popup.canGoBack()) {
                popup.goBack()
            } else {
                closeInternalPopup()
            }
            return true
        }
        val wv = webView ?: return false
        if (wv.canGoBack()) {
            wv.goBack()
            return true
        }
        return false
    }

    fun handleBackPress(onExitScreen: () -> Unit) {
        if (fullscreenManager.isInFullscreen()) {
            fullscreenManager.exitFullscreen()
            updateState { it.copy(hasFullscreenVideo = false) }
            return
        }
        if (popupWebView != null) {
            goBack()
            return
        }
        when (currentConfig.backBehavior) {
            BackNavigationBehavior.DIRECT_EXIT -> onExitScreen()
            BackNavigationBehavior.HISTORY_THEN_EXIT -> {
                if (!goBack()) onExitScreen()
            }
            BackNavigationBehavior.HISTORY_THEN_CONFIRM_EXIT -> {
                if (!goBack()) {
                    updateState { it.copy(confirmExitPrompt = true) }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fun dismissExitConfirm(exitNow: Boolean, onExitScreen: () -> Unit) {
        updateState { it.copy(confirmExitPrompt = false) }
        if (exitNow) onExitScreen()
    }

    fun goForward(): Boolean {
        val wv = webView ?: return false
        if (wv.canGoForward()) {
            wv.goForward()
            return true
        }
        return false
    }

    fun openCurrentInExternalBrowser(): Boolean {
        val target = state.currentUrl.ifBlank { currentConfig.initialUrl }
        val uri = runCatching { Uri.parse(target) }.getOrNull() ?: return false
        return navigationHandler.openExternal(uri)
    }

    fun resolveRationale(confirmed: Boolean) {
        val pending = state.pendingRationale
        updateState { it.copy(pendingRationale = null) }
        if (pending != null) {
            if (confirmed) pending.onConfirmGrant() else pending.onUserDecline()
        }
    }

    fun resolveExternalNavigation(openExternal: Boolean) {
        val decision = state.pendingExternalConfirm
        updateState { it.copy(pendingExternalConfirm = null) }
        if (decision != null && openExternal) {
            navigationHandler.openExternal(decision.uri)
        }
    }

    fun resolveJsDialog(confirmed: Boolean) {
        val res = pendingJsResult
        pendingJsResult = null
        updateState { it.copy(pendingJsDialog = null) }
        if (confirmed) res?.confirm() else res?.cancel()
    }

    fun resolveSslDecision(proceed: Boolean) {
        errorHandler.resolvePendingSsl(proceed)
        updateState { it.copy(sslWarningUrl = null) }
    }

    fun prepareCameraCapture(): Pair<Intent, Uri>? {
        return uploadManager.prepareCameraCaptureIntent(context)
    }

    fun requestCameraForUpload(onResult: (Boolean) -> Unit) {
        val origin = state.currentUrl.ifBlank { currentConfig.initialUrl }
        permissionManager.requestCameraForUpload(origin, onResult)
    }

    fun completeUploadResult(resultCode: Int, data: Intent?) {
        val uris = uploadManager.parseActivityResult(resultCode, data)
        uploadManager.complete(uris)
        uploadCallbackSink = null
        updateState { it.copy(pendingUploadOptions = null) }
    }

    fun completeCameraCapture(success: Boolean) {
        uploadManager.completeWithCameraSuccess(success)
        uploadCallbackSink = null
        updateState { it.copy(pendingUploadOptions = null) }
    }

    fun cancelUpload() {
        uploadManager.cancelPending()
        uploadCallbackSink = null
        updateState { it.copy(pendingUploadOptions = null) }
    }

    fun refreshDownloads() {
        val updated = state.activeDownloads.map { item ->
            if (item.status == DownloadItemStatus.RUNNING || item.status == DownloadItemStatus.PENDING || item.status == DownloadItemStatus.PAUSED) {
                downloadManager.refreshDownloadStatus(item)
            } else {
                item
            }
        }
        updateState { it.copy(activeDownloads = updated) }
    }

    fun cancelDownload(downloadId: Long) {
        downloadManager.cancelDownload(downloadId)
        updateState { s ->
            s.copy(
                activeDownloads = s.activeDownloads.map {
                    if (it.downloadId == downloadId) it.copy(status = DownloadItemStatus.CANCELLED) else it
                }
            )
        }
    }

    fun openDownload(item: WebDownloadItem): Boolean = downloadManager.openDownloadedFile(item)

    fun closeInternalPopup() {
        val pView = popupWebView
        popupWebView = null
        onShowPopupWebView(null)
        lifecycleManager.destroy(pView, WebViewFactory.JS_BRIDGE_NAME)
        updateState { it.copy(hasInternalPopup = false) }
    }

    fun requestClearSiteDataConfirmation() {
        updateState { it.copy(confirmClearSiteData = true) }
    }

    fun confirmClearSiteData(proceed: Boolean) {
        updateState { it.copy(confirmClearSiteData = false) }
        if (proceed) {
            WebStorageManager.clearAllSiteData(webView)
            updateState {
                it.copy(
                    hasSessionCookies = false,
                    blockedNotice = "کوکی‌ها، حافظه سایت و کش موقت پاک شدند.",
                )
            }
        }
    }

    fun clearSessionCache() {
        WebStorageManager.clearAllSiteData(webView)
        updateState { it.copy(hasSessionCookies = false) }
    }

    fun saveWebViewState(outState: Bundle? = null): Bundle? =
        lifecycleManager.saveState(webView, outState)

    fun restoreWebViewState(inState: Bundle? = null): Boolean =
        lifecycleManager.restoreState(webView, inState)

    fun onLowMemory() {
        lifecycleManager.onLowMemory(webView)
    }

    fun onResume() {
        lifecycleManager.onResume(webView, popupWebView)
    }

    fun onPause() {
        lifecycleManager.saveState(webView)
        lifecycleManager.onPause(webView, popupWebView)
    }

    fun destroy() {
        errorHandler.stopReconnectMonitor()
        pendingJsResult?.cancel()
        pendingJsResult = null
        uploadManager.cancelPending()
        uploadCallbackSink = null
        closeInternalPopup()
        fullscreenManager.exitFullscreen()
        lifecycleManager.destroy(webView, WebViewFactory.JS_BRIDGE_NAME)
        webView = null
    }
}
package com.example.webviewengine

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.content.ClipData
import android.content.ClipboardManager
import android.content.Context
import android.view.ViewGroup
import android.webkit.JavascriptInterface
import android.webkit.WebView

/**
 * Configures WebView instances via [WebViewConfiguration] and attaches a strictly
 * origin-gated native JavaScript bridge ([WebinoNativeBridge]) when JS is enabled.
 */
object WebViewFactory {

    const val JS_BRIDGE_NAME = "WebinoBridge"

    @SuppressLint("SetJavaScriptEnabled")
    fun configure(
        view: WebView,
        config: WebViewEngineConfig,
        allowedOriginHost: String = "",
        onShareRequested: ((String) -> Unit)? = null,
    ) {
        view.layoutParams = ViewGroup.LayoutParams(
            ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
            ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
        )
        WebViewConfiguration.applySettings(view.settings, config)
        WebSecurityManager.enforceSafeSettings(view.settings)
        WebStorageManager.applyStorageAndCache(
            webView = view,
            domStorageEnabled = config.domStorageEnabled,
            webSqlEnabled = config.webSqlEnabled,
            cacheMode = config.cacheMode,
        )

        runCatching { view.removeJavascriptInterface(JS_BRIDGE_NAME) }
        if (config.javaScriptEnabled) {
            view.addJavascriptInterface(
                WebinoNativeBridge(
                    context = view.context,
                    webView = view,
                    allowedOriginHost = allowedOriginHost,
                    httpsOnly = config.httpsOnly,
                    clipboardAllowed = config.clipboardAccessEnabled,
                    onShareRequested = onShareRequested,
                ),
                JS_BRIDGE_NAME,
            )
        }
    }

    class WebinoNativeBridge(
        private val context: Context,
        private val webView: WebView,
        private val allowedOriginHost: String,
        private val httpsOnly: Boolean,
        private val clipboardAllowed: Boolean,
        private val onShareRequested: ((String) -> Unit)?,
    ) {
        @JavascriptInterface
        fun shareText(text: String?) {
            if (!isTrusted()) return
            onShareRequested?.invoke(text.orEmpty())
        }

        @JavascriptInterface
        fun copyToClipboard(text: String?): Boolean {
            if (!clipboardAllowed || !isTrusted() || text.isNullOrEmpty()) return false
            return runCatching {
                val cm = context.getSystemService(Context.CLIPBOARD_SERVICE) as? ClipboardManager ?: return false
                cm.setPrimaryClip(ClipData.newPlainText("WEBINO", text.take(4096)))
                true
            }.getOrDefault(false)
        }

        @JavascriptInterface
        fun getEngineCapabilitiesJson(): String {
            if (!isTrusted()) return "{}"
            val report = WebViewConfiguration.inspectCapabilities(context)
            return """{"sdk":${report.androidSdkInt},"webView":"${report.webViewVersionName}","indexedDb":${report.supportsIndexedDb},"webShare":${report.supportsWebShareApi},"notifications":${report.supportsWebNotifications}}"""
        }

        private fun isTrusted(): Boolean {
            val currentUrl: String? = if (android.os.Looper.myLooper() == android.os.Looper.getMainLooper()) {
                webView.url
            } else {
                var fetched: String? = null
                val latch = java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch(1)
                webView.post {
                    fetched = webView.url
                    latch.countDown()
                }
                runCatching { latch.await(400, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS) }
                fetched
            }
            return WebSecurityManager.isOriginTrusted(currentUrl, allowedOriginHost, httpsOnly)
        }
    }
}
package com.example.webviewengine

import android.os.Bundle
import android.view.ViewGroup
import android.webkit.WebView

/**
 * Manages WebView lifecycle transitions (onResume, onPause), state preservation across
 * Activity rotation/recreation (saveState/restoreState), memory trimming, and leak-free teardown.
 */
class WebViewLifecycleManager {

    private var savedWebState: Bundle? = null

    fun onResume(mainWebView: WebView?, popupWebView: WebView? = null) {
        runCatching { mainWebView?.onResume() }
        runCatching { popupWebView?.onResume() }
    }

    fun onPause(mainWebView: WebView?, popupWebView: WebView? = null) {
        runCatching { popupWebView?.onPause() }
        runCatching { mainWebView?.onPause() }
    }

    fun saveState(webView: WebView?, outState: Bundle? = null): Bundle? {
        val target = outState ?: Bundle()
        val ok = runCatching { webView?.saveState(target) != null }.getOrDefault(false)
        if (ok) {
            savedWebState = Bundle(target)
            return target
        }
        return null
    }

    fun restoreState(webView: WebView?, inState: Bundle? = null): Boolean {
        val source = inState ?: savedWebState ?: return false
        return runCatching {
            webView?.restoreState(source) != null
        }.getOrDefault(false)
    }

    fun onLowMemory(webView: WebView?) {
        runCatching {
            webView?.clearCache(false)
            webView?.freeMemory()
        }
    }

    fun destroy(webView: WebView?, jsBridgeName: String = "WebinoBridge") {
        if (webView == null) return
        runCatching {
            webView.stopLoading()
            webView.removeJavascriptInterface(jsBridgeName)
            webView.loadUrl("about:blank")
            (webView.parent as? ViewGroup)?.removeView(webView)
            webView.removeAllViews()
            webView.destroy()
        }
    }
}
package com.example.work

import android.Manifest
import android.app.NotificationChannel
import android.app.NotificationManager
import android.app.PendingIntent
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.content.pm.ServiceInfo
import android.os.Build
import androidx.core.app.NotificationCompat
import androidx.core.app.NotificationManagerCompat
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import androidx.work.ForegroundInfo
import com.example.MainActivity
import com.example.domain.model.BuildStatus
import red.line.vebino.R

/**
 * Progress + result notifications for background builds.
 * Text is passed in already localized by the caller (see [BuildNotifier.Texts]).
 */
class BuildNotifier(private val context: Context) {

    data class Texts(
        val channelName: String,
        val channelDesc: String,
        val running: String,
        val queued: String,
        val succeeded: String,
        val failed: String,
        val cancelled: String,
        val cancelAction: String,
        val tapToOpen: String,
    )

    private val manager = NotificationManagerCompat.from(context)

    fun ensureChannel(texts: Texts) {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < Build.VERSION_CODES.O) return
        val sys = context.getSystemService(Context.NOTIFICATION_SERVICE) as NotificationManager
        val progress = NotificationChannel(CHANNEL_PROGRESS, texts.channelName, NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_LOW)
        progress.description = texts.channelDesc
        progress.setShowBadge(false)
        sys.createNotificationChannel(progress)
        val result = NotificationChannel(CHANNEL_RESULT, texts.channelName, NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_DEFAULT)
        result.description = texts.channelDesc
        sys.createNotificationChannel(result)
    }

    private fun openAppIntent(): PendingIntent {
        val intent = Intent(context, MainActivity::class.java).apply {
            flags = Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK or Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_SINGLE_TOP
        }
        return PendingIntent.getActivity(
            context,
            0,
            intent,
            PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT or PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE,
        )
    }

    fun progressNotification(
        taskId: Long,
        appName: String,
        stepLabel: String,
        percent: Int,
        queued: Boolean,
        texts: Texts,
        cancelIntent: PendingIntent?,
    ) = NotificationCompat.Builder(context, CHANNEL_PROGRESS)
        .setSmallIcon(R.mipmap.ic_launcher)
        .setContentTitle("${if (queued) texts.queued else texts.running} • $appName")
        .setContentText(stepLabel)
        .setProgress(100, percent.coerceIn(0, 100), queued)
        .setOngoing(true)
        .setOnlyAlertOnce(true)
        .setSilent(true)
        .setCategory(NotificationCompat.CATEGORY_PROGRESS)
        .setContentIntent(openAppIntent())
        .apply { if (cancelIntent != null) addAction(0, texts.cancelAction, cancelIntent) }
        .build()

    fun foregroundInfo(taskId: Long, notification: android.app.Notification): ForegroundInfo {
        val id = NOTIFICATION_BASE + (taskId % 100000).toInt()
        return if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.Q) {
            ForegroundInfo(id, notification, ServiceInfo.FOREGROUND_SERVICE_TYPE_DATA_SYNC)
        } else {
            ForegroundInfo(id, notification)
        }
    }

    fun showResult(taskId: Long, appName: String, status: BuildStatus, detail: String?, texts: Texts) {
        if (!canPost()) return
        val title = when (status) {
            BuildStatus.SUCCEEDED -> texts.succeeded
            BuildStatus.CANCELLED -> texts.cancelled
            else -> texts.failed
        }
        val n = NotificationCompat.Builder(context, CHANNEL_RESULT)
            .setSmallIcon(R.mipmap.ic_launcher)
            .setContentTitle("$title • $appName")
            .setContentText(detail?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: texts.tapToOpen)
            .setStyle(NotificationCompat.BigTextStyle().bigText(detail?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: texts.tapToOpen))
            .setAutoCancel(true)
            .setContentIntent(openAppIntent())
            .build()
        runCatching { manager.notify(NOTIFICATION_BASE + 500000 + (taskId % 100000).toInt(), n) }
    }

    fun canPost(): Boolean =
        Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU ||
            ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(context, Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS) ==
            PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED

    companion object {
        const val CHANNEL_PROGRESS = "webino_build_progress"
        const val CHANNEL_RESULT = "webino_build_result"
        private const val NOTIFICATION_BASE = 4200
    }
}
package com.example.work

import android.content.Context
import androidx.work.CoroutineWorker
import androidx.work.WorkerParameters
import com.example.WebinoApplication
import com.example.domain.model.AppLanguage
import com.example.domain.model.BuildStatus
import com.example.domain.model.BuildStep
import kotlinx.coroutines.coroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.first
import kotlinx.coroutines.isActive
import kotlinx.coroutines.NonCancellable
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

class BuildWorker(
    appContext: Context,
    params: WorkerParameters,
) : CoroutineWorker(appContext, params) {

    override suspend fun doWork(): Result = coroutineScope {
        val taskId = inputData.getLong(KEY_TASK_ID, -1L)
        if (taskId <= 0L) return@coroutineScope Result.failure()

        val app = applicationContext as? WebinoApplication ?: return@coroutineScope Result.failure()
        val buildDao = app.database.buildDao()
        val projectDao = app.database.projectDao()

        val isFa = runCatching { app.repository.preferences.first().language == AppLanguage.FA }.getOrDefault(true)
        val texts = textsFor(isFa)
        val notifier = BuildNotifier(applicationContext)
        notifier.ensureChannel(texts)

        val initialTask = buildDao.getBuildTask(taskId)
        val appName = initialTask
            ?.let { runCatching { projectDao.getProjectById(it.projectId)?.project?.name }.getOrNull() }
            ?: "WEBINO"

        val cancelIntent = androidx.work.WorkManager.getInstance(applicationContext).createCancelPendingIntent(id)

        // Promote to a foreground service so the build survives the app going to background.
        runCatching {
            val first = notifier.progressNotification(
                taskId, appName, stepLabel(BuildStep.CHECKING_ENV.name, isFa), 0, true, texts, cancelIntent,
            )
            setForeground(notifier.foregroundInfo(taskId, first))
        }

        // Keep the notification in sync with the DB progress while the build runs.
        val progressJob = launch {
            var lastShown = -1 to ""
            while (isActive) {
                val t = buildDao.getBuildTask(taskId)
                if (t != null) {
                    val key = t.progressPercent to t.currentStep
                    if (key != lastShown) {
                        lastShown = key
                        val n = notifier.progressNotification(
                            taskId = taskId,
                            appName = appName,
                            stepLabel = stepLabel(t.currentStep, isFa),
                            percent = t.progressPercent,
                            queued = t.status == BuildStatus.QUEUED.name,
                            texts = texts,
                            cancelIntent = cancelIntent,
                        )
                        runCatching { setForeground(notifier.foregroundInfo(taskId, n)) }
                    }
                }
                delay(1000)
            }
        }

        val ok = try {
            app.repository.runBuildTask(taskId, externalCancelSignal = { isStopped })
        } catch (e: kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException) {
            // Cancelled from the notification (or by the system): make the DB reflect it, otherwise
            // the history would keep showing "building" forever.
            withContext(NonCancellable) {
                val t = buildDao.getBuildTask(taskId)
                if (t != null && t.status != BuildStatus.CANCELLED.name &&
                    t.status != BuildStatus.SUCCEEDED.name && t.status != BuildStatus.FAILED.name
                ) {
                    buildDao.updateBuildTask(
                        t.copy(
                            status = BuildStatus.CANCELLED.name,
                            finishedAt = System.currentTimeMillis(),
                            errorMessage = if (isFa) "ساخت لغو شد." else "Build cancelled.",
                        )
                    )
                }
            }
            throw e
        } finally {
            progressJob.cancel()
        }

        val finalTask = buildDao.getBuildTask(taskId)
        val status = runCatching { BuildStatus.valueOf(finalTask?.status ?: "") }
            .getOrDefault(if (ok) BuildStatus.SUCCEEDED else BuildStatus.FAILED)
        notifier.showResult(taskId, appName, status, finalTask?.errorMessage, texts)

        if (ok) Result.success() else Result.failure()
    }

    private fun stepLabel(step: String, isFa: Boolean): String {
        val known = runCatching { BuildStep.valueOf(step) }.getOrNull()
        return known?.let { if (isFa) it.titleFa else it.titleEn } ?: step
    }

    private fun textsFor(isFa: Boolean) = if (isFa) {
        BuildNotifier.Texts(
            channelName = "ساخت اپلیکیشن",
            channelDesc = "پیشرفت و نتیجه‌ی ساخت APK و AAB",
            running = "در حال ساخت",
            queued = "در صف ساخت",
            succeeded = "ساخت موفق",
            failed = "خطا در ساخت",
            cancelled = "ساخت لغو شد",
            cancelAction = "لغو",
            tapToOpen = "برای مشاهده‌ی نتیجه لمس کنید",
        )
    } else {
        BuildNotifier.Texts(
            channelName = "App builds",
            channelDesc = "Progress and result of APK and AAB builds",
            running = "Building",
            queued = "Queued",
            succeeded = "Build succeeded",
            failed = "Build failed",
            cancelled = "Build cancelled",
            cancelAction = "Cancel",
            tapToOpen = "Tap to see the result",
        )
    }

    companion object {
        const val KEY_TASK_ID = "task_id"
    }
}

package com.example

import android.content.Context
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import androidx.test.core.app.ApplicationProvider
import com.example.buildengine.BuildSpec
import com.example.buildengine.EnvironmentChecker
import com.example.buildengine.OnDeviceBuildOrchestrator
import com.example.buildengine.OutputValidator
import com.example.data.crypto.SigningManager
import com.example.domain.model.BuildOutputFormat
import com.example.domain.model.BuildStep
import kotlinx.coroutines.test.runTest
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertNotNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test
import org.junit.runner.RunWith
import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
import org.robolectric.annotation.Config
import java.io.File
import java.io.RandomAccessFile
import java.nio.ByteBuffer
import java.nio.ByteOrder
import java.util.zip.ZipEntry
import java.util.zip.ZipFile

@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
@Config(sdk = [36])
class OnDeviceBuildEngineTest {

    @Test
    fun `on-device build engine compiles packages signs and validates real APK and AAB`() = runTest {
        val context = ApplicationProvider.getApplicationContext<Context>()

        val env = EnvironmentChecker.inspect(context)
        assertTrue("Environment must be ready: ${env.issues}", env.isReady)
        assertTrue(env.hasEmbeddedStubs)
        assertTrue(env.hasJavaCompiler)
        assertTrue(env.hasDexCompiler)
        assertTrue(env.hasApkSigner)

        val signingManager = SigningManager(context)
        val ksInfo = signingManager.createNewKeystore(
            alias = "webino_poc",
            password = "StrongTestPassword#1405",
            commonName = "WEBINO PoC",
            organization = "WEBINO",
            country = "IR",
        )
        val signingMaterial = signingManager.loadSigningMaterial(
            keystorePath = ksInfo.keystorePath,
            alias = ksInfo.alias,
            encryptedPasswordToken = ksInfo.encryptedPasswordToken,
        )

        val spec = BuildSpec(
            projectId = 1L,
            appName = "وبینو تست",
            packageName = "ir.example.webinotest",
            versionName = "1.2.3",
            versionCode = 12,
            websiteUrl = "https://example.ir",
            developerName = "Webino Team",
            enableDeepLinks = true,
            deepLinkScheme = "webinotest",
            outputFormat = BuildOutputFormat.BOTH,
            keyAlias = signingMaterial.alias,
            privateKey = signingMaterial.privateKey,
            certificateChain = signingMaterial.certificateChain,
        )

        val outputDir = File(context.filesDir, "outputs_test").apply { deleteRecursively(); mkdirs() }
        val orchestrator = OnDeviceBuildOrchestrator(context)
        val seenSteps = mutableSetOf<BuildStep>()
        var lastPercent = 0

        val result = orchestrator.executeBuild(spec, outputDir) { progress ->
            seenSteps.add(progress.step)
            assertTrue("Progress must be monotonic", progress.progressPercent >= lastPercent)
            lastPercent = progress.progressPercent
        }

        assertTrue("Build failed: ${result.errorMessage}\nLogs: ${result.logs.joinToString("\n") { it.message }}", result.success)
        assertEquals(2, result.artifacts.size)
        assertEquals(100, lastPercent)
        assertTrue(seenSteps.contains(BuildStep.COMPILING))
        assertTrue(seenSteps.contains(BuildStep.SIGNING))
        assertTrue(seenSteps.contains(BuildStep.VALIDATING))
        assertTrue(seenSteps.contains(BuildStep.COMPLETED))

        val apkArtifact = result.artifacts.first { it.format == BuildOutputFormat.APK }
        val apkFile = File(apkArtifact.filePath)
        assertTrue(apkFile.exists())

        // Verify APK signature (v1, v2, v3) via official Google ApkVerifier
        val apkValidation = OutputValidator.validateApk(apkFile)
        assertTrue("APK validation failed: ${apkValidation.summary}", apkValidation.isValid)
        assertTrue(apkValidation.v2Verified)
        assertTrue(apkValidation.v3Verified)

        // Verify resources.arsc is STORED and 4-byte aligned
        ZipFile(apkFile).use { zip ->
            val arsc = zip.getEntry("resources.arsc")
            assertNotNull(arsc)
            assertEquals(ZipEntry.STORED, arsc!!.method)
        }
        val arscOffset = findZipEntryDataOffset(apkFile, "resources.arsc")
        assertTrue("resources.arsc offset ($arscOffset) must be 4-byte aligned", arscOffset > 0 && arscOffset % 4L == 0L)

        // Verify Android PackageManager parses our binary AndroidManifest.xml cleanly
        @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
        val pkgInfo = context.packageManager.getPackageArchiveInfo(
            apkFile.absolutePath,
            PackageManager.GET_ACTIVITIES or PackageManager.GET_PERMISSIONS
        )
        assertNotNull("PackageManager failed to parse generated APK manifest", pkgInfo)
        assertEquals("ir.example.webinotest", pkgInfo!!.packageName)
        assertEquals("1.2.3", pkgInfo.versionName)
        assertTrue(pkgInfo.requestedPermissions?.contains("android.permission.INTERNET") == true)
        assertEquals("ir.example.webinotest.MainActivity", pkgInfo.activities?.firstOrNull()?.name)

        // Verify AAB archive structure & CMS JAR signature
        val aabArtifact = result.artifacts.first { it.format == BuildOutputFormat.AAB }
        val aabFile = File(aabArtifact.filePath)
        assertTrue(aabFile.exists())
        val aabValidation = OutputValidator.validateAab(aabFile)
        assertTrue("AAB validation failed: ${aabValidation.summary}", aabValidation.isValid)
    }

    private fun findZipEntryDataOffset(zipFile: File, targetName: String): Long {
        RandomAccessFile(zipFile, "r").use { raf ->
            val len = raf.length()
            val searchStart = (len - 65560L).coerceAtLeast(0L)
            val tail = ByteArray((len - searchStart).toInt())
            raf.seek(searchStart)
            raf.readFully(tail)
            val tailBuf = ByteBuffer.wrap(tail).order(ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN)
            var eocdIdx = -1
            for (i in tail.size - 22 downTo 0) {
                if (tail[i] == 0x50.toByte() &&
                    tail[i + 1] == 0x4B.toByte() &&
                    tail[i + 2] == 0x05.toByte() &&
                    tail[i + 3] == 0x06.toByte()
                ) {
                    eocdIdx = i
                    break
                }
            }
            if (eocdIdx < 0) return -1L
            val cdSize = tailBuf.getInt(eocdIdx + 12).toLong() and 0xFFFFFFFFL
            val cdOffset = tailBuf.getInt(eocdIdx + 16).toLong() and 0xFFFFFFFFL

            var pos = cdOffset
            val cdEnd = cdOffset + cdSize
            val cdHdr = ByteArray(46)
            while (pos + 46 <= cdEnd) {
                raf.seek(pos)
                raf.readFully(cdHdr)
                val bb = ByteBuffer.wrap(cdHdr).order(ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN)
                if (bb.getInt(0) != 0x02014B50) break
                val nameLen = bb.getShort(28).toInt() and 0xFFFF
                val extraLen = bb.getShort(30).toInt() and 0xFFFF
                val commentLen = bb.getShort(32).toInt() and 0xFFFF
                val localHeaderOffset = bb.getInt(42).toLong() and 0xFFFFFFFFL
                val nameBytes = ByteArray(nameLen)
                raf.readFully(nameBytes)
                val name = String(nameBytes, Charsets.UTF_8)
                if (name == targetName) {
                    val lfh = ByteArray(30)
                    raf.seek(localHeaderOffset)
                    raf.readFully(lfh)
                    val lfhBuf = ByteBuffer.wrap(lfh).order(ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN)
                    if (lfhBuf.getInt(0) != 0x04034B50) return -1L
                    val lfhNameLen = lfhBuf.getShort(26).toInt() and 0xFFFF
                    val lfhExtraLen = lfhBuf.getShort(28).toInt() and 0xFFFF
                    return localHeaderOffset + 30L + lfhNameLen + lfhExtraLen
                }
                pos += 46L + nameLen + extraLen + commentLen
            }
        }
        return -1L
    }
}
package com.example

import android.content.Context
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.graphics.Bitmap
import android.graphics.Canvas
import android.graphics.Color
import androidx.room.Room
import androidx.test.core.app.ApplicationProvider
import com.example.buildengine.BuildConfiguration
import com.example.buildengine.BuildReportGenerator
import com.example.buildengine.BuildSpec
import com.example.buildengine.BuildStorageManager
import com.example.buildengine.OutputValidator
import com.example.buildengine.ProjectGenerator
import com.example.data.crypto.SigningManager
import com.example.data.local.db.BuildTaskEntity
import com.example.data.local.db.WebinoDatabase
import com.example.data.local.prefs.UserPreferencesDataStore
import com.example.data.network.WebsiteInspector
import com.example.data.repository.WebinoRepository
import com.example.domain.model.AppTemplateType
import com.example.domain.model.BuildOutputFormat
import com.example.domain.model.BuildStatus
import com.example.domain.usecase.CancelBuildUseCase
import com.example.domain.usecase.RecoverInterruptedBuildsUseCase
import com.example.domain.usecase.RetryBuildUseCase
import com.example.domain.usecase.StartBuildUseCase
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.first
import kotlinx.coroutines.test.runTest
import org.junit.After
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertNotNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Before
import org.junit.Test
import org.junit.runner.RunWith
import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
import org.robolectric.annotation.Config
import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream
import java.io.File

@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
@Config(sdk = [36])
class Prompt03BuildEngineTest {

    private lateinit var context: Context
    private lateinit var db: WebinoDatabase
    private lateinit var repo: WebinoRepository

    @Before
    fun setUp() {
        context = ApplicationProvider.getApplicationContext()
        if (!androidx.work.WorkManager.isInitialized()) {
            androidx.work.WorkManager.initialize(
                context,
                androidx.work.Configuration.Builder().build(),
            )
        }
        db = Room.inMemoryDatabaseBuilder(context, WebinoDatabase::class.java)
            .allowMainThreadQueries()
            .build()
        repo = WebinoRepository(
            context = context,
            db = db,
            prefsStore = UserPreferencesDataStore(context),
            signingManager = SigningManager(context),
            websiteInspector = WebsiteInspector(),
        )
    }

    @After
    fun tearDown() {
        db.close()
    }

    private fun createCustomPngBytes(): ByteArray {
        val bmp = Bitmap.createBitmap(128, 128, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
        val canvas = Canvas(bmp)
        canvas.drawColor(Color.parseColor("#7C3AED"))
        return ByteArrayOutputStream().apply {
            bmp.compress(Bitmap.CompressFormat.PNG, 100, this)
        }.toByteArray()
    }

    @Test
    fun `operational test builds WEBINO Test App APK and AAB with 10-point validation`() = runTest {
        repo.initializeDefaults()
        val key = repo.signingKeys.first().first()

        val projectId = repo.createProject(
            name = "WEBINO Test App",
            websiteUrl = "https://example.com",
            packageName = "ir.webino.testapp",
            versionName = "1.0",
            versionCode = 1,
            description = "Operational Prompt 03 verification project",
            template = AppTemplateType.STORE,
            faviconUrl = null,
            themeColorOverride = "#7C3AED",
        )

        val iconFile = File(context.filesDir, "test_custom_icon.png").apply {
            writeBytes(createCustomPngBytes())
        }
        val bundle = repo.observeProject(projectId).first()!!
        repo.updateProjectBundle(
            project = bundle.project,
            settings = bundle.settings!!.copy(
                appName = "WEBINO Test App",
                iconFilePath = iconFile.absolutePath,
            ),
            webSettings = bundle.webSettings!!.copy(httpsOnly = true),
            changeNote = "Set custom icon",
        )

        val startUseCase = StartBuildUseCase(repo)
        val taskId = startUseCase(projectId, BuildOutputFormat.BOTH, key.id).getOrThrow()
        assertTrue(taskId > 0L)

        val ok = repo.runBuildTask(taskId)
        assertTrue("Operational build task must succeed", ok)

        val task = repo.observeBuildTask(taskId).first()!!
        assertEquals(BuildStatus.SUCCEEDED.name, task.status)
        assertEquals(100, task.progressPercent)

        val results = repo.allBuildResults.first().filter { it.taskId == taskId }
        assertEquals(2, results.size)

        val apkEntity = results.first { it.format == BuildOutputFormat.APK.name }
        val aabEntity = results.first { it.format == BuildOutputFormat.AAB.name }
        val apkFile = File(apkEntity.filePath)
        val aabFile = File(aabEntity.filePath)

        val expectedSpec = repo.validateBuildSpec(projectId, BuildOutputFormat.BOTH, key.id).getOrThrow().first

        // 10-point APK validation against expected BuildSpec
        val apkReport = OutputValidator.validateApk(apkFile, expectedSpec = expectedSpec)
        assertTrue("APK validation failed: ${apkReport.errors}", apkReport.isValid)
        assertTrue(apkReport.isSigned)
        assertTrue(apkReport.v1Verified)
        assertTrue(apkReport.v2Verified)
        assertTrue(apkReport.v3Verified)
        assertEquals("ir.webino.testapp", apkReport.parsedManifest?.packageName)
        assertEquals(1, apkReport.parsedManifest?.versionCode)
        assertEquals("1.0", apkReport.parsedManifest?.versionName)

        // PackageManager archive inspection
        @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
        val pkgInfo = context.packageManager.getPackageArchiveInfo(
            apkFile.absolutePath,
            PackageManager.GET_ACTIVITIES or PackageManager.GET_PERMISSIONS,
        )
        assertNotNull(pkgInfo)
        assertEquals("ir.webino.testapp", pkgInfo!!.packageName)
        assertEquals("1.0", pkgInfo.versionName)
        assertEquals("ir.webino.testapp.MainActivity", pkgInfo.activities?.firstOrNull()?.name)

        // Verify AAB CMS cryptographic signature & spec match
        val aabReport = OutputValidator.validateAab(aabFile, expectedSpec = expectedSpec)
        assertTrue("AAB validation failed: ${aabReport.errors}", aabReport.isValid)
        assertTrue(aabReport.isSigned)
        assertTrue(aabReport.certSha256.isNotBlank())

        // Verify report JSON was persisted & temp build folder was cleaned
        val reportFile = File(BuildStorageManager.getReportsDir(context), "build_report_$taskId.json")
        assertTrue("Build report JSON should exist", reportFile.exists())
        val tempLeftovers = BuildStorageManager.getBuildsCacheRoot(context).listFiles().orEmpty()
        assertTrue("Temp workspace should be cleaned up", tempLeftovers.isEmpty())

        // Verify generated target Java source contains expected URL & Back-navigation handler
        val srcVerifyDir = File(context.cacheDir, "verify_src_tmp").apply { deleteRecursively(); mkdirs() }
        val srcFiles = ProjectGenerator.generateSources(expectedSpec, srcVerifyDir)
        val javaCode = srcFiles.first().readText()
        assertTrue(javaCode.contains("https://example.com"))
        assertTrue(javaCode.contains("KeyEvent.KEYCODE_BACK"))
        assertTrue(javaCode.contains("webView.goBack()"))
        srcVerifyDir.deleteRecursively()
    }

    @Test
    fun `pre-build validation rejects invalid BuildSpec with Persian messages`() = runTest {
        repo.initializeDefaults()
        val key = repo.signingKeys.first().first()
        val signingMaterial = SigningManager(context).loadSigningMaterial(
            key.keystorePath, key.alias, key.encryptedPasswordToken
        )

        val invalidSpec = BuildSpec(
            projectId = 1L,
            appName = "",
            packageName = "bad_pkg",
            versionName = "",
            versionCode = 0,
            minSdk = 30,
            targetSdk = 28,
            websiteUrl = "http://insecure.ir",
            httpsOnly = true,
            primaryColorHex = "not-a-color",
            iconPngBytes = byteArrayOf(1, 2, 3),
            keyAlias = signingMaterial.alias,
            privateKey = signingMaterial.privateKey,
            certificateChain = signingMaterial.certificateChain,
        )

        val validation = BuildConfiguration.validate(invalidSpec)
        assertFalse(validation.isValid)
        assertTrue(validation.errors.size >= 6)

        // Also test via repository StartBuildUseCase on invalid persisted project
        val badId = repo.createProject(
            name = "تست نامعتبر",
            websiteUrl = "http://plain-http.ir",
            packageName = "invalid",
            versionName = "1.0",
            versionCode = 1,
            description = "",
            template = AppTemplateType.STORE,
            faviconUrl = null,
            themeColorOverride = null,
        )
        val b = repo.observeProject(badId).first()!!
        repo.updateProjectBundle(
            project = b.project,
            settings = b.settings!!,
            webSettings = b.webSettings!!.copy(httpsOnly = true),
            changeNote = "force httpsOnly",
        )
        val startResult = StartBuildUseCase(repo)(badId, BuildOutputFormat.APK, key.id)
        assertTrue("StartBuildUseCase must fail on invalid project config", startResult.isFailure)
    }

    @Test
    fun `cancel retry and interrupted build recovery work cleanly`() = runTest {
        repo.initializeDefaults()
        val key = repo.signingKeys.first().first()
        val projectId = repo.createProject(
            name = "بازیابی تست",
            websiteUrl = "https://example.com",
            packageName = "ir.webino.recoverytest",
            versionName = "1.0",
            versionCode = 1,
            description = "",
            template = AppTemplateType.PERSONAL,
            faviconUrl = null,
            themeColorOverride = null,
        )

        // 1. Cancel test
        val cancelledTaskId = StartBuildUseCase(repo)(projectId, BuildOutputFormat.APK, key.id).getOrThrow()
        CancelBuildUseCase(repo)(cancelledTaskId)
        val cancelledTask = repo.observeBuildTask(cancelledTaskId).first()!!
        assertEquals(BuildStatus.CANCELLED.name, cancelledTask.status)
        assertFalse("Cancelled task must not build", repo.runBuildTask(cancelledTaskId))

        // 2. Retry test
        val retriedTaskId = RetryBuildUseCase(repo)(cancelledTaskId).getOrThrow()
        assertTrue(retriedTaskId > cancelledTaskId)
        assertTrue("Retried build should succeed", repo.runBuildTask(retriedTaskId))

        // 3. Interrupted build recovery (simulate orphaned RUNNING task after process death)
        val stuckTaskId = db.buildDao().insertBuildTask(
            BuildTaskEntity(
                projectId = projectId,
                outputFormat = BuildOutputFormat.APK.name,
                signingKeyId = key.id,
                status = BuildStatus.RUNNING.name,
                currentStep = "COMPILING",
                progressPercent = 44,
                workRequestId = null,
            )
        )
        val recoveredCount = RecoverInterruptedBuildsUseCase(repo)()
        assertTrue(recoveredCount >= 1)
        val recoveredTask = db.buildDao().getBuildTask(stuckTaskId)!!
        assertEquals(BuildStatus.FAILED.name, recoveredTask.status)
        assertNotNull(recoveredTask.errorMessage)
    }

    @Test
    fun `build report generator redacts passwords and vault tokens`() {
        val raw = "Keystore password=MySecret123 token=AKS:AbCdEfGhIjKlMnOpQrStUvWxYz=="
        val redacted = BuildReportGenerator.redactSensitive(raw, enabled = true)
        assertFalse(redacted.contains("MySecret123"))
        assertFalse(redacted.contains("AbCdEfGhIjKlMnOpQrStUvWxYz"))
        assertTrue(redacted.contains("***REDACTED***"))
    }
}
